FILE: older/test/amend.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// The five targeted corrections of 20 Nov 2026 (older-3), each with its own test.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, quoteFor, H, T0, BASE } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { newEntry, advance, observe, COST } from "../src/book.mjs";
import { liqValue, sellSol } from "../src/fills.mjs";
import { decodeEvent, applyEvent, DISC, dec } from "../src/decoder.mjs";

const st = (quote, t) => ({ base: 2e8, quote, feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003, t }), yes = () => true;

test("1. scale-out keeps the trail intact: an existing drawdown is neither erased nor enlarged by selling half; costs charged once per leg", () => {
  const e = newEntry({ mint: "M", pool: "P", ag: "A1", t: 1000, families: ["LOW4H"], features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1 });
  let cur = st(200, 999); advance(e, 1001, cur, yes); const v = e.versions.find((x) => x.policy === "scale" && x.stop === 0.25), tok = e.fill.tok;
  const ev = (s) => { advance(e, s.t, cur, yes); cur = s; observe(e, s, s.t); };
  ev(st(440, 1100));                                    // peak; full value ≥ 2× cost → half-sale scheduled for 1100.7
  ev(st(400, 1100.4));                                  // price slips ~9% BEFORE the half-sale executes: a real drawdown exists
  const ddBefore = liqValue(cur, v.tok) / v.peak; assert.ok(ddBefore < 0.93 && ddBefore > 0.88);
  advance(e, 1101, cur, yes);                           // half-sale executes at 1100.7 on the 400 state
  assert.ok(v.half && Math.abs(v.tok - tok / 2) < 1e-6);
  const ddAfter = liqValue(cur, v.tok) / v.peak;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(ddAfter - ddBefore) < 1e-12, `drawdown from the high-water mark is identical before (${ddBefore}) and after (${ddAfter}) the partial sale`);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.half.sol - (sellSol(cur, tok / 2) - 0.0005)) < 1e-12, "the half-sale leg pays one transaction cost");
  ev(st(330, 1200)); advance(e, 1201, cur, yes);         // 330 is −25% from 440: the ORIGINAL trail still fires (it was not reset to the 400 level)
  assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.reason, "trail");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.sol - (v.half.sol + sellSol(st(330, 0), tok / 2) - 0.0005)) < 1e-9, "second leg pays its own transaction cost: total costs = buy + 2 sells");
  const plain = e.versions.find((x) => x.policy === "trail" && x.stop === 0.25); assert.equal(plain.status, "closed", "the pure-trailing row triggered at the same level");
});

test("2. USD history: every observation is converted at the SOL/USD of its own moment; positions are valued in SOL", () => {
  let usd = 100; const e = mkEngine({ solUsd: () => usd }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H });
  const sw = (t, quote) => e.onSwap({ t, mint: "MintAAApump", pool: "PoolA", base: BASE, quote, feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u" });
  sw(T0, 200); usd = 200; sw(T0 + 10, 200);             // same pool price in SOL; SOL/USD doubled between the two observations
  const raw = e.coins.get("MintAAApump").series.raw;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(raw[0][1] - (200 / BASE) * 100) < 1e-15 && Math.abs(raw[1][1] - (200 / BASE) * 200) < 1e-15, "the earlier observation keeps the earlier rate; it is not re-priced at today's rate");
});

test("3. SECOND-WAVE lives across an age boundary: classified by age AT ENTRY, seed age recorded, one active setup per mint", () => {
  const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); const create = T0 - (16 * H - 900); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: create }); // 15 min before the 16 h boundary
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  swap(e, T0 + 605, 205_000);                           // seed at age 15 h 55 min → A1
  swap(e, T0 + 700, 260_000); swap(e, T0 + 800, 190_000); // high 260K, pullback freezes it (still A1)
  swap(e, T0 + 1000, 250_000);                          // age 16 h 01 min 40 s → now A2; setup still alive
  swap(e, T0 + 1010, 262_000);                          // reclaim in A2
  const sw = [...e.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("SECOND-WAVE")); assert.ok(sw, "the setup was not dropped at the boundary");
  assert.equal(sw.ag, "A2"); const d = sw.cands.find((c) => c.family === "SECOND-WAVE").detail; assert.equal(d.seed_ag, "A1"); assert.equal(d.entry_ag, "A2"); assert.equal(d.seed_age_s, 15 * H + 55 * 60 + 5);
  const c = e.coins.get("MintAAApump"); assert.equal(c.sw, null); assert.ok(c.groups.A1.done["SECOND-WAVE"] && c.groups.A2.done["SECOND-WAVE"], "seed group's attempt and entry group's slot are both used");
});

test("4. HH-BREAK windows: signal [B−15 min, B), baseline sixteen buckets over [B−255 min, B−15 min); the unfinished minute is in neither", () => {
  const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 3.5 * H });
  path(e, T0, T0 + 4.5 * H, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 1 });               // 1 SOL a minute for 4.5 h
  const t1 = T0 + 4.5 * H;
  swap(e, t1 + 5, 101_000, { sol: 500 });                                 // a huge print in the CURRENT (unfinished) minute, new 60-min high
  assert.ok(![...e.entries.values()].some((x) => x.families.includes("HH-BREAK")), "volume of the unfinished minute does not count towards the signal window");
  path(e, t1 + 60, t1 + 60 + 60, () => 100_500, 60, { sol: 1 });           // that minute is now complete
  swap(e, t1 + 125, 102_000, { sol: 1 });                                 // new high again; the completed 500-SOL minute is inside [B−15, B)
  const h = [...e.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("HH-BREAK")); assert.ok(h);
  const d = h.cands.find((c) => c.family === "HH-BREAK").detail, B = Math.floor((t1 + 125) / 60) * 60;
  assert.equal(d.B, B); assert.deepEqual(d.signal_window, [B - 900, B]); assert.deepEqual(d.baseline_window, [B - 255 * 60, B - 900]); assert.equal(d.windows.length, 16);
  assert.ok(d.windows.every((w) => w === 1500), "each baseline bucket = 15 completed minutes × 1 SOL × $100");
});

test("5. an unusual observed fee rate is a FAILED measurement: counted, never installed, never clamped — the pool keeps the last rate it really showed", () => {
  const u = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigUInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, i = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, pk = (n) => Buffer.alloc(32, n);
  const B = 200_000_000_000_000n, Q = 200_000_000_000n, b = 2_000_000_000_000n, q = (Q * b) / (B - b);
  const mk = (pct) => Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(DISC.BuyEvent), i(1), u(b), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(B), u(Q), u(q), u(20), u(0), u(5), u(0), u(q), u(q + (q * BigInt(pct)) / 1000n), pk(9), pk(2)]).toString("base64");
  // no earlier rate: the 30% reading is not installed → the fill says the fee was assumed
  const p = { vq: 0 }, n0 = dec.feeOdd, m0 = dec.feeNotMeasured, r = applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(mk(300)));
  assert.equal(p.feeBuy, undefined); assert.equal(dec.feeOdd, n0 + 1); assert.equal(dec.feeNotMeasured, m0 + 1); assert.equal(r.state.feeBuy, null);
  const e = newEntry({ mint: "M", pool: "P", ag: "A1", t: 1000, families: ["LOW4H"], features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1 }); advance(e, 1001, { ...r.state, t: 999 }, yes);
  assert.equal(e.fill.fee_src, "assumed"); assert.ok(Math.abs(e.fill.fee_buy - 0.003) < 1e-12);
  // a real 1% reading is installed; a later nonsense reading (negative) does not replace it
  const p2 = { vq: 0 }; applyEvent(p2, decodeEvent(mk(10))); assert.ok(Math.abs(p2.feeBuy - 0.01) < 1e-9);
  const neg = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(DISC.BuyEvent), i(1), u(b), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(B - b), u(Q + q), u((((Q + q) * b) / (B - b - b))), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(0), u((((Q + q) * b) / (B - b - b))), u((((Q + q) * b) / (B - b - b)) / 2n), pk(9), pk(2)]).toString("base64");
  const r2 = applyEvent(p2, decodeEvent(neg)); assert.ok(r2, "the reserves of that event are still a valid observation"); assert.ok(Math.abs(r2.state.feeBuy - 0.01) < 1e-9, "−50% was not installed");
});

FILE: older/test/anyage.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// older-8 (21 Nov 2026, owner's decisions): the eight any-age books — VOL300K / VOL500K (± price up) and RUG450 / RUG950 (knife, bounce).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { bookRows } from "../src/report.mjs";
import { booksApi } from "../src/page.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", has = (e, f) => [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes(f));
const young = (opts = {}) => { const e = mkEngine(opts); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); return e; }; // a coin 2 hours old: inside its first 8 hours
// SOL = $100 in the tests: a trade of `sol` SOL is $100 × sol of volume

test("VOL300K / VOL500K: a migrated coin in its first 8 hours is bought when $300K (then $500K) has traded in the last 5 minutes; the -UP books also need the price above 5 minutes ago", () => {
  const e = young();
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 100 });                    // 10 minutes at $10K a minute: $50K per 5 minutes
  for (const f of S.VOL.map((x) => x[0])) assert.equal(has(e, f).length, 0, f + ": $50K in 5 minutes is not enough");
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) swap(e, T0 + 600 + k * 30, 100_000, { sol: 700 }); // 4 × $70K in 90 seconds, price flat: $280K + older trades
  const v = has(e, "VOL300K"); assert.equal(v.length, 1, "VOL300K entered"); assert.equal(v[0].ag, "A0"); const d = v[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "VOL300K").detail;
  assert.ok(d.vol5_usd >= 300_000 && d.vol5_usd < 500_000, "volume recorded: " + d.vol5_usd); assert.equal(d.change_5m, 0);
  assert.equal(has(e, "VOL300K-UP").length, 0, "price is not above 5 minutes ago: the -UP book waits"); assert.equal(has(e, "VOL500K").length, 0);
  swap(e, T0 + 720, 104_000, { sol: 700 });                                  // price now 4% above 5 minutes ago, 5-minute volume still over $300K
  assert.equal(has(e, "VOL300K-UP").length, 1); assert.ok(has(e, "VOL300K-UP")[0].cands[0].detail.change_5m > 0); assert.equal(has(e, "VOL300K").length, 1, "one buy per book");
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) swap(e, T0 + 730 + k * 10, 105_000, { sol: 800 }); // more volume: over $500K in the window
  assert.equal(has(e, "VOL500K").length, 1); assert.equal(has(e, "VOL500K-UP").length, 1); assert.equal(has(e, "VOL500K")[0].id, has(e, "VOL500K-UP")[0].id, "one shared buy for the two books that fired together");
  assert.equal(has(e, "CONT").length + has(e, "FAST150-1M").length + has(e, "HH15-5M").length, 0, "no other book looks at a coin under 8 hours old");
});
test("VOL books: $40K–$2M at the buy (the owner's limit for these books); an unobserved stretch inside the 5 minutes blocks the decision; also bought at 8 hours and older", () => {
  const big = young(); path(big, T0, T0 + 600, () => 2_500_000, 60, { sol: 100 }); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) swap(big, T0 + 600 + k * 20, 2_500_000, { sol: 900 });
  assert.equal(has(big, "VOL500K").length, 0, "$2.5M is above the $2M limit");
  const ok = young(); path(ok, T0, T0 + 600, () => 1_500_000, 60, { sol: 100 }); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) swap(ok, T0 + 600 + k * 20, 1_500_000, { sol: 900 });
  assert.equal(has(ok, "VOL500K").length, 1, "$1.5M is inside it (the other books stop at $1M)");
  const gap = young(); path(gap, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 100 }); gap.feedDown(T0 + 601); gap.feedUp(T0 + 660); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) swap(gap, T0 + 665 + k * 20, 100_000, { sol: 900 });
  assert.equal(has(gap, "VOL300K").length, 0, "a feed gap inside the last 5 minutes: the volume is not known, nothing is bought");
  path(gap, T0 + 800, T0 + 1000, () => 100_000, 20, { sol: 900 }); assert.equal(has(gap, "VOL300K").length, 1, "bought once five clean minutes have passed");
  const old = mkEngine(); old.feedUp(T0); old.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); path(old, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 100 }); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) swap(old, T0 + 600 + k * 20, 100_000, { sol: 900 });
  assert.equal(has(old, "VOL500K").length, 1); assert.equal(has(old, "VOL500K")[0].ag, "A2", "a 20-hour-old coin is bought in its own age group");
});
test("RUG450 / RUG950: bought at the fresh fall to 80% below the observed peak; the 950 books need a $950K peak; the bounce books wait for +30% off the low", () => {
  const e = young();
  path(e, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 1400, 20);              // climbs to about $500K
  swap(e, T0 + 300, 520_000); swap(e, T0 + 320, 300_000); swap(e, T0 + 340, 150_000); // peak $520K, then the fall: 20% of the peak is $104K
  assert.equal(has(e, "RUG450").length, 0, "$150K is 71% down: not yet");
  swap(e, T0 + 360, 100_000);                                                // crosses $104K
  const k = has(e, "RUG450"); assert.equal(k.length, 1); const d = k[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "RUG450").detail;
  assert.equal(d.peak_mc, 520_000); assert.ok(d.fall >= 0.8 && d.fall < 0.82); assert.equal(d.fall_s, 60); assert.equal(k[0].ag, "A0");
  assert.equal(has(e, "RUG950").length, 0, "the peak was under $950K"); assert.equal(has(e, "RUG450-BOUNCE").length, 0);
  swap(e, T0 + 380, 70_000); swap(e, T0 + 400, 60_000); swap(e, T0 + 420, 70_000); // low $60K; $70K is +17%: not a 30% bounce
  assert.equal(has(e, "RUG450-BOUNCE").length, 0);
  swap(e, T0 + 440, 80_000);                                                 // +33% off the low
  const b = has(e, "RUG450-BOUNCE"); assert.equal(b.length, 1); const bd = b[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "RUG450-BOUNCE").detail;
  assert.equal(bd.low_mc, 60_000); assert.ok(bd.bounce >= 0.3); assert.equal(bd.wait_s, 80); assert.equal(has(e, "RUG950-BOUNCE").length, 0);
  swap(e, T0 + 460, 50_000); swap(e, T0 + 480, 90_000); assert.equal(has(e, "RUG450-BOUNCE").length, 1, "the setup is spent: no second bounce buy");

  const e2 = young(); path(e2, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 200_000 + (t - T0) * 2800, 20); swap(e2, T0 + 300, 1_000_000); swap(e2, T0 + 320, 400_000); swap(e2, T0 + 340, 190_000);
  const both = has(e2, "RUG950"); assert.equal(both.length, 1, "a $1M peak, 81% down at $190K"); assert.ok(both[0].families.includes("RUG450"), "the books nest: one shared buy for RUG450 and RUG950");
  const e3 = young(); path(e3, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 1400, 20); swap(e3, T0 + 300, 520_000); e3.feedDown(T0 + 301); e3.feedUp(T0 + 330); swap(e3, T0 + 340, 90_000);
  assert.equal(has(e3, "RUG450").length, 0, "the fall happened during a feed gap: not a fresh crossing, nothing is bought");
  const e4 = young(); path(e4, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 600, 20); swap(e4, T0 + 300, 300_000); swap(e4, T0 + 320, 50_000);
  assert.equal(has(e4, "RUG450").length, 0, "a $300K peak is below $450K");
});
test("RUG: nothing is carried across a restart — a fall measured against a peak from before the restart is not bought; a new peak and collapse watched in full is. A setup expires after 24 hours; a bounce outside the band is not bought", () => {
  const db = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-")), "rug.db"); let st = new Store(db); const e = young({ store: st }); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H, info: {} });
  path(e, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 1400, 20); swap(e, T0 + 300, 540_000); swap(e, T0 + 320, 300_000); swap(e, T0 + 340, 150_000); // peak $540K (line $108K), not yet 80% down
  e.flush(); st = new Store(db); const e2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(e2); e2.restartGap(st.lastAlive(), T0 + 400); e2.feedUp(T0 + 400);
  assert.equal(e2.coins.get(M).rug ?? null, null, "no rug state is restored"); assert.equal(e2.coins.get(M).info.rug, undefined, "and none is stored with the coin");
  swap(e2, T0 + 410, 105_000); swap(e2, T0 + 430, 99_000); swap(e2, T0 + 450, 60_000); swap(e2, T0 + 470, 90_000);
  assert.equal(has(e2, "RUG450").length, 0, "the reviewer's case: $105K → $99K after a restart is not a fresh 80% fall — the peak was not watched without a break"); assert.equal(has(e2, "RUG450-BOUNCE").length, 0);
  path(e2, T0 + 500, T0 + 800, (t) => 90_000 + (t - T0 - 500) * 1500, 20); swap(e2, T0 + 800, 540_000); swap(e2, T0 + 820, 200_000); swap(e2, T0 + 840, 100_000);
  assert.equal(has(e2, "RUG450").length, 1, "a new peak and its collapse, both watched after the restart, is bought");
  const x = young(); path(x, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 1400, 20); swap(x, T0 + 300, 520_000); swap(x, T0 + 320, 110_000); swap(x, T0 + 340, 30_000); // armed at $30K: under the $40K floor, so no knife buy
  assert.equal(has(x, "RUG450").length, 0); swap(x, T0 + 360, 20_000); swap(x, T0 + 380, 27_000); assert.equal(has(x, "RUG450-BOUNCE").length, 0, "a bounce to $27K is under $40K: not bought");
  const y = young(); path(y, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 1400, 20); swap(y, T0 + 300, 520_000); swap(y, T0 + 320, 110_000); swap(y, T0 + 340, 100_000); swap(y, T0 + 360, 60_000);
  path(y, T0 + 400, T0 + 25 * H, () => 62_000, 600); swap(y, T0 + 25 * H, 62_000); swap(y, T0 + 25 * H + 20, 90_000);
  assert.equal(has(y, "RUG450-BOUNCE").length, 0, "more than 24 hours after arming: the setup has expired");
});
test("older-8 tables: 1,340 rows (1,240 + the 50 each of VOL100K and DIP15K, older-11) — the any-age books have an 'under 8h' row, the others do not; the page can show the under-8h group", () => {
  const rows = bookRows([]); assert.equal(rows.length, 1340);
  assert.equal(rows.filter((r) => r.ag === "A0").length, 10 * 5 * 2); assert.ok(rows.filter((r) => r.ag === "A0").every((r) => S.ANY_AGE.has(r.family)));
  const e = young(); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 100 }); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) swap(e, T0 + 600 + k * 20, 100_000, { sol: 900 });
  const j = booksApi([...e.entries.values()], { stop: 0.25, ag: "A0" }); assert.ok(j.books["VOL300K"].length >= 1); assert.equal(j.rows_total, 1340); assert.ok(j.rules["RUG950-BOUNCE"] && j.rules["VOL500K-UP"]);
});
test("the books page's own script parses (a quoting slip in the page text once left the site blank; unit tests on the data could not see it)", async () => {
  const { BOOKS_PAGE } = await import("../src/page.mjs"); const m = BOOKS_PAGE.match(/<script>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/); assert.ok(m && m[1].length > 2000);
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => new Function(m[1]), "the page script must be valid JavaScript");
});
test("books page data: each trade carries the market cap at our buy and at our sale, from the pool it was priced on", async () => {
  const { newEntry, advance, observe } = await import("../src/book.mjs"); const st = (q, t) => ({ base: 2e8, quote: q, feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003, t }), yes = () => true;
  const e = newEntry({ mint: "M", pool: "P", ag: "A4", t: 0, families: ["LOW4H"], features: {}, mc: 95_000, px: 1 }); e.evidence = { trigger: { sol_usd: 100, supply: 1e9 } };
  advance(e, 1.5, st(200, 0), yes);                                            // bought on a pool worth $100K (the signal was seen at $95K)
  observe(e, st(100, 60), 60); advance(e, 61, st(100, 60), yes);               // −50%: every stop sells on a pool worth $50K
  const r = booksApi([e], { stop: 0.25 }).books.LOW4H[0]; assert.equal(r.mc, 95_000); assert.equal(r.mc_buy, 100_000); assert.equal(r.mc_sold, 50_000); assert.equal(r.mc_approx, undefined);
  // a trade stored before sale pools were saved: the sale pool is rebuilt from the sale, exact when the pool's k did not change
  const sk = (q, t) => ({ base: 4e10 / q, quote: q, feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003, t }), e2 = newEntry({ mint: "M2", pool: "P", ag: "A4", t: 0, families: ["LOW4H"], features: {}, mc: 95_000, px: 1 }); e2.evidence = { trigger: { sol_usd: 100, supply: 1e9 } };
  advance(e2, 1.5, sk(200, 0), yes); const q50 = Math.sqrt(4e10 * 5e-7); observe(e2, sk(q50, 60), 60); advance(e2, 61, sk(q50, 60), yes);
  const saved = booksApi([e2], { stop: 0.25 }).books.LOW4H[0].mc_sold; for (const v of e2.versions) if (v.exit) delete v.exit.pool;
  const rebuilt = booksApi([e2], { stop: 0.25 }).books.LOW4H[0].mc_sold; assert.equal(saved, 50_000); assert.ok(Math.abs(rebuilt - 50_000) <= 5, "rebuilt from the sale: " + rebuilt);
});
test("the books page reloads itself after an update: the data carries the server's build id and the page script compares it", async () => {
  const { BOOKS_PAGE } = await import("../src/page.mjs");
  assert.equal(booksApi([], { build: "abc123:1" }).build, "abc123:1"); assert.equal(booksApi([], {}).build, null);
  assert.ok(/BUILD&&BUILD!==j\.build/.test(BOOKS_PAGE) && /location\.reload\(\)/.test(BOOKS_PAGE));
});

FILE: older/test/books11.test.mjs

// older-11 — which books buy. The list is applied to finished candidates: an inactive book makes no new buy, an active book
// decides exactly as with all books active, and the default run is the legacy 29 books (VOL100K off).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", fams = (e) => [...e.entries.values()].flatMap((x) => x.families).sort();
const young = () => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); return e; }; // a coin 2 hours old; SOL = $100 in the tests
// ten quiet minutes at $100K, then `sol` SOL four times in 90 seconds with no price move: only a 5-minute-volume book can fire
const burst = (e, sol) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 10 }); for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) swap(e, T0 + 600 + k * 30, 100_000, { sol }); };

test("booksOf: legacy = the 29 books of older-10 (no VOL100K); older-11 = the owner's 13 pool books; an unknown book and a clock book without its base are refused", () => {
  assert.equal(S.FAMILIES.length, 31); assert.equal(S.booksOf("").length, 29); assert.ok(!S.booksOf("legacy").includes("VOL100K"));
  assert.deepEqual(S.booksOf("older-11"), ["HH-BREAK", "HH-BREAK-4H", "VOL100K", "VOL300K", "VOL500K", "FAST-1M", "FAST-3M", "FAST-5M", "RUG450", "RUG950", "LOW4H", "SLOW-BUILD", "DIP15K"]);
  assert.deepEqual(S.booksOf(" VOL300K , RUG450 "), ["VOL300K", "RUG450"]);
  assert.throws(() => S.booksOf("NOPE"), /unknown book/); assert.throws(() => S.booksOf("HH-BREAK-4H"), /shares the buys of HH-BREAK/);
});
test("default run: VOL100K makes no buy — the legacy 29 behave as before", () => {
  const e = young(); burst(e, 300); // 4 × $30K = $120K in 90 seconds
  assert.deepEqual(fams(e), [], "$120K in five minutes fires no legacy book"); assert.ok(e.stats.inactiveCands >= 1, "the VOL100K candidate was counted and dropped");
});
test("older-11 run: VOL100K buys at $100K of 5-minute volume; with $300K it shares ONE buy with VOL300K; a book outside the list makes no buy even when its rule fires", () => {
  const a = young(); a.setBooks(S.booksOf("older-11")); burst(a, 300); assert.deepEqual(fams(a), ["VOL100K"]);
  const d = [...a.entries.values()][0].cands[0].detail; assert.equal(d.threshold_usd, 100_000); assert.ok(d.vol5_usd >= 100_000 && d.vol5_usd < 300_000, "volume recorded: " + d.vol5_usd);
  const L = young(); burst(L, 800); assert.deepEqual(fams(L), ["VOL300K"], "legacy: the price did not rise, so no -UP book");
  const N = young(); N.setBooks(S.booksOf("older-11")); burst(N, 800); assert.deepEqual(fams(N).filter((f) => f !== "VOL100K"), ["VOL300K"]); assert.ok(fams(N).includes("VOL100K"));
  const only = young(); only.setBooks(S.booksOf("RUG450")); burst(only, 800); assert.deepEqual(fams(only), [], "VOL300K's rule fired but the book is not in this run's list");
});
test("a book switched off still has its open position managed: the list stops new buys only", () => {
  const e = young(); burst(e, 800); const en = [...e.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("VOL300K")); assert.ok(en, "VOL300K bought");
  swap(e, T0 + 700, 100_000); swap(e, T0 + 705, 100_000); assert.equal(en.status, "filled");
  e.setBooks(S.booksOf("RUG450")); // VOL300K is not active from here
  swap(e, T0 + 720, 50_000); swap(e, T0 + 725, 50_000); swap(e, T0 + 730, 50_000); // −50%: the trailing stops up to 40% sell
  const closed = en.versions.filter((v) => v.status === "closed" && v.policy === "trail" && !v.stall).map((v) => v.stop).sort(); assert.deepEqual(closed, [0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.4]);
  assert.equal([...e.entries.values()].length, 1, "and nothing new was bought");
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/checkpoint.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// Check-pointing of open positions (23 Nov 2026). An open position's decision state (peak, last value, runner high) must be in
// the database at most CHECKPOINT_S after it changes — and a position nothing happened to must NOT be re-written every second
// (at 1,350 open positions that was ~38 MB/s of writing and a full processor core: the feed ran 10 s late and dropped).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { CHECKPOINT_S } from "../src/spec.mjs";

function setup() {
  const file = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-ck-")), "t.db"), store = new Store(file), e = mkEngine({ store });
  e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 40 * H });
  let t = T0, mc = 100_000; // a steady climb with volume: the books buy
  for (let k = 0; k < 400 && !e.entries.size; k++) { t += 20; mc *= 1.012; swap(e, t, mc, { sol: 30, user: "u" + (k % 40) }); e.tick(t + 1.5); }
  for (let k = 0; k < 20; k++) { t += 1; swap(e, t, mc, { sol: 1 }); e.tick(t + 0.5); } // let the buys fill
  const open = [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.status === "filled");
  assert.ok(open.length > 0, "the scenario must produce at least one open position");
  let saves = 0; const orig = store.saveEntry.bind(store); store.saveEntry = (x) => { saves++; return orig(x); };
  return { e, store, file, t, mc, open, count: () => saves, reset: () => { saves = 0; } };
}

test("a position nothing happened to is not re-written by clock ticks", () => {
  const s = setup(); let now = s.t + 5; s.e.tick(now); s.e.tick(now += CHECKPOINT_S + 0.1); s.reset(); // drain what the setup left
  for (let k = 0; k < 300; k++) s.e.tick(now += 0.1); // 30 s of ticks, no trade on the coin
  assert.equal(s.count(), 0, "30 s without a price: nothing may be written");
  assert.equal(s.e.dirty.size, 0);
});

test("a new price is saved within CHECKPOINT_S, and a restart resumes with the same peaks", () => {
  const s = setup(); let now = s.t + 5; s.e.tick(now); s.e.tick(now += CHECKPOINT_S + 0.1); s.reset();
  const hi = s.mc * 1.05; swap(s.e, now + 0.05, hi, { sol: 1 }); // a new high, no order triggered
  assert.ok(s.e.dirty.size >= 1, "the observed positions are marked");
  for (let k = 0; k < 12; k++) s.e.tick(now += 0.1); // 1.2 s of ticks
  assert.ok(s.count() >= 1, "the regular checkpoint wrote them"); assert.equal(s.e.dirty.size, 0);
  const want = new Map([...s.e.entries.values()].map((x) => [x.id, JSON.stringify(x)]));
  const e2 = mkEngine({ store: new Store(s.file) }); e2.store.restore(e2);
  for (const [id, j] of want) assert.equal(JSON.stringify(e2.entries.get(id)), j, `entry ${id} restored exactly as it stood`);
});

test("changes made by the clock alone are still saved (max hold, clock books, runner horizon)", () => {
  const s = setup(); let now = s.t + 5; s.e.tick(now); s.e.tick(now += CHECKPOINT_S + 0.1); s.reset();
  // jump the clock past every time-based rule without a single trade: orders are scheduled / horizons close → written
  s.e.tick(now += 25 * H); s.e.tick(now += CHECKPOINT_S + 0.1);
  assert.ok(s.count() >= s.open.length, "every position's time-based change reached the database");
  const e2 = mkEngine({ store: new Store(s.file) }); e2.store.restore(e2);
  for (const x of s.e.entries.values()) assert.equal(JSON.stringify(e2.entries.get(x.id)), JSON.stringify(x), `entry ${x.id} identical after restart`);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/decoder.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { decodeEvent, applyEvent, DISC, dec, pumpPoolAuthority, canonicalPool, isCanonical, onCurve, bs58dec } from "../src/decoder.mjs";

// Builders that write events in the IDL's field order (pump-public-docs idl/pump_amm.json), PRE-swap reserves.
const u = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigUInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, i = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; };
const i128 = (v) => { v = BigInt.asUintN(128, BigInt(v)); const x = Buffer.alloc(16); x.writeBigUInt64LE(v & 0xffffffffffffffffn, 0); x.writeBigUInt64LE(v >> 64n, 8); return x; };
const pk = (n) => Buffer.alloc(32, n), bool = (b) => Buffer.from([b ? 1 : 0]), str = (s) => { const n = Buffer.alloc(4); n.writeUInt32LE(s.length); return Buffer.concat([n, Buffer.from(s)]); };
function swap({ buy, B, Q, vq = 0n, b, feeFrac = 0.0125, lp = 0n, supply = 1_000_000_000_000_000n, truncate = null, ix = "buy", raw = null }) {
  const E = Q + vq; let q = buy ? (E * b) / (B - b) : (E * b) / (B + b), fee = BigInt(Math.round(Number(q) * feeFrac)), user = buy ? q + fee : q - fee;
  if (raw) { q = raw.quoteAmt; user = raw.userQuote; } // exact on-chain field values
  const head = [Buffer.from(buy ? DISC.BuyEvent : DISC.SellEvent), i(1800000000), u(b), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(B), u(Q), u(q), u(20), u(lp), u(5), u(0), u(q), u(user), pk(9), pk(2)];
  const creator = [pk(3), pk(4), pk(5), pk(6), pk(7), u(30), u(0)];
  const buyOnly = buy ? [bool(false), u(0), u(0), u(0), i(0), u(0), str(ix)] : [];
  const tail = [u(0), u(0), u(10), u(0), i128(vq), bool(false), u(supply), u(30), u(0), u(0)]; // cashback, buyback, virtual, can_boost, base_supply, holder rewards (same charge as creator fee), unclaimed
  const parts = [...head, ...creator, ...buyOnly, ...tail];
  return Buffer.concat(truncate == null ? parts : parts.slice(0, truncate)).toString("base64");
}
const B0 = 200_000_000_000_000n, Q0 = 200_000_000_000n; // 200M tokens, 200 SOL

test("BuyEvent / SellEvent decode in IDL order, including the appended fields (ix name, virtual reserves, supply, holder rewards)", () => {
  const e = decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, vq: -30_000_000n, b: 1_000_000_000n }));
  assert.equal(e.type, "buy"); assert.equal(e.lpBps, 20); assert.equal(e.creatorBps, 30); assert.equal(e.holderBps, 30); assert.equal(e.buybackBps, 10);
  assert.equal(e.virtualQuote, -30_000_000n); assert.equal(e.baseSupply, 1_000_000_000_000_000n); assert.equal(e.ix, "buy"); assert.equal(e.poolBase, B0);
  const s = decodeEvent(swap({ buy: false, B: B0, Q: Q0, b: 1_000_000_000n })); assert.equal(s.type, "sell"); assert.equal(s.virtualQuote, 0n); assert.equal(s.holderBps, 30);
});

test("pricing uses EFFECTIVE quote reserves (vault + signed virtual), and a negative virtual value is handled", () => {
  const p = {}, r = applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, vq: -5_000_000_000n, b: 1_000_000_000n })));
  assert.ok(r, "an event priced on effective reserves is accepted");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.state.quote - (195 + r.sol)) < 1e-6, "post-swap effective quote = (200 − 5) + quote in"); assert.ok(Math.abs(r.state.base - (200_000_000 - 1000)) < 1e-6);
  assert.equal(r.state.supply, 1_000_000_000);
});

test("fee per side is the user's net debit/credit — overlapping fee fields (creator fee = holder rewards) are never added together", () => {
  const p = {}; applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, b: 1_000_000_000n, feeFrac: 0.0125 })));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(p.feeBuy - 0.0125) < 1e-4, "not 20+5+30+10+30 bps = 0.0095, and not a double-count: what the user actually paid");
  applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: false, B: B0 - 1_000_000_000n, Q: Q0 + 1_000_005n, b: 500_000_000n, feeFrac: 0.01 }))); assert.ok(Math.abs(p.feeSell - 0.01) < 1e-4);
});

test("pre/post evidence: each swap is scored against both hypotheses, consecutive events are chained, and a non-fitting event is rejected", () => {
  const p = {}, c0 = { ...dec };
  // only trades that are large against the pool separate the two hypotheses (they differ by ≈ 2 × trade/pool); tiny trades fit both
  const tiny0 = dec.fitBoth; applyEvent({}, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, b: 1_000_000_000n }))); assert.equal(dec.fitBoth, tiny0 + 1);
  const r1 = applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, b: 2_000_000_000_000n })));
  const B1 = BigInt(Math.round(r1.state.base * 1e6)), Q1 = BigInt(Math.round(r1.state.quote * 1e9));
  applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: false, B: B1, Q: Q1, b: 3_000_000_000_000n })));   // next event starts where the last one ended
  assert.equal(dec.fitPre - c0.fitPre, 2); assert.equal(dec.chainOk - c0.chainOk, 1);
  const before = dec.chainBad; applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0 * 2n, Q: Q0 * 2n, b: 1_000_000_000n }))); assert.equal(dec.chainBad, before + 1, "a jump in reserves between events is counted (missed event or wrong hypothesis)");
  // an event written with POST-swap reserves does not fit the pinned 'pre' interpretation → rejected, counted as fitPost
  const b = 20_000_000_000_000n, q = (Q0 * b) / (B0 - b), bad = Buffer.from(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, b }), "base64"); bad.writeBigUInt64LE(B0 - b, 8 + 8 * 5); bad.writeBigUInt64LE(Q0 + q, 8 + 8 * 6);
  const f0 = dec.fitPost; assert.equal(applyEvent({}, decodeEvent(bad.toString("base64"))), null); assert.equal(dec.fitPost, f0 + 1);
});

test("older, shorter events still decode; virtual reserves that were never seen are UNKNOWN, not zero → no state", () => {
  const e = decodeEvent(swap({ buy: false, B: B0, Q: Q0, b: 1_000_000_000n, truncate: 24 })); assert.equal(e.virtualQuote, null); assert.equal(e.creatorBps, 30);
  const n = dec.noVirtual, k = dec.unknownVirtual; assert.equal(applyEvent({}, e), null, "unknown virtual reserves: not a trustworthy state"); assert.equal(dec.unknownVirtual, k + 1);
  assert.ok(applyEvent({ vq: 0 }, e), "with the pool's virtual reserves known, the event is used"); assert.equal(dec.noVirtual, n + 2);
  const dep = { type: "deposit", poolBase: 1000n, poolQuote: 1000n, baseAmt: 1n, quoteAmt: 1n }; assert.equal(applyEvent({}, dep), null, "a deposit on a pool whose virtual reserves are unknown gives no state"); assert.ok(applyEvent({ vq: 5 }, dep));
});

test("deposit / withdraw change reserves without a swap; a fee sweep is price-neutral", () => {
  const p = {}; applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B: B0, Q: Q0, b: 1_000_000_000n }))); const q0 = p.quote, b0 = p.base;
  const wd = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(DISC.WithdrawEvent), i(1800000001), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(BigInt(Math.round(b0 * 1e6))), u(BigInt(Math.round(q0 * 1e9))), u(BigInt(Math.round(b0 * 1e6 / 2))), u(BigInt(Math.round(q0 * 1e9 / 2))), u(0), pk(9), pk(2), pk(3), pk(4), pk(5)]).toString("base64");
  const r = applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(wd)); assert.ok(Math.abs(r.state.quote - q0 / 2) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(r.state.base - b0 / 2) < 1, "half the liquidity withdrawn is visible immediately");
  const sw = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(DISC.SweepPoolFeeEvent), i(1800000002), pk(9), pk(1), pk(2), pk(3), pk(4), u(1_000_000), Buffer.from([0])]).toString("base64");
  const s = applyEvent(p, decodeEvent(sw)); assert.equal(s.changed, false); assert.ok(Math.abs(p.quote - q0 / 2) < 1e-6);
});

test("canonical pool check: pool-authority and canonical pool derivations match the standard Solana library (fixed vectors)", () => {
  const V = [["2hbAWmY7RweotBAgx2yqaNaPXUhoH7S8kVFKi2xZLsrZ", "HQyRPzqjzDC982E4xcQvYLghygbKaEPsti4ZHg76tNSA", "8zKMSLJs9XPzRU7wM9pYhKAeoszfLV14Ef2DGLax2yGm"], ["ASmhLFn6LzdRpUUEmJVjZ6GJMivHgfXAiB8jdyHoijLn", "CS2gFbQ2ZXumCn8YAaXNtLSeehUq9SCaqWbnHbkAo66u", "AgAEUW3VxcJCTmBVWWRzENmcadaJiYDBsu5JuWmLQBHD"], ["6aeTXWTQBocht8JbvdmvhffrXJQLoENL6JocByWBNpGH", "3AYjUcEFNu4ipxmFQDUA95JCshCzgZbVYyYcw8GP4D2f", "FanrqXB5FCZeog3TBg3xH7v67MF5BsGRDD1WQ4GC35XN"]];
  for (const [mint, auth, pool] of V) { assert.equal(pumpPoolAuthority(mint), auth); assert.equal(canonicalPool(mint), pool); assert.ok(isCanonical(mint, auth)); assert.ok(!isCanonical(mint, mint)); assert.ok(!onCurve(bs58dec(auth)), "a program-derived address is off the curve"); assert.ok(onCurve(bs58dec(mint))); }
});

test("REAL transaction (20 Nov 2026 validation): buy_exact_quote_in carries the user's total in quote_amount_in and the curve amount in user_quote_amount_in", () => {
  // pool reserves, virtual reserves, amounts and vault balances copied from tx-validation.json (first sample)
  const B = 38399940021850n, Q = 619809195382n, vq = 17581142980n, b = 92577266n, vaultPost = 619810735126n;
  const ev = (ix) => decodeEvent(swap({ buy: true, B, Q, vq, b, lp: 3074n, ix, raw: { quoteAmt: 1550502n, userQuote: 1536670n } }));
  const n0 = dec.fitNeither; assert.equal(applyEvent({}, ev("buy")), null, "read as an ordinary buy, the curve does not reproduce the amount"); assert.equal(dec.fitNeither, n0 + 1);
  const p = {}, r = applyEvent(p, ev("buy_exact_quote_in")); assert.ok(r, "read with the exact-quote-in field meaning, it fits");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.sol - 1536670 / 1e9) < 1e-12, "trade size = the curve amount");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.state.quote - Number(vaultPost + vq) / 1e9) < 2e-9, "derived post-swap effective reserves = the vault balance after the transaction + virtual reserves");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(p.feeBuy - (1550502 / 1536670 - 1)) < 1e-12 && Math.abs(p.feeBuy - 0.0090) < 2e-4, "fee = 20 + 5 + 65 bps, exactly what the user paid over the curve amount"); assert.equal(r.state.feeOdd, undefined);
});

FILE: older/test/dip.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// older-11 — DIP15K: a coin that has been to $100K or more comes down into $5K–$15K (owner, 25 Nov 2026).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", dips = (e) => [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("DIP15K"));
const mk = (books = "older-11") => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); if (books) e.setBooks(S.booksOf(books)); return e; };
// watched from $120K down to `end`, a trade every 20 s
const fall = (e, steps) => { let t = T0; for (const mc of steps) { swap(e, t, mc); t += 20; } return t; };

test("DIP15K: a watched peak of $120K, then the fall — bought at the first trade at or under $15K (still $5K or more), below the other books' $40K floor", () => {
  const e = mk(); fall(e, [120_000, 90_000, 60_000, 30_000, 16_000]); assert.equal(dips(e).length, 0, "$16K is not in yet");
  swap(e, T0 + 100, 14_000); const d = dips(e); assert.equal(d.length, 1); assert.equal(d[0].ag, "A0"); assert.equal(Math.round(d[0].signalMc), 14_000);
  const c = d[0].cands.find((x) => x.family === "DIP15K").detail; assert.equal(c.peak_watched_mc, 120_000); assert.equal(c.peak_evidence, "watched"); assert.equal(c.prev_mc, 16_000); assert.ok(Math.abs(c.fall - (1 - 14 / 120)) < 1e-3);
  swap(e, T0 + 120, 14_000); swap(e, T0 + 125, 14_000); assert.equal(d[0].status, "filled");
  swap(e, T0 + 140, 20_000); swap(e, T0 + 160, 13_000); assert.equal(dips(e).length, 1, "one buy per coin and age group: a second crossing does not buy again (buying again is a separate switch)");
});
test("DIP15K is not bought: without a watched $100K peak; when the coin falls through the whole band in one step; when it is first seen already inside the band; or in a legacy run", () => {
  const a = mk(); fall(a, [80_000, 40_000, 16_000, 14_000]); assert.equal(dips(a).length, 0, "peak watched was $80K");
  const b = mk(); fall(b, [120_000, 60_000, 16_000, 4_000]); assert.equal(dips(b).length, 0, "from $16K straight to $4K");
  const c = mk(); fall(c, [12_000, 11_000, 13_000]); assert.equal(dips(c).length, 0, "first seen at $12K: nothing was watched arriving");
  const L = mk(null); fall(L, [120_000, 60_000, 16_000, 14_000]); assert.equal(dips(L).length, 0, "legacy run: the book is off"); assert.ok(L.stats.inactiveCands >= 1);
});
test("DIP15K with the owner's gates: the provider's all-time high stands in for the watched peak, and decides at the buy", async () => {
  const mkG = (ath) => { const e = mk(); e.gates = S.GATES_11; let now = T0; e.clock = () => now; const info = { ath_price: ath, total_supply: "1000000000", total_fee: "20", link: { twitter_username: "x/status/1" }, stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.22", top_bundler_trader_percentage: "0.1", top_rat_trader_percentage: "0.05", top70_sniper_hold_rate: "0.01" } };
    e.readGate = () => Promise.resolve(info); e.at = (t) => { now = t; return new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r)); }; return e; };
  const a = mkG(0.00025); fall(a, [80_000, 40_000, 16_000]); await a.at(T0 + 60.2); swap(a, T0 + 61, 14_000); await a.at(T0 + 61.3);
  const d = dips(a); assert.equal(d.length, 1, "peak watched was only $80K, but the gates are on"); assert.equal(d[0].cands[0].detail.peak_evidence, "provider all-time high (tested at the buy)");
  swap(a, T0 + 64, 14_000); swap(a, T0 + 65, 14_000); assert.equal(d[0].status, "filled"); assert.equal(d[0].gate.verdict, "pass"); assert.equal(Math.round(d[0].gate.reading.ath_usd), 250_000);
  const b = mkG(0.00006); fall(b, [80_000, 40_000, 16_000]); swap(b, T0 + 61, 14_000); const x = dips(b)[0]; await b.at(T0 + 61.3); swap(b, T0 + 64, 14_000); // (a cancelled entry leaves the engine's memory: keep the object)
  assert.equal(x.status, "cancelled"); assert.deepEqual(x.gate.fails, ["all-time high $60K: has not reached $100K"]);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/exits.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { newEntry, advance, observe, versionsFor, COST, inventories, entryDone, unresolvedCount } from "../src/book.mjs";
import { liqValue, sellSol } from "../src/fills.mjs";

const st = (quote, t, base = 2e8) => ({ base, quote, feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003, t });
const mk = (families = ["LOW4H"]) => newEntry({ mint: "M", pool: "P", ag: "A1", t: 1000, families, features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1 });
const yes = () => true, no = () => false;
// a tiny driver: `cur` is the state in force; ev(newState) = orders due before it run on the old state, then it is observed
function drv(e, s0) { let cur = s0; return { ev(s, trusted = yes) { advance(e, s.t, cur, trusted); cur = s; observe(e, s, s.t); }, tick(t, trusted = yes) { advance(e, t, cur, trusted); }, get cur() { return cur; } }; }
const trail = (e, stop, stall = false) => e.versions.find((v) => v.stop === stop && v.policy === "trail" && v.stall === stall);
const scale = (e, stop) => e.versions.find((v) => v.stop === stop && v.policy === "scale" && !v.stall);

test("buy fills AT signal + 1 s from the state in force then — no other trader is needed, and a later swap does not move the fill", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999));
  d.tick(1000.5); assert.equal(e.status, "pending");
  d.tick(1001.2); assert.equal(e.status, "filled"); assert.equal(e.fill.t, 1001); assert.equal(e.fill.delay_s, 0); assert.equal(e.fill.state_age_s, 2);
  const e2 = mk(), d2 = drv(e2, st(200, 999)); d2.ev(st(400, 1005));         // first swap after the signal arrives 5 s later, at double the price
  assert.equal(e2.fill.t, 1001); assert.equal(e2.fill.quote_sol, 200, "priced on the state in force at 1001, not on the later swap");
});

test("a swap landing inside the 1 s delay IS the state in force at the scheduled time", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.ev(st(260, 1000.6)); d.tick(1001.1);
  assert.equal(e.fill.quote_sol, 260);
});

test("buy pricing timeout: no trustworthy state within 5 s → cancelled as a data timeout; a trustworthy state inside 5 s is used with its delay", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1003, no); assert.equal(e.status, "pending"); d.tick(1006.1, no);
  assert.equal(e.status, "cancelled"); assert.match(e.flags[0], /pricing\/data timeout/);
  const e2 = mk(); advance(e2, 1002, st(200, 999), no); observe(e2, st(210, 1003), 1003);
  assert.equal(e2.status, "filled"); assert.equal(e2.fill.delay_s, 2); assert.equal(e2.fill.quote_sol, 210);
});

test("four trailing stops exit independently; the sale prices at trigger + 0.7 s on the state in force then — not at the trigger level", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001);
  d.ev(st(380, 1100));                        // peak (below 2× so the scale policy stays out of this test)
  d.ev(st(290, 1200));                        // ≈ −24% in pool SOL → the 20% stop triggers; 25/30/40 do not
  assert.equal(trail(e, 0.2).status, "selling"); assert.equal(trail(e, 0.25).status, "open"); assert.equal(trail(e, 0.4).status, "open");
  d.ev(st(150, 1200.3));                      // a dump lands 0.3 s later, BEFORE trigger + 0.7 s → it is the state in force at 1200.7
  d.tick(1200.9);
  const x = trail(e, 0.2); assert.equal(x.status, "closed"); assert.equal(x.exit.t, 1200.7); assert.equal(x.exit.delay_s, 0);
  assert.ok(x.exit.sol < x.trigValue * 0.6, "sold far below the trigger value: a trailing stop is a trigger, not a guaranteed price");
  assert.equal(trail(e, 0.4).status, "selling"); // the dump took the 40% version through its own stop
});

test("a triggered sell survives a data failure: it waits, records the real delay, and is never dropped — even past 24 h", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001); d.ev(st(380, 1100)); d.ev(st(280, 1200));
  d.tick(1260, no); assert.equal(trail(e, 0.2).status, "selling");
  advance(e, 1300, d.cur, no); observe(e, st(100, 1300), 1300);              // first trustworthy state 99.3 s late, much lower
  const x = trail(e, 0.2); assert.equal(x.status, "closed"); assert.ok(Math.abs(x.exit.delay_s - 99.3) < 1e-6); assert.ok(x.exit.ret < -0.4);
  // no price for more than 24 h: marked unresolved (visible exposure) but STILL pending, still asking, and it closes when a price returns
  const e2 = mk(), d2 = drv(e2, st(200, 999)); d2.tick(1001); d2.ev(st(380, 1100)); d2.ev(st(280, 1200)); d2.tick(1200.7 + 86401, no);
  const y = trail(e2, 0.2); assert.equal(y.status, "selling"); assert.ok(y.unresolvedSince, "flagged unresolved"); assert.equal(unresolvedCount(e2), e2.versions.filter((v) => v.unresolvedSince).length);
  assert.ok(inventories(e2).length > 0, "its inventory is still requested"); assert.equal(entryDone(e2), false);
  const t3 = 1200.7 + 90000; advance(e2, t3, d2.cur, no); observe(e2, st(150, t3), t3);
  assert.equal(y.status, "closed"); assert.ok(y.exit.was_unresolved_s > 3000); assert.ok(y.exit.delay_s > 86400);
});

test("CONT-STALL: at fill + 60 min, closes if after-cost value never reached 1.15× cost; plain CONT is unaffected", () => {
  const e = mk(["CONT", "CONT-STALL"]), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001); d.ev(st(220, 2000)); d.tick(1001 + 3600 + 1);
  for (const v of versionsFor(e, "CONT-STALL")) { assert.equal(v.reason, "stall"); assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.exit.t, 1001 + 3600 + 0.7); }
  for (const v of versionsFor(e, "CONT")) assert.equal(v.status, "open");
  const e2 = mk(["CONT", "CONT-STALL"]), d2 = drv(e2, st(200, 999)); d2.tick(1001); d2.ev(st(260, 2000)); d2.ev(st(250, 2100)); d2.tick(1001 + 3601);
  for (const v of versionsFor(e2, "CONT-STALL")) assert.notEqual(v.reason, "stall"); // peaked above 1.15× → survives
});

test("24 h maximum hold from the fill, on a quiet coin, with no further trade needed", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001); d.tick(1001 + 86400 + 5);
  for (const v of e.versions) { assert.equal(v.reason, "max-hold"); assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.exit.hold_s, 86401); }
  assert.ok(e.versions[0].exit.ret < 0 && e.versions[0].exit.ret > -0.02, "flat price → small loss = fees + impact + transaction costs");
});

test("scale-out: half the ORIGINAL tokens sold when full value reaches 2× cost; the rest trails on a normalised high-water mark", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001); const tok = e.fill.tok;
  d.ev(st(420, 1100));                        // full liquidation value ≥ 2× cost → scale trigger
  assert.equal(scale(e, 0.25).status, "scaling"); assert.equal(trail(e, 0.25).status, "open");
  d.tick(1101);
  const v = scale(e, 0.25); assert.equal(v.status, "open"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.tok - tok / 2) < 1e-6); assert.ok(v.half.sol > 0.19 && v.half.sol < 0.23);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.peak - liqValue(d.cur, tok / 2)) < 1e-9, "selling half is not a 50% drawdown: the peak is rescaled to the remaining tokens");
  d.ev(st(400, 1150)); assert.equal(v.status, "open");                        // −5%: nowhere near the 25% trail
  d.ev(st(300, 1200)); d.tick(1201);                                          // −28.6% from 420 → trail on the remainder
  assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.reason, "trail");
  const expectLast = sellSol(st(300, 0), tok / 2) - 0.0005; assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.last_sol - expectLast) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.sol - (v.half.sol + expectLast)) < 1e-12); assert.ok(v.exit.ret > trail(e, 0.25).exit.ret, "here banking half at 2× beat the pure trail");
});

test("scale-out never sells the same tokens twice: a trail trigger during the pending half-sale becomes ONE full sale", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001); d.ev(st(420, 1100)); const v = scale(e, 0.2); assert.equal(v.status, "scaling");
  d.ev(st(300, 1100.3));                      // collapses before the half-sale's 0.7 s is up
  assert.equal(v.status, "selling"); assert.equal(v.reason, "trail"); d.tick(1102);
  assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.half, null); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.sol - (sellSol(st(300, 0), e.fill.tok) - 0.0005)) < 1e-9);
});

test("runner tracking continues after an exit and is separate from realised profit", () => {
  const e = mk(), d = drv(e, st(200, 999)); d.tick(1001); d.ev(st(150, 1100)); d.tick(1102); d.ev(st(700, 5000));
  assert.ok(trail(e, 0.2).status === "closed" && trail(e, 0.2).exit.ret < 0); assert.ok(e.runner.reached[2] && e.runner.reached[3]); assert.ok(e.runner.maxX >= 3);
});

test("liquidation value = effective-reserve quote, less the pool's sell fee, less the transaction cost", () => {
  const s = st(200, 0), tok = 1e5, gross = (200 * tok) / (2e8 + tok); assert.ok(Math.abs(liqValue(s, tok) - (gross * 0.997 - 0.0005)) < 1e-12); assert.equal(COST, 0.2005);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/fast.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
const setup = (createAgo = 9 * H) => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - createAgo }); return e; };
const has = (e, f) => [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes(f));

test("FAST: fresh-crossing times differ by window — 5M fires first, 1M later; entries are shared only when signals fire together", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  // a steady climb: +1%/10 s. 5-min low is the flat 100K → FAST-5M (2×) when price hits 200K; the 1-min low trails the price, so FAST-1M never doubles
  let mc = 100_000; for (let t = T0 + 600; t < T0 + 600 + 290; t += 10) { mc *= 1.03; swap(e, t, mc); }
  const f5 = has(e, "FAST-5M"); assert.equal(f5.length, 1); assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-1M").length, 0);
  assert.ok(has(e, "FAST150-5M")[0].decisionT < f5[0].decisionT, "the 1.5× version of the same window fires earlier and is its own entry");
  assert.ok(f5[0].evidence.obs.length > 10 && f5[0].evidence.prev && f5[0].evidence.trigger.quote_eff > 0, "window observations, previous observation and trigger inputs stored once on the entry");
});

test("FAST-1M: a sudden double inside one minute fires all three windows on the same update → one shared entry", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  swap(e, T0 + 605, 150_000); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000);
  const f1 = has(e, "FAST-1M"); assert.equal(f1.length, 1);
  assert.ok(f1[0].families.includes("FAST-3M") && f1[0].families.includes("FAST-5M"));
  assert.ok(!f1[0].families.includes("FAST150-1M"), "the 1.5× crossing happened on the earlier update (150K) and is a different entry");
  assert.equal(has(e, "FAST150-1M")[0].signalMc < 151_000, true);
});

test("FAST window boundary: a low just outside the 60 s window does not count; the price standing at the window start does", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  swap(e, T0 + 600, 150_000);                         // price standing from here
  swap(e, T0 + 665, 205_000);                         // 65 s later: the 100K prints are outside 60 s; the standing price in the window is 150K
  assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-1M").length, 0); assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-3M").length, 1);
});

test("FAST needs a covered window: nothing fires across a feed outage, and held (replayed) events never create signals", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  e.feedDown(T0 + 601); e.feedUp(T0 + 620); swap(e, T0 + 621, 210_000); swap(e, T0 + 630, 215_000);
  assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-1M").length + has(e, "FAST-3M").length + has(e, "FAST-5M").length, 0);
  const e2 = setup(); path(e2, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  e2.onSwap({ t: T0 + 605, mint: "MintAAApump", pool: "PoolA", base: 2e8, quote: (210_000 / 1e11) * 2e8, feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u", replay: true });
  assert.equal(e2.entries.size, 0);
});

test("SECOND-WAVE: seed on a 2× FAST trigger → ≥ 25% pullback freezes the high → buy strictly above it; one attempt; expires after 60 min", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  swap(e, T0 + 605, 205_000);                         // seed (FAST fires)
  swap(e, T0 + 700, 260_000);                         // running high H = 260K
  swap(e, T0 + 800, 190_000);                         // ≤ 0.75 × 260K → frozen high 260K
  swap(e, T0 + 900, 260_000); assert.equal(has(e, "SECOND-WAVE").length, 0, "equal to the frozen high is not strictly above");
  swap(e, T0 + 910, 262_000);
  const sw = has(e, "SECOND-WAVE"); assert.equal(sw.length, 1);
  const d = sw[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "SECOND-WAVE").detail; assert.ok(Math.abs(d.frozen_high / (260_000 / 1e9) - 1) < 1e-9); assert.deepEqual(d.seed_windows.sort(), ["FAST-1M", "FAST-3M", "FAST-5M"]);
  swap(e, T0 + 1000, 150_000); swap(e, T0 + 1100, 300_000); assert.equal(has(e, "SECOND-WAVE").length, 1, "no repeated attempt in the age group");

  const e2 = setup(); path(e2, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); swap(e2, T0 + 605, 205_000); swap(e2, T0 + 800, 150_000);
  swap(e2, T0 + 605 + 3601, 210_000); assert.equal(has(e2, "SECOND-WAVE").length, 0, "reclaim after 60 min is expired");
});

test("90-day ceiling at entry: a coin older than 90 days is not evaluated; exactly the same move on an 89-day coin enters", () => {
  const old = setup(91 * 24 * H); path(old, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); swap(old, T0 + 605, 210_000); assert.equal(old.entries.size, 0); assert.ok(old.stats.tooOld > 0);
  const ok = setup(89 * 24 * H); path(ok, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); swap(ok, T0 + 605, 210_000); assert.ok(has(ok, "FAST-1M").length === 1); assert.equal(has(ok, "FAST-1M")[0].ag, "A4");
});

test("SOL/USD stale: new entries are suspended, open positions are still managed in SOL, and the stale period is not covered history", () => {
  let usd = 100; const e = mkEngine({ solUsd: () => usd }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H });
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); swap(e, T0 + 605, 150_000); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 620, 206_000);
  const pos = has(e, "FAST-1M")[0]; assert.equal(pos.status, "filled"); const n = e.entries.size;
  usd = null; swap(e, T0 + 700, 70_000); swap(e, T0 + 702, 70_000);         // price collapses (−66%: through every stop, the 60% one included) while the USD reference is stale
  assert.ok(pos.versions.filter((v) => v.policy === "trail").every((v) => v.status === "closed"), "stops still sold");
  usd = 100; swap(e, T0 + 760, 90_000); swap(e, T0 + 770, 200_000);
  assert.equal(e.entries.size, n, "no new entry from a window that spans the stale period");
});

test("startup tag: entries in the first 24 h after the project's first start are tagged, later ones are not", () => {
  const e = mkEngine({ firstStart: T0 }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H });
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); swap(e, T0 + 605, 210_000); assert.equal([...e.entries.values()][0].startup, true);
  const e2 = mkEngine({ firstStart: T0 - 2 * 86400 }); e2.feedUp(T0); e2.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H });
  path(e2, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); swap(e2, T0 + 605, 210_000); assert.equal([...e2.entries.values()][0].startup, false);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/gates.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// older-11 — the owner's gates. One provider reading per buy signal, judged at the time of the buy; missing is never zero.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
import { parseInfo as parseRaw, judge, gatesFor } from "../src/gates.mjs";
const parseInfo = (info, supply = null, pump = true) => parseRaw(info, supply, pump); // the readings in these tests are of a verified pump.fun coin unless a test says otherwise
const M = "MintAAApump";
// the provider's real answer of 25 Nov 2026 10:02 UTC for AGmKjJ…Cqxqpump (fields the gates read; values as returned: strings)
const REAL = { ath_price: 0.00019252105, total_fee: "64.55971947358077", trade_fee: "37.84892266864744", holder_count: 484, total_supply: "965557393.800231", creation_timestamp: 1791667383, open_timestamp: 1791668325, launchpad: "pump",
  link: { twitter_username: "_numanuk/status/2109033918683111935", website: "" }, stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.2281", top_rat_trader_percentage: "0", top_bundler_trader_percentage: "0.0344", top70_sniper_hold_rate: "0.0217269507", dev_team_hold_rate: "0", creator_hold_rate: "0" },
  wallet_tags_stat: { sniper_wallets: 0, rat_trader_wallets: 0, bundler_wallets: 490 }, dev: { creator_token_status: "creator_close" } };
const good = (o = {}) => { const j = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(REAL)); j.stat.top_bundler_trader_percentage = "0.12"; j.stat.top_rat_trader_percentage = "0.06"; Object.assign(j.stat, o.stat || {}); if (o.link) j.link = o.link; if ("total_fee" in o) j.total_fee = o.total_fee; if ("ath_price" in o) j.ath_price = o.ath_price; return j; };

test("parseInfo reads the provider's real answer: fractions, fees in SOL, a tweet as the Twitter link, all-time high as a market cap; anything absent is unknown, not zero", () => {
  const r = parseInfo(REAL); assert.equal(r.top10, 0.2281); assert.equal(r.bundlers, 0.0344); assert.equal(r.insiders, 0); assert.equal(r.snipers, 0.0217269507);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.fees_sol - 64.5597) < 1e-3); assert.equal(r.twitter, "_numanuk/status/2109033918683111935"); assert.ok(Math.abs(r.ath_usd - 185890.12) < 0.01, "highest price × the supply in the reading (965.6M after burns): a lower bound, not × a full billion"); assert.match(r.ath_basis, /provider total_supply/); assert.equal(r.holders, 484); assert.equal(r.bundler_wallets, 490);
  const e = parseInfo({}); for (const k of ["top10", "bundlers", "insiders", "snipers", "fees_sol", "twitter", "ath_usd"]) assert.equal(e[k], null, k + " unknown");
  assert.equal(parseInfo({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "" }, link: { twitter_username: "" } }).top10, null, "an empty string is unknown"); assert.equal(parseInfo({ link: { twitter_username: " " } }).twitter, "", "a link block with no Twitter = known: none");
});
test("judge with the owner's settings: the real coin fails on bundlers and insiders only; each limit is inclusive except 'snipers under 5%'; missing figures fail as missing", () => {
  const G = S.GATES_11, v = judge(parseInfo(REAL), G, "HH-BREAK"); assert.equal(v.pass, false); assert.deepEqual(v.fails, ["bundlers 3.44%: below 5%", "insiders 0%: below 2.5%"]); assert.deepEqual(v.missing, []);
  const ok = judge(parseInfo(good()), G, "HH-BREAK"); assert.equal(ok.pass, true); assert.deepEqual(ok.options, { bundlers_main: true, bundlers_second: true, insiders_main: true, insiders_second: true });
  const J = (o, f = "HH-BREAK") => judge(parseInfo(good(o)), G, f);
  assert.equal(J({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.18" } }).pass, true, "18% is inside"); assert.equal(J({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.1799" } }).pass, false); assert.equal(J({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.30" } }).pass, true); assert.equal(J({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.3001" } }).pass, false);
  assert.equal(J({ stat: { top70_sniper_hold_rate: "0.0499" } }).pass, true); assert.equal(J({ stat: { top70_sniper_hold_rate: "0.05" } }).pass, false, "5% is not under 5%");
  assert.equal(J({ total_fee: "5" }).pass, true); assert.deepEqual(J({ total_fee: "4.99" }).fails, ["total fees 4.99 SOL: below 5 SOL"]);
  assert.deepEqual(J({ link: { twitter_username: "" } }).fails, ["no Twitter link"]); assert.deepEqual(J({ ath_price: 0.00009 }).fails, ["all-time high $87K: has not reached $100K"]);
  // the second options: bundlers 6% is outside the main range (7–50%) but inside the second (5–20%) — bought and tagged
  const b6 = J({ stat: { top_bundler_trader_percentage: "0.06" } }); assert.equal(b6.pass, true); assert.equal(b6.options.bundlers_main, false); assert.equal(b6.options.bundlers_second, true);
  const i30 = J({ stat: { top_rat_trader_percentage: "0.30" } }); assert.equal(i30.pass, true); assert.equal(i30.options.insiders_main, false); assert.equal(i30.options.insiders_second, true);
  assert.equal(J({ stat: { top_bundler_trader_percentage: "0.51" } }).pass, false); assert.equal(J({ stat: { top_rat_trader_percentage: "0.41" } }).pass, false);
  const miss = judge(parseInfo({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.2" } }), G, "HH-BREAK"); assert.equal(miss.pass, false); assert.deepEqual(miss.fails, []); assert.ok(miss.missing.includes("insiders") && miss.missing.includes("total fees"));
});
test("a book's own settings: the rug books need top 10 UNDER 12% and are not asked for the $100K all-time high; a clock book uses its base book's settings", () => {
  const G = S.GATES_11, at = (x) => parseInfo(good({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: x }, ath_price: 0.00001 }));
  assert.equal(judge(at("0.08"), G, "RUG450").pass, true); assert.equal(judge(at("0.1199"), G, "RUG950").pass, true); assert.equal(judge(at("0"), G, "RUG450").pass, true, "no floor");
  assert.deepEqual(judge(at("0.12"), G, "RUG450").fails, ["top 10 holders 12%: not under 12%"]); assert.equal(judge(at("0.20"), G, "RUG950").pass, false);
  assert.equal(judge(at("0.08"), G, "HH-BREAK").pass, false, "the common 18–30% range and the $100K test still apply to the other books"); assert.equal(judge(at("0.08"), G, "HH-BREAK-4H").pass, false);
  assert.equal(gatesFor(G, "HH-BREAK-4H"), gatesFor(G, "HH-BREAK")); assert.equal(gatesFor(G, "RUG950").fees_sol.buy[0], 5, "5 SOL applies to the rug books too");
});

// ── engine: a 2-hour-old coin, $320K traded in five minutes → VOL300K (legacy list). The reading is supplied by the test.
const setup = (reader, { at = 0.2 } = {}) => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); e.gates = S.GATES_11; let now = T0, pend = [];
  e.clock = () => now; e.readGate = (mint) => new Promise((res, rej) => pend.push({ mint, res, rej })); e.answer = (t, info) => { now = t; const p = pend.shift(); if (info instanceof Error) p.rej(info); else p.res(info); return new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r)); }; e.pendingReads = () => pend.length; return e; };
const burst = (e) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 10 }); for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) swap(e, T0 + 600 + k * 30, 100_000, { sol: 800 }); };
const entry = (e) => [...e.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("VOL300K"));

test("a buy signal asks for ONE reading; a reading that passes and arrived before the buy lets the buy fill, and the options are recorded on it", async () => {
  const e = setup(); burst(e); const en = entry(e); assert.ok(en && en.status === "pending"); assert.equal(e.pendingReads(), 1); assert.equal(en.gate.req_t, en.decisionT ?? en.gate.req_t);
  await e.answer(en.gate.req_t + 0.4, good()); assert.equal(en.gate.ms, 400);
  swap(e, en.schedT + 2, 100_000); swap(e, en.schedT + 3, 100_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "pass"); assert.equal(en.gate.options.bundlers_main, true); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.passed, 1); assert.equal(en.gate.reading.top10, 0.2281);
});
test("a reading that fails cancels the buy and says why — the real coin: bundlers and insiders", async () => {
  const e = setup(); burst(e); const en = entry(e); await e.answer(en.gate.req_t + 0.3, REAL); swap(e, en.schedT + 2, 100_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "fail"); assert.deepEqual(en.gate.fails, ["bundlers 3.44%: below 5%", "insiders 0%: below 2.5%"]);
  assert.ok(en.flags.some((f) => /cancelled before the buy: the owner's gates: bundlers 3\.44%: below 5%; insiders 0%: below 2\.5%/.test(f))); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.failed, 1); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.fails.bundlers, 1); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.fails.insiders, 1);
});
test("no reading by the time of the buy → cancelled, never treated as zero; a reading that arrives afterwards is kept on the entry and marked late", async () => {
  const e = setup(); burst(e); const en = entry(e); swap(e, en.schedT + 2, 100_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "no reading"); assert.ok(en.flags.some((f) => /no provider reading had arrived by the time of the buy/.test(f))); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.no_reading, 1);
  await e.answer(en.schedT + 4, good()); assert.equal(en.gate.late, true); assert.equal(en.gate.reading.bundlers, 0.12, "what it would have said is recorded"); assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled");
  // …and a reading that arrived 0.3 s AFTER the scheduled buy, seen when the buy is processed: still too late
  const e2 = setup(); burst(e2); const en2 = entry(e2); await e2.answer(en2.schedT + 0.3, good()); swap(e2, en2.schedT + 2, 100_000);
  assert.equal(en2.status, "cancelled"); assert.ok(en2.flags.some((f) => /arrived 0\.30 s after the time of the buy/.test(f)));
});
test("a failed request (refused by the provider) cancels the buy with the provider's error; with the gates off nothing is asked and the buy fills as before", async () => {
  const e = setup(); burst(e); const en = entry(e); await e.answer(en.gate.req_t + 0.2, new Error("GMGN rate limited")); swap(e, en.schedT + 2, 100_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled"); assert.ok(en.flags.some((f) => /the provider reading failed \(GMGN rate limited\)/.test(f))); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.errors, 1);
  const L = mkEngine(); L.feedUp(T0); L.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); burst(L); const el = entry(L); swap(L, el.schedT + 2, 100_000); swap(L, el.schedT + 3, 100_000);
  assert.equal(el.status, "filled"); assert.equal(el.gate, undefined); assert.equal(L.stats.gate, undefined);
});
test("books that fire together are judged each by its own settings: the one that fails is taken off the buy, the other fills", async () => {
  const e = setup(); e.gates = { ...S.GATES_11, byFamily: { ...S.GATES_11.byFamily, VOL300K: { fees_sol: { buy: [100, null] } } } }; e.setBooks(S.booksOf("VOL100K,VOL300K"));
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 10 }); swap(e, T0 + 600, 100_000, { sol: 3300 }); // $330K in one trade: both volume books fire on the same observation and share ONE buy
  assert.equal(e.entries.size, 1); assert.equal(e.pendingReads(), 1, "one reading for the shared buy");
  const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; assert.deepEqual([...en.families].sort(), ["VOL100K", "VOL300K"]); await e.answer(en.gate.req_t + 0.2, good()); swap(e, en.schedT + 2, 100_000); swap(e, en.schedT + 3, 100_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.deepEqual(en.families, ["VOL100K"]); assert.deepEqual(en.gate.dropped, { VOL300K: ["total fees 64.56 SOL: below 100 SOL"] }); assert.ok(en.flags.some((f) => /books taken off this buy by their gates: VOL300K/.test(f)));
});

// older-11 — the GMGN client's two kinds of call: a buy-check reading goes first and fails fast; history repair leaves a reserve.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
process.env.GMGN_API_KEY = "test-key"; process.env.GMGN_WPS = "1000"; process.env.GMGN_HOST = "http://gmgn.test";
const { gcall, tokenInfoFirst, gm, _reset } = await import("../src/gmgn.mjs");
const realFetch = globalThis.fetch; let calls = [], plan = [];
const answer = ({ status = 200, body = { code: 0, data: { ok: 1 } }, remaining = null, resetInS = null } = {}) => ({ status, headers: new Headers({ ...(remaining != null ? { "x-ratelimit-limit": "5", "x-ratelimit-remaining": String(remaining) } : {}), ...(resetInS != null ? { "x-ratelimit-reset": String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + resetInS) } : {}) }), text: async () => JSON.stringify(body) });
const install = () => { calls = []; plan = []; _reset(); gm.reserve = 0; globalThis.fetch = async (url, o) => { const u = new URL(url); calls.push({ path: u.pathname, t: Date.now(), key: o.headers["X-APIKEY"] }); return answer(plan.shift() || {}); }; };
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
test.after(() => { globalThis.fetch = realFetch; });

test("a buy-check reading is sent at once, ahead of repair calls that are waiting, and its time is recorded", async () => {
  install(); plan.push({ status: 429, body: { code: 429, error: "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" }, resetInS: 1 }); // the first repair call is refused: the queue pauses 0.5–1.5 s (whole-second reset time + 0.5 s)
  const a = gcall("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol" }).catch((e) => e.message); await sleep(20);
  const b = gcall("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol" }); await sleep(20); // queued behind the pause
  const before = gm.first.ok, info = await tokenInfoFirst("MintX", 3000); // waits out the short pause (≤ maxWaitMs) but not the queue
  assert.deepEqual(info, { ok: 1 }); assert.equal(calls[1].path, "/v1/token/info", "the reading went out before the queued repair call"); assert.equal(gm.first.ok, before + 1);
  assert.equal(await a, "GMGN rate limited"); await b; assert.equal(calls[2].path, "/v1/market/token_kline");
});
test("when the provider has said to wait longer than the reading can wait, the reading is not sent and fails at once", async () => {
  install(); plan.push({ status: 429, body: { code: 429, error: "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" }, resetInS: 15 });
  await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol" }).catch(() => {}); const n = calls.length, ns = gm.first.not_sent, t = Date.now();
  await assert.rejects(tokenInfoFirst("MintX", 300), /allowance exhausted for another 1[45]\.\d s/); assert.ok(Date.now() - t < 100, "failed fast"); assert.equal(calls.length, n, "nothing was sent"); assert.equal(gm.first.not_sent, ns + 1);
});
test("with a reserve set, a repair call is held back while it would take the allowance below the reserve, and goes when the allowance refills; a reading still goes at once", async () => {
  install(); gm.reserve = 2; plan.push({ remaining: 3, resetInS: 2 }); // after this answer: 3 units left until the reset in ~1–2 s
  await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol" }); const t0 = Date.now(), hb = gm.held_back;
  const repair = gcall("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol" }); await sleep(100); assert.equal(calls.length, 1, "weight 2 would leave 1 < reserve 2: held back"); assert.ok(gm.held_back > hb);
  await tokenInfoFirst("MintX"); assert.equal(calls[1].path, "/v1/token/info", "the reading used the reserved units");
  await repair; assert.equal(calls[2].path, "/v1/market/token_kline"); assert.ok(calls[2].t - t0 >= 900, "sent only after the reset time");
});
test("without a reserve the repair queue is exactly as before: in order, nothing held back; and the key never appears in an error", async () => {
  install(); plan.push({ remaining: 1, resetInS: 5 }); const hb = gm.held_back; await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol" }); await gcall("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol" }); assert.equal(calls.length, 2); assert.equal(gm.held_back, hb);
  plan.push({ body: { code: 40003, error: "BAD", message: "nope" } }); const err = await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol" }).catch((e) => e.message); assert.ok(!/test-key/.test(err)); assert.equal(calls[2].key, "test-key");
});
test("review of bbe1115: a reading is not sent into an allowance the provider has just reported as used up, and calls in flight share one count", async () => {
  install(); plan.push({ remaining: 0, resetInS: 15 }); // a successful answer that says: nothing left for about 15 s
  await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol" }); const n = calls.length, ns = gm.first.not_sent, t = Date.now();
  await assert.rejects(tokenInfoFirst("MintX", 300), /allowance exhausted for another 1[345]\.\d s/); assert.ok(Date.now() - t < 100, "failed fast"); assert.equal(calls.length, n, "not sent: it would only have been refused"); assert.equal(gm.first.not_sent, ns + 1);
  install(); plan.push({ remaining: 2, resetInS: 15 }); await gcall("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol" }); // two units left
  const a = tokenInfoFirst("A"), b = tokenInfoFirst("B"); await assert.rejects(tokenInfoFirst("C", 300), /allowance exhausted/, "the third finds the two units already taken by the readings still in flight");
  await a; await b; assert.equal(calls.length, 3);
});

FILE: older/test/helpers.mjs

// Synthetic market for tests: one PumpSwap pool, SOL = $100, supply 1e9, pool base fixed at 2e8 tokens so
// market cap $X ⇔ quote reserve X × 0.002 SOL (e.g. $100K ⇔ 200 SOL, liquidity $40K).
import { Engine } from "../src/engine.mjs";
export const SOLUSD = 100, BASE = 2e8;
export const quoteFor = (mc) => (mc / (SOLUSD * 1e9)) * BASE;
import { SPL_TOKEN } from "../src/risk.mjs";
/** FIXTURES (ninth review). The production guards are not switched off for the tests; the synthetic market declares what a
 *  real feed would supply:
 *   tokens — every synthetic coin is a CLASSIC SPL token (the program has no transfer fee, so its fee is known) unless the test
 *            passes `tokens: null` and sets the coin's token program / mint-account evidence itself;
 *   stream — every synthetic event arrives 1 s after its block (ev.lag overrides it) and other pools' traffic keeps arriving
 *            on time between events and at every clock tick, as on a busy program. `stream: "manual"`: the test feeds the
 *            stream's delay itself (eng.noteLag, or swap(…, { lag })) — nothing is assumed current. */
export function mkEngine(opts = {}) {
  const logs = [], { tokens = SPL_TOKEN, stream = "current", ...rest } = opts; const e = new Engine({ solUsd: () => SOLUSD, log: (s) => logs.push(s), ...rest }); e.logs = logs;
  if (tokens) { const coin = e.coin.bind(e); e.coin = (mint, o = {}) => { const c = coin(mint, o); if (c.info.token_program == null && !c.info.risk) c.info.token_program = tokens; return c; }; }
  // the fixture drives the stream exactly as feed.mjs does: one engine.tx per transaction (delay noted → states installed → txDone)
  const manual = stream === "manual", keep = (upTo) => { const L = e.stream; if (L.at == null) { e.tx(upTo, 1); return; } while (L.at + 4 < upTo) e.tx(L.at + 4, 1); };
  const onEvent = e.onEvent.bind(e); let inTx = false;
  e.onEvent = (ev) => { if (ev.replay || inTx) return onEvent(ev); const lag = ev.lag != null ? ev.lag : manual ? null : 1; if (lag == null) return onEvent(ev);
    if (!manual) keep(ev.t); let r; inTx = true; try { e.tx(ev.t, lag, () => { r = onEvent(ev); }); } finally { inTx = false; } return r; };
  const tick = e.tick.bind(e); e.tick = (now) => { if (!manual) { keep(now); e.tx(now, 1); } return tick(now); };
  const feedUp = e.feedUp.bind(e); e.feedUp = (t) => { feedUp(t); if (!manual) e.tx(t, 1); }; // the first transaction on the connection arrives at once and on time
  return e;
}
export function swap(eng, t, mc, { mint = "MintAAApump", pool = "PoolA", sol = 1, isBuy = true, user = "u1", feeBps = 30, lag = undefined } = {}) {
  eng.onSwap({ t, mint, pool, base: BASE, quote: quoteFor(mc), feeBps, sol, isBuy, user, ...(lag !== undefined ? { lag } : {}) });
}
/** trades every `step` s from t0 to t1 (exclusive) at a market cap given by f(t) */
export function path(eng, t0, t1, f, step = 60, o = {}) { for (let t = t0; t < t1; t += step) swap(eng, t, f(t), typeof o === "function" ? o(t) : o); }
export const H = 3600, T0 = 1_800_000_000; // a round epoch for the tests
