// older-11 — LABEL, DON'T CANCEL (owner 25 Nov 2026; ChatGPT's second opinion on c1d543c) and the two execution faults it found.
// Every signal is paper-bought as without the gates; the provider reading is recorded and each book on the buy is labelled:
// passed / failed in time, passed / failed late, or no reading. The site filters the SAME buys into three views.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { booksApi, checkOf } from "../src/page.mjs";
import { num, athUsd, parseInfo } from "../src/gates.mjs";
import { saveWarm, loadWarm } from "../src/warm.mjs";
import { startBackfill, bf } from "../src/backfill.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", tick = () => new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
const GOOD = { ath_price: 0.0004, total_supply: "1000000000", total_fee: "20", link: { twitter_username: "x" }, stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.22", top_bundler_trader_percentage: "0.1", top_rat_trader_percentage: "0.05", top70_sniper_hold_rate: "0.01" } };
const BAD = { ...GOOD, stat: { ...GOOD.stat, top_10_holder_rate: "0.5" } };
const RUN = { name: "t", books: ["FAST-1M"], gates: true, gate_mode: "label", rebuy: false, min_age_h: 6, sampled: false };
// a 20-hour-old coin, FAST-1M only: ten quiet minutes at $100K, the double at T0 + 610, the buy at T0 + 611
const mk = (first, late = undefined) => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); e.rebuy = false; e.setBooks(["FAST-1M"]); e.gates = S.GATES_11; e.gateMode = "label"; let now = T0; e.clock = () => now; e.at = async (t) => { now = t; await tick(); };
  const give = (x) => (x instanceof Error ? Promise.reject(x) : Promise.resolve(x)); e.asked = { first: 0, late: 0 }; e.readGate = () => { e.asked.first++; return give(first); }; if (late !== undefined) e.readGateLate = () => new Promise((res, rej) => { e.asked.late++; e.lateGive = () => (late instanceof Error ? rej(late) : res(late)); }); return e; };
const buy = async (e) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; await e.at(T0 + 610.3); swap(e, T0 + 612, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 613, 205_000); return en; };
const view = (en, checks) => booksApi([en], { stop: 0.25, run: RUN, checks }).books["FAST-1M"];

test("a reading that FAILS in time no longer cancels: the buy is made as in the baseline, labelled failed with the reason, and only the 'all buys' view shows it", async () => {
  const e = mk(BAD), en = await buy(e); assert.equal(en.status, "filled", "bought: " + en.flags.join("; ")); assert.deepEqual(en.families, ["FAST-1M"], "no book is taken off a buy for a label");
  assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "fail"); assert.equal(en.gate.timely, true); assert.deepEqual(en.gate.by["FAST-1M"], { pass: false, why: ["top 10 holders 50%: above 30%"] }); assert.equal(checkOf(en, "FAST-1M"), "fail");
  assert.equal(e.stats.gate.failed, 1); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.fails["top 10 holders"], 1);
  assert.equal(view(en, "all").length, 1); assert.equal(view(en, "all")[0].chk, "fail"); assert.deepEqual(view(en, "all")[0].chk_why, ["top 10 holders 50%: above 30%"]); assert.equal(view(en, "pass").length, 0); assert.equal(view(en, "late").length, 0);
});
test("a reading that PASSES in time: labelled passed, with the owner's two options, and it is in every view", async () => {
  const e = mk(GOOD), en = await buy(e); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "pass"); assert.equal(en.gate.timely, true); assert.equal(en.gate.late, undefined); assert.equal(en.gate.options.bundlers_main, true); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.passed, 1);
  for (const c of ["all", "pass", "late"]) assert.equal(view(en, c).length, 1, c); assert.equal(view(en, "pass")[0].chk, "pass"); assert.equal(booksApi([en], { stop: 0.25, run: RUN, checks: "pass" }).run.checks, "pass");
});
test("the first request is refused: the buy is made, ONE ordinary request follows, and its answer is a LATE label — shown in 'incl. late', never in 'passed in time'", async () => {
  const e = mk(new Error("GMGN rate limited"), GOOD), en = await buy(e); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(e.asked.late, 1, "one queued retry"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "no reading"); assert.equal(checkOf(en, "FAST-1M"), "none"); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.no_reading, 1);

  assert.equal(checkOf(en, "FAST-1M"), "pass-late"); assert.equal(view(en, "pass").length, 0, "a late reading can reflect what happened after the buy: not a usable filter result"); assert.equal(view(en, "late").length, 1);
  swap(e, T0 + 700, 205_000); assert.equal(e.asked.late, 1, "and it is never asked for again");
});
test("no reading at all (the retry fails too): the buy stays in the baseline, labelled 'no reading' — kept apart from a genuine failure; no attempt is given back, nothing is held", async () => {
  const e = mk(new Error("GMGN allowance exhausted for another 12 s"), new Error("GMGN rate limited")), en = await buy(e); e.lateGive(); await e.at(T0 + 640);
  assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "no reading"); assert.equal(en.gate.by, undefined); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.retry_errors, 1); assert.equal(en.attempt_returned, undefined); assert.equal(e.coins.get(M).gateHold ?? null, null);
  assert.equal(view(en, "all")[0].chk, "none"); assert.equal(view(en, "late").length, 0); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.failed, 0, "not a failure of the checks");
});
test("a reading that arrives between the signal and the buy is judged AT the buy; one that arrives a moment after it is late", async () => {
  const e = mk(GOOD); let give; e.readGate = () => new Promise((r) => { give = r; }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0];
  swap(e, T0 + 612, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 613, 205_000); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "no reading"); give(GOOD); await e.at(en.schedT + 1.4);
  assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "pass"); assert.equal(en.gate.late, true); assert.equal(checkOf(en, "FAST-1M"), "pass-late"); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.late_passed, 1);
});
test("a reading that arrived after the buy time but before the next trade of the coin is a late label too (no_reading still counts it as 'nothing at the buy')", async () => {
  const e = mk(GOOD); let give; e.readGate = () => new Promise((r) => { give = r; }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0];
  give(GOOD); await e.at(en.schedT + 0.4); assert.equal(en.status, "pending", "no trade of this coin has come since the signal"); swap(e, T0 + 613, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 614, 205_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(checkOf(en, "FAST-1M"), "pass-late"); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.no_reading, 1); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.late_passed, 1); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.passed, 0);
});
test("DIP15K is the one book whose RULE needs the reading: without a watched $100K peak the provider's all-time high must be there in time and be $100K or more", async () => {
  const mkD = (info) => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); e.rebuy = false; e.setBooks(["DIP15K"]); e.gates = S.GATES_11; e.gateMode = "label"; let now = T0; e.clock = () => now; e.at = async (t) => { now = t; await tick(); }; e.readGate = () => (info instanceof Error ? Promise.reject(info) : Promise.resolve(info)); return e; };
  const fall = async (e) => { swap(e, T0, 80_000); swap(e, T0 + 20, 40_000); swap(e, T0 + 40, 16_000); swap(e, T0 + 60, 14_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; await e.at(T0 + 60.3); swap(e, T0 + 62, 14_000); swap(e, T0 + 63, 14_000); return en; };
  const ok = await fall(mkD(BAD)); assert.equal(ok.status, "filled", "all-time high $400K: the rule holds — and the failed top-10 check is only a label"); assert.equal(ok.gate.verdict, "fail"); assert.equal(checkOf(ok, "DIP15K"), "fail");
  const low = mkD({ ...GOOD, ath_price: 0.00006 }), x = await fall(low); assert.equal(x.status, "cancelled"); assert.match(x.gate.rule, /DIP15K not established: the provider's all-time high is \$60K/); assert.equal(x.attempt_returned, undefined, "a coin that has not been to $100K is a decision about the coin"); assert.equal(low.stats.gate.rule_cancelled, 1);
  const none = mkD(new Error("GMGN rate limited")), y = await fall(none); assert.equal(y.status, "cancelled"); assert.match(y.gate.rule, /no provider reading had arrived/); assert.equal(y.attempt_returned, true, "no reading is not the coin's fault: the book may buy on a fresh crossing later");
});

test("second opinion — a pool state installed by a transaction of unknown delay prices NOTHING: the pending buy waits for a measured state", () => {
  const e = mkEngine({ stream: "manual" }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H });
  for (let t = T0; t < T0 + 610; t += 2) { if ((t - T0) % 20 === 0) swap(e, t, 100_000, { lag: 1 }); else e.noteLag(t, 1); }
  swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000, { lag: 1 }); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "pending"); assert.equal(en.schedT, T0 + 611);
  e.tx(T0 + 610.5, null, () => e.onSwap({ t: T0 + 610.5, mint: M, pool: "PoolA", base: 2e8, quote: 800, feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u" })); // unmeasured: the pool is now at $400K
  assert.equal(e.coins.get(M).state.unmeasured, true); e.noteLag(T0 + 611.2, 1); e.tick(T0 + 611.2);
  assert.equal(en.status, "pending", "the old code filled here on the $400K pool (quote 800 SOL) — a price no measured transaction had confirmed");
  swap(e, T0 + 612, 300_000, { lag: 1 }); assert.equal(e.coins.get(M).state.unmeasured, undefined); swap(e, T0 + 613, 300_000, { lag: 1 });
  assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(Math.round(en.fill.quote_sol), 600, "priced on the first MEASURED state after the buy time ($300K), inside the usual 5-second window");
  // …and if no measured state comes within 5 seconds, the ordinary pricing timeout cancels it
  const f = mkEngine({ stream: "manual" }); f.feedUp(T0); f.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); for (let t = T0; t < T0 + 610; t += 2) { if ((t - T0) % 20 === 0) swap(f, t, 100_000, { lag: 1 }); else f.noteLag(t, 1); }
  swap(f, T0 + 610, 205_000, { lag: 1 }); const fn = [...f.entries.values()][0]; f.tx(T0 + 610.5, null, () => f.onSwap({ t: T0 + 610.5, mint: M, pool: "PoolA", base: 2e8, quote: 800, feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u" }));
  for (let t = T0 + 612; t <= T0 + 620; t += 2) { f.noteLag(t, 1); f.tick(t); } assert.equal(fn.status, "cancelled"); assert.ok(fn.flags.some((x) => /timeout/.test(x)), fn.flags.join("; "));
});
test("second opinion — a figure must be finite; the all-time-high market cap needs a VERIFIED pump.fun coin, not just a supply under a billion", () => {
  assert.equal(num("1e999"), null); assert.equal(num("-1e999"), null); assert.equal(num(1e308 * 10), null); assert.equal(num("1e3"), 1000);
  assert.equal(athUsd(0.0004, 9e8, null, false).usd, null); assert.match(athUsd(0.0004, 9e8, null, false).basis, /not a verified pump\.fun coin/); assert.equal(Math.round(athUsd(0.0004, 9e8, null, true).usd), 360_000);
  assert.equal(parseInfo(GOOD).ath_usd, null, "unknown unless the caller has the evidence"); assert.equal(Math.round(parseInfo(GOOD, null, true).ath_usd), 400_000);
  const e = mkEngine(); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA" }); e.coin("Other", { mode: "polled" }); assert.equal(e._pump(M), true); assert.equal(e._pump("Other"), false); e.coins.get(M).info.population = "solana-wide"; assert.equal(e._pump(M), false);
});
test("history repair can be paused without a restart: nothing is asked of the provider and the skipped gap repairs are counted; a book's hold survives a planned restart", async () => {
  let off = true; const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); const b = startBackfill({ engine: e, store: null, jup: null, log: () => {}, paused: () => off }), before = bf.gapsSkippedPaused || 0, errs = bf.errors;
  await b.patchGap(T0, T0 + 60, ["A", "B", "C"]); assert.equal((bf.gapsSkippedPaused || 0) - before, 3); assert.equal(bf.errors, errs, "no request was attempted");
  const d = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-lab-")), a = mkEngine(); a.feedUp(T0); a.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); path(a, T0, T0 + 300, () => 100_000, 20); a.coins.get(M).gateHold = { "A2|VOL300K": true }; saveWarm(join(d, "w"), a, T0 + 300);
  const n = mkEngine(); assert.ok(loadWarm(join(d, "w"), n, T0 + 305).loaded); assert.deepEqual(n.coins.get(M).gateHold, { "A2|VOL300K": true });
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/polled.test.mjs

import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Poller } from "../src/polled.mjs";
import { classifyMintAccount, classifyJupToken, TOKEN_2022, SPL_TOKEN } from "../src/risk.mjs";
import { budget } from "../src/capacity.mjs";
import * as J from "../src/jupiter.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";

// a fake Jupiter: prices/quotes come from a table the test controls; time is a manual clock
function world() {
  const w = { t: T0, px: new Map(), omit: new Set(), route: true, reqs: { price: 0, quote: 0 }, quoteDelay: 0.3 };
  const tok = (mint, o = {}) => ({ mint, decimals: 6, token_program: SPL_TOKEN, circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: (w.px.get(mint) || 0) * 1e9, launchpad: o.launchpad ?? "raydium-launchlab", first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - (o.ageH ?? 9) * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true, is_sus: false, ...o });
  w.tok = tok;
  w.jup = {
    prices: async (mints) => { w.reqs.price++; const m = new Map(), om = []; for (const x of mints) { if (w.omit.has(x) || !w.px.has(x)) om.push(x); else m.set(x, { px: w.px.get(x), liqUsd: 60_000, decimals: 6 }); } return { t: w.t, req: w.t, prices: m, omitted: om }; },
    quote: async (mint, side, raw) => { w.reqs.quote++; const req = w.t; w.t += w.quoteDelay; if (!w.route) return { ok: false, t: w.t, why: "no route" }; const px = w.px.get(mint) / 100; /* SOL per token at $100/SOL */
      return side === "buy" ? { ok: true, req, t: w.t, in: raw, out: ((raw / 1e9) * 0.99 / px) * 1e6, route: ["Meteora DLMM"], router: "metis", feeBps: 0 } : { ok: true, req, t: w.t, in: raw, out: (raw / 1e6) * px * 0.99 * 1e9, route: ["Meteora DLMM"], router: "metis" }; },
    recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async (ms) => ms.map((m) => tok(m)),
  };
  w.eng = mkEngine(); w.poll = new Poller({ engine: w.eng, jup: w.jup, now: () => w.t });
  w.step = async (dt, mint, mc) => { w.t += dt; if (mint) w.px.set(mint, mc / 1e9); await w.poll.sweep(); w.eng.tick(w.t); await w.poll.quotes(); w.eng.tick(w.t); };
  return w;
}
const M = "MeteoraCoin1111111111111111111111111111111111";

test("Solana-wide adapter: a non-pump coin on another venue is discovered, sampled, and enters with a ROUTED QUOTE — never back-dated", async () => {
  const w = world(); w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); assert.equal(w.poll.add(w.tok(M, { launchpad: "meteora-dbc" }), "test inventory"), true);
  for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000);                 // 5 minutes of 10 s samples at $100K
  await w.step(10, M, 205_000);                                              // sampled double → FAST fires on this sample
  const e = [...w.eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.ok(e, "entry created"); assert.ok(e.families.includes("FAST-5M"));
  assert.equal(e.mode, "polled"); assert.equal(e.exec, "routed-quote"); assert.equal(e.pop, "solana-wide"); assert.equal(e.token.origin, "meteora-dbc"); assert.equal(e.evidence.trigger.coverage, "sampled"); assert.equal(e.evidence.trigger.cadence_s, 10);
  assert.equal(e.status, "pending", "no quote yet: the buy is scheduled 1 s after the signal");
  w.t += 1.1; await w.poll.quotes();
  assert.equal(e.status, "filled"); assert.ok(e.fill.quote.req_t >= e.schedT, "quote requested at/after the scheduled time");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(e.fill.t - e.fill.quote.resp_t) < 1e-9 && e.fill.delay_s > 0.3, "fill time = when the quote became available, delay recorded");
  assert.deepEqual(e.fill.quote.route, ["Meteora DLMM"]); assert.equal(e.fill.fee_src, "inside the routed quote");
  assert.ok(!e.families.includes("HH-BREAK"), "HH-BREAK needs volume: not claimed from price polling");
});

test("routed-quote exits: one quote per DISTINCT inventory (not per row); a sale waits for a quote requested after its trigger", async () => {
  const w = world(); w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w.poll.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000);
  await w.step(10, M, 205_000); w.t += 1.1; await w.poll.quotes(); const e = [...w.eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(e.status, "filled");
  const q0 = w.reqs.quote; w.t += 11; await w.poll.quotes();
  assert.ok(w.reqs.quote - q0 <= 2, `2 sizes (full, half) for ${e.versions.length} result rows — got ${w.reqs.quote - q0} requests`);
  w.px.set(M, 75_000 / 1e9); w.t += 11; await w.poll.quotes();               // −63%: every trail (20…60%) triggers on this valuation
  const trails = e.versions.filter((v) => v.policy === "trail"); assert.ok(trails.every((v) => v.status === "selling"));
  w.t += 0.8; await w.poll.quotes();                                          // the next quote is requested after trigger + 0.7 s → it prices the sale
  assert.ok(trails.every((v) => v.status === "closed")); assert.ok(trails[0].exit.quote.req_t >= trails[0].schedT - 1e-9); assert.equal(trails[0].exit.exec, "routed-quote");
  assert.ok(trails[0].exit.ret < -0.35);
});

test("no route: the buy is cancelled as a pricing/data timeout; an open position with no exit route stays visible, never removed", async () => {
  const w = world(); w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w.poll.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000);
  w.route = false; await w.step(10, M, 205_000); const e = [...w.eng.entries.values()][0]; w.t += 1.1; await w.poll.quotes(); w.t += 6; w.eng.tick(w.t);
  assert.equal(e.status, "cancelled"); assert.match(e.flags.join(), /timeout/); assert.match(e.flags.join(), /quote unavailable/);
  const w2 = world(); w2.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w2.poll.add(w2.tok(M), "t"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w2.step(10, M, 100_000);
  await w2.step(10, M, 205_000); w2.t += 1.1; await w2.poll.quotes(); const e2 = [...w2.eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(e2.status, "filled");
  w2.route = false; w2.px.set(M, 50_000 / 1e9); w2.t += 3600; await w2.poll.quotes(); w2.eng.tick(w2.t);
  assert.ok(e2.versions.every((v) => v.status === "open" || v.status === "selling"), "unpriced ≠ zero and ≠ closed"); assert.ok(w2.poll.stats.noRoute > 0);
});

test("a token the price API omits is missing coverage: counted, history restarts, and no signal is made across the hole", async () => {
  const w = world(); w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w.poll.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000);
  w.omit.add(M); for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) await w.step(10); assert.ok(w.poll.stats.omitted >= 3 && w.poll.stats.lost >= 1);
  w.omit.delete(M); await w.step(10, M, 210_000); await w.step(10, M, 215_000);
  assert.equal(w.eng.entries.size, 0);
});

test("a mint already observed live by the PumpSwap adapter is not polled twice; restricted tokens are not entered", async () => {
  const w = world(); w.eng.feedUp(T0); w.eng.coin("LiveCoinpump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); w.px.set("LiveCoinpump", 1e-4);
  assert.equal(w.poll.add(w.tok("LiveCoinpump"), "t"), false); assert.equal(w.poll.stats.skippedLive, 1);
  w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w.poll.add(w.tok(M), "t"); w.eng.coins.get(M).info.risk = { status: "restricted", blocking: ["transferHook"] };
  for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000); await w.step(10, M, 205_000);
  assert.equal(w.eng.entries.size, 0); assert.equal(w.eng.stats.riskBlocked, 1);
});

test("risk evidence: Token-2022 restrictions block the execution assumption; missing data is 'missing', not clean", () => {
  const mint = (ext, extra = {}) => ({ owner: TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1000000000000", mintAuthority: null, freezeAuthority: null, extensions: ext, ...extra } } } });
  assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(mint([{ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 600 }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 0 } } }])).status, "restricted"); // above 5%: still blocked (the higher of the fee in force and the scheduled one counts)
  const f3 = classifyMintAccount(mint([{ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 300 }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 100 } } }])); assert.equal(f3.status, "flagged"); assert.equal(f3.fee_bps, 300); assert.match(f3.warnings.join(), /transfer fee 300 bps/);
  assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(mint([{ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 500 }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 0 } } }])).status, "flagged", "exactly 5% is allowed");
  assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(mint([{ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 100 }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 0 } } }, { extension: "permanentDelegate", state: {} }])).status, "restricted", "a small fee does not unblock another restriction");
  assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(mint([{ extension: "transferHook", state: { programId: "Hook111" } }])).status, "restricted");
  assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(mint([{ extension: "metadataPointer", state: {} }, { extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 0 }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: 0 } } }])).status, "clean");
  assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(mint([], { freezeAuthority: "X" })).status, "flagged"); assert.equal(classifyMintAccount(null).status, "missing");
  assert.equal(classifyJupToken({ token_program: TOKEN_2022, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true }).status, "missing");
  assert.equal(classifyJupToken({ token_program: SPL_TOKEN, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true }).status, "clean"); assert.equal(classifyJupToken({ token_program: SPL_TOKEN }).status, "missing");
});

test("capacity is budgeted per job (scans, position quotes, entry/exit quotes, discovery) and picks the lowest tier with headroom", () => {
  const a = budget({ hot: 3000, hotEvery: 10, inventories: 50, quoteEvery: 2 }); assert.equal(a.jobs.price_scan_hot, 6); assert.equal(a.jobs.position_quotes, 25); assert.equal(a.tier, "Launch");
  const b = budget({ hot: 500, cold: 2500, inventories: 10 }); assert.equal(b.tier, "Developer"); assert.ok(b.credits_month < 25e6);
  assert.equal(budget({ hot: 100, hotEvery: 60 }).tier, "Keyless");
});

test("Jupiter client: omitted mints are reported, not priced as zero; quotes return route and timing; no route is explicit", async () => {
  const calls = []; J.setFetch(async (url) => { calls.push(url); const u = new URL(url);
    if (u.pathname === "/price/v3") return { status: 200, headers: [], json: async () => ({ A: { usdPrice: 0.5, liquidity: 1000, decimals: 6 } }) };
    if (u.pathname === "/swap/v2/order") return u.searchParams.get("outputMint") === "NOROUTE" ? { status: 200, headers: [], json: async () => ({ errorMessage: "No routes found" }) } : { status: 200, headers: [], json: async () => ({ inAmount: "200000000", outAmount: "123456789", router: "metis", feeBps: 5, routePlan: [{ swapInfo: { label: "Raydium CLMM" } }, { swapInfo: { label: "Orca Whirlpool" } }] }) };
    return { status: 404, headers: [], json: async () => ({}) }; });
  process.env.JUP_RPS = "1000";
  const p = await J.prices(["A", "B"]); assert.equal(p.prices.get("A").px, 0.5); assert.deepEqual(p.omitted, ["B"]);
  const q = await J.quote("TOKEN", "buy", 0.2e9); assert.equal(q.ok, true); assert.equal(q.out, 123456789); assert.deepEqual(q.route, ["Raydium CLMM", "Orca Whirlpool"]); assert.ok(q.t >= q.req);
  assert.ok(!calls.some((c) => /taker|execute/.test(c)), "quote-only: no taker, no execute endpoint");
  const n = await J.quote("NOROUTE", "buy", 0.2e9); assert.equal(n.ok, false); assert.match(n.why, /No routes/);
});

test("transfer-fee coins (owner, 22 Nov 2026): a 5% fee coin is bought on the sampled method and marked; the fee is charged once — inside the quote as measured (nothing more deducted), or, under the other assumption, off the tokens at the buy and off the proceeds at the sale; on the live method it stays blocked", async () => {
  const run = async (bps, feeInQuote = false) => { const w = world(); w.poll.feeInQuote = feeInQuote; w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w.poll.add(w.tok(M), "t"); if (bps) w.eng.coins.get(M).info.risk = { status: "flagged", blocking: [], warnings: [], fee_bps: bps };
    for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000); await w.step(10, M, 205_000); w.t += 1.1; await w.poll.quotes(); const e = [...w.eng.entries.values()][0];
    w.px.set(M, 75_000 / 1e9); w.t += 11; await w.poll.quotes(); w.t += 0.8; await w.poll.quotes(); return e; };
  const plain = await run(0), fee = await run(500), t = (e) => e.versions.find((v) => v.policy === "trail" && v.stop === 0.2);
  assert.equal(fee.status, "filled"); assert.equal(fee.token.xfer_bps, 500); assert.equal(fee.fill.quote.xfer_bps, 500); assert.equal(plain.token.xfer_bps, 0);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(fee.fill.tok / plain.fill.tok - 0.95) < 1e-9, "5% fewer tokens arrive at the buy");
  assert.equal(t(fee).status, "closed"); const gross = 1 + t(plain).exit.ret, net = 1 + t(fee).exit.ret;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(net / gross - 0.95 * 0.95) < 0.002, `the same trade is worth 0.95 × 0.95 of the fee-free one, less the fixed transaction cost (${(net / gross).toFixed(4)})`); assert.ok(net < gross * 0.9025, "never better than two full fees");
  // THE MEASURED CASE (spec XFER_FEE_IN_QUOTE = true): the quote is already net of the coin's fee, so the amounts are used as quoted
  assert.equal(S.XFER_FEE_IN_QUOTE, true, "22 Nov 2026 measurement: Jupiter quotes include the transfer fee"); assert.equal(world().poll.feeInQuote, true, "the poller runs the assumption in the spec");
  const inq = await run(500, true); assert.equal(inq.token.xfer_bps, 500, "still a fee coin: marked, in FEE-COINS"); assert.equal(inq.fill.quote.xfer_in_quote, true); assert.ok(Math.abs(inq.fill.tok / plain.fill.tok - 1) < 1e-9, "tokens exactly as quoted"); assert.ok(Math.abs(inq.fill.tok - inq.fill.quote.raw_tok) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(t(inq).exit.ret - t(plain).exit.ret) < 1e-9, "and the result is the quoted result — the fee is not taken a second time"); assert.equal(t(inq).exit.quote.xfer_in_quote, true);
  const { booksApi } = await import("../src/page.mjs"); const api = booksApi([fee, plain], { stop: 0.2, policy: "trail" }), row = api.books["FEE-COINS"][0];
  assert.equal(api.books["FEE-COINS"].length, 1, "ONE row per fee-coin buy, in the separate book"); assert.equal(row.fee, 500); assert.equal(row.via, fee.families.join(" + "), "the row says which rule(s) bought it"); assert.equal(api.order.at(-1), "FEE-COINS");
  for (const f of fee.families) assert.ok(api.books[f].every((r) => r.id !== fee.id), `the fee coin is not in ${f}`); assert.ok(api.books["FAST-5M"].some((r) => r.id === plain.id), "an ordinary coin stays in its rule book");
  const { bookRows } = await import("../src/report.mjs"); const rr = bookRows([fee]), rp = bookRows([plain]); assert.equal(rr.reduce((n, r) => n + r.entries, 0), 0, "and it is not in the full table rows"); assert.ok(rp.reduce((n, r) => n + r.entries, 0) > 0);
  const { swap, path } = await import("./helpers.mjs"); const L = mkEngine(); L.feedUp(T0); const c = L.coin("LiveFeeCoin", { pool: "PoolL", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); c.info.risk = { status: "flagged", blocking: [], fee_bps: 300 };
  for (let k = 0; k <= 30; k++) swap(L, T0 + k * 10, 100_000, { mint: "LiveFeeCoin", pool: "PoolL" }); swap(L, T0 + 320, 210_000, { mint: "LiveFeeCoin", pool: "PoolL" });
  assert.equal(L.entries.size, 0, "pool-state fills do not model the fee: not entered on the live method"); assert.equal(L.stats.riskBlocked, 1);
});

test("fee coins in their first 8 hours (owner, 22 Nov 2026): a 2-hour-old fee coin is bought by the ordinary sampled rules, in age group A0 and in FEE-COINS; a 2-hour-old coin without a fee is still not evaluated; a 2-hour-old coin with a fee over 5% is blocked", async () => {
  const run = async (risk) => { const w = world(); w.px.set(M, 100_000 / 1e9); w.poll.add(w.tok(M, { ageH: 2 }), "t"); if (risk) w.eng.coins.get(M).info.risk = risk;
    for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await w.step(10, M, 100_000); await w.step(10, M, 205_000); w.t += 1.1; await w.poll.quotes(); return w; };
  const fee = await run({ status: "flagged", blocking: [], warnings: [], fee_bps: 200 }), e = [...fee.eng.entries.values()][0];
  assert.ok(e, "bought"); assert.equal(e.ag, "A0"); assert.ok(e.families.includes("FAST-5M")); assert.ok(!e.families.includes("AGE-DOUBLE"), "AGE-DOUBLE has no age-group start under 8 hours"); assert.equal(e.status, "filled"); assert.equal(e.token.xfer_bps, 200); assert.ok(fee.eng.stats.evalsFeeYoung > 0);
  const { booksApi } = await import("../src/page.mjs"); const api = booksApi([e], { stop: 0.2, policy: "trail", ag: "A0" }); assert.equal(api.books["FEE-COINS"].length, 1, "shown under the page's 'under 8h' filter, in FEE-COINS"); assert.equal(api.books["FAST-5M"].length, 0);
  const plain = await run(null); assert.equal(plain.eng.entries.size, 0, "no fee: the 8-hour rule stands"); assert.equal(plain.eng.stats.evals, 0);
  const big = await run({ status: "restricted", blocking: ["transferFee 900 bps"], fee_bps: 900 }); assert.equal(big.eng.entries.size, 0, "9% fee: blocked at any age");
});



====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/rebuy.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// older-9 (22 Nov 2026, owner's decision): BUYING AGAIN. A book that has bought a coin may buy it again when its rule fires and
// it no longer holds the coin — judged separately on each stop and exit. No waiting time. First buys are exactly the older-8 buys.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { booksApi } from "../src/page.mjs"; import { bookRows } from "../src/report.mjs";
import { Poller } from "../src/polled.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", all = (e) => e._all, of = (e, f) => all(e).filter((x) => x.families.includes(f)), firsts = (e, f) => of(e, f).filter((x) => !x.again?.[f]), agains = (e, f) => of(e, f).filter((x) => x.again?.[f]);
const eng = (o = {}) => { const e = mkEngine(o); e._all = []; const add = e.addEntry.bind(e); e.addEntry = (x) => { e._all.push(x); add(x); }; e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - (o.age ?? 20 * H) }); return e; };
const rows = (x) => x.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === false).map((v) => (v.policy === "trail" ? "t" : "h") + Math.round(v.stop * 100)).join(" ");
// five hours watched with a $60K low, then a fresh double: the standard LOW4H buy (shared by other books that fire with it)
const base = (e) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 2 * H, () => 100_000, 60); path(e, T0 + 2 * H, T0 + 5 * H, () => 60_000, 60); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H, 110_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 2, 125_000); };

test("while a book holds the coin on every row it does not buy again; once a row has sold, the next signal is a re-buy carrying exactly the free rows", () => {
  const e = eng(); base(e); assert.equal(firsts(e, "LOW4H").length, 1); const f = firsts(e, "LOW4H")[0]; e.tick(T0 + 5 * H + 4);
  swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 10, 118_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 12, 126_000); assert.equal(agains(e, "LOW4H").length, 0, "nothing sold yet: a second crossing changes nothing");
  path(e, T0 + 5 * H + 20, T0 + 5 * H + 200, () => 80_000, 10);           // −36% from the high: the 20, 25 and 30% stops sell; 40% and 60% still hold
  assert.deepEqual(f.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === false && v.status === "closed").map((v) => v.stop).sort(), [0.2, 0.2, 0.25, 0.25, 0.3, 0.3]);
  swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 210, 110_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 212, 125_000);   // LOW4H's rule fires again: 2 × the $60K low, freshly crossed
  const r = agains(e, "LOW4H"); assert.equal(r.length, 1, "one re-buy"); assert.equal(rows(r[0]), "t20 t25 t30 h20 h25 h30", "on the rows that had sold — the 40% and 60% rows are still in the first buy");
  assert.equal(r[0].again.LOW4H, true); assert.ok(r[0].id !== f.id && r[0].decisionT > f.decisionT); assert.equal(firsts(e, "LOW4H").length, 1, "the first buy is untouched");
  e.tick(T0 + 5 * H + 214); assert.equal(r[0].status, "filled"); assert.ok(r[0].fill.t > f.fill.t, "a re-buy is a real new buy at its own time and price");
});

test("the page and the full table: a stop that sold shows the first buy and the re-buy; a stop still holding shows only the first; 'first only' is the older-8 figure", () => {
  const e = eng(); base(e); e.tick(T0 + 5 * H + 4); path(e, T0 + 5 * H + 20, T0 + 5 * H + 200, () => 80_000, 10); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 210, 110_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 212, 125_000); e.tick(T0 + 5 * H + 214);
  path(e, T0 + 5 * H + 220, T0 + 5 * H + 400, () => 85_000, 10);            // the re-buy's 20–30% stops sell too
  const at = (stop, first = false) => booksApi(all(e), { stop, policy: "trail", first }).books.LOW4H;
  assert.equal(at(0.2).length, 2); assert.deepEqual(at(0.2).map((t) => t.again), [false, true]); assert.equal(at(0.6).length, 1, "the 60% row never sold: one buy"); assert.equal(at(0.2, true).length, 1, "first only");
  const row = bookRows(all(e)).find((r) => r.family === "LOW4H" && r.ag === firsts(e, "LOW4H")[0].ag && r.stop === 0.2 && r.policy === "trail");
  assert.equal(row.closed, 1, "the comparison row counts the first buy only"); assert.equal(row.rebuys_closed, 1, "the re-buy is beside it"); assert.ok(Math.abs(row.net_sol + row.rebuys_net_sol - at(0.2).reduce((s, t) => s + t.net, 0)) < 2e-4, "and the page total = first buys + re-buys");
});

test("no waiting time: a volume book stopped out during heavy trading buys again at the next trade, and again — every one marked", () => {
  const e = eng({ age: 2 * H }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 300_000, 20, { sol: 300 });                  // $30K of volume per trade: the 5-minute volume is over $300K
  assert.equal(firsts(e, "VOL300K").length, 1); let t = T0 + 600, mc = 300_000;
  for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) { t += 5; mc *= 0.93; swap(e, t, mc, { sol: 300 }); e.tick(t + 2); }       // a slide of 7% per trade, still on huge volume
  const r = agains(e, "VOL300K"); assert.ok(r.length >= 3, `re-bought ${r.length} times on the way down`); assert.ok(r.every((x) => x.again.VOL300K)); assert.equal(firsts(e, "VOL300K").length, 1);
  const t20 = booksApi(all(e), { stop: 0.2, policy: "trail" }).books.VOL300K.filter((x) => x.status === "closed"); assert.ok(t20.length >= 3 && t20.every((x) => x.ret < 0), "each of them a loss here: what 'no wait' costs in a crash is visible, not hidden");
  assert.ok(r.every((x, i) => i === 0 || x.decisionT > r[i - 1].decisionT));
});

test("the safety limit: no more than REBUY_MAX_PER_DAY re-buys of one coin by one book in 24 hours, recorded when it bites; an older re-buy ages out", () => {
  const e = eng(); base(e); e.tick(T0 + 5 * H + 4); path(e, T0 + 5 * H + 20, T0 + 5 * H + 200, () => 80_000, 10); const c = e.coins.get(M);
  c.rebuys = { LOW4H: Array.from({ length: S.REBUY_MAX_PER_DAY }, (_, k) => T0 + 5 * H + k) };            // as if this book had already re-bought it 50 times today
  swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 210, 110_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 212, 125_000); assert.equal(agains(e, "LOW4H").length, 0); assert.equal(e.stats.rebuyCapped, 1);
  path(e, T0 + 5 * H + 220, T0 + 5 * H + 400, () => 80_000, 10); c.rebuys.LOW4H = c.rebuys.LOW4H.map(() => T0 - 20 * H); // …more than 24 hours ago
  swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 410, 110_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 412, 125_000); assert.equal(agains(e, "LOW4H").length, 1);
});

test("a clock book on its own: CONT-STALL sold at 60 minutes while CONT still holds — the next CONT signal is a CONT-STALL re-buy on its own rows, and no CONT buy", () => {
  const e = eng(); path(e, T0, T0 + 2 * H, () => 100_000, 60); swap(e, T0 + 2 * H, 131_000);              // +31% on 15 minutes ago, at the hour's high: CONT + CONT-STALL
  assert.equal(firsts(e, "CONT").length, 1); const f = firsts(e, "CONT")[0]; assert.ok(f.families.includes("CONT-STALL"));
  path(e, T0 + 2 * H + 10, T0 + 3 * H + 30, () => 128_000, 30);            // an hour sideways, never 1.15×: the stall rows are sold by the clock, the plain rows are all still open
  assert.ok(f.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === true).every((v) => v.status === "closed")); assert.ok(f.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === false).every((v) => v.status !== "closed"));
  path(e, T0 + 3 * H + 40, T0 + 3 * H + 1000, () => 128_000, 30); swap(e, T0 + 3 * H + 1010, 170_000);   // CONT's condition again
  const r = agains(e, "CONT-STALL"); assert.equal(r.length, 1); assert.deepEqual(r[0].families, ["CONT-STALL"]); assert.ok(r[0].versions.length === 10 && r[0].versions.every((v) => v.stall === true), "only the stall rows"); assert.equal(agains(e, "CONT").length, 0, "CONT holds every row: no CONT buy");
});

test("the sampled method buys again too (a non-pump.fun coin priced by quotes)", async () => {
  const w = { t: T0, px: new Map(), quoteDelay: 0.3 }, P = "MeteoraCoin1111111111111111111111111111111111";
  w.jup = { prices: async (ms) => { const m = new Map(); for (const x of ms) if (w.px.has(x)) m.set(x, { px: w.px.get(x), liqUsd: 6e4, decimals: 6 }); return { t: w.t, req: w.t, prices: m, omitted: [] }; },
    quote: async (mint, side, raw) => { const req = w.t; w.t += w.quoteDelay; const px = w.px.get(mint) / 100; return side === "buy" ? { ok: true, req, t: w.t, in: raw, out: ((raw / 1e9) * 0.99 / px) * 1e6, route: ["X"], router: "m", feeBps: 0 } : { ok: true, req, t: w.t, in: raw, out: (raw / 1e6) * px * 0.99 * 1e9, route: ["X"], router: "m" }; }, recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async () => [] };
  const e = mkEngine(); e._all = []; const add = e.addEntry.bind(e); e.addEntry = (x) => { e._all.push(x); add(x); }; const po = new Poller({ engine: e, jup: w.jup, now: () => w.t });
  const step = async (dt, mc) => { w.t += dt; w.px.set(P, mc / 1e9); await po.sweep(); e.tick(w.t); await po.quotes(); e.tick(w.t); };
  w.px.set(P, 1e-4); po.add({ mint: P, decimals: 6, token_program: "TokenkegQfeZyiNwAJbNbGKPFXCWuBvf9Ss623VQ5DA", circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: 1e5, launchpad: "raydium-launchlab", first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - 9 * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true, is_sus: false }, "t");
  for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await step(10, 100_000); await step(10, 205_000); await step(2, 205_000); assert.equal(firsts(e, "FAST-5M").length, 1); assert.equal(firsts(e, "FAST-5M")[0].status, "filled");
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) await step(10, 60_000);                       // −70%: every stop sells; five quiet minutes
  await step(10, 125_000); await step(2, 125_000);                           // doubles again inside 5 minutes
  const r = agains(e, "FAST-5M"); assert.equal(r.length, 1); assert.equal(r[0].exec, "routed-quote"); assert.equal(r[0].status, "filled"); assert.equal(rows(r[0]), "t20 t25 t30 t40 t60 h20 h25 h30 h40 h60", "everything had sold: the re-buy carries every row");
});

test("first buys are the older-8 buys: on random markets, the first buys with buying-again switched on are identical to those with it switched off", () => {
  let seed = 7; const rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff) / 0x7fffffff;
  for (let run = 0; run < 3; run++) {
    const trades = []; let t = T0, mc = 60_000 + rnd() * 200_000, drift = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 6000; k++) { t += 5 + rnd() * 40; if (rnd() < 0.01) drift = (rnd() - 0.45) * 0.08; if (rnd() < 0.003) mc *= 0.35 + rnd() * 0.3; mc = Math.max(8_000, Math.min(4_000_000, mc * (1 + drift + (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.06))); trades.push([t, mc, rnd() < 0.05 ? 200 + rnd() * 400 : 0.5 + rnd() * 5, rnd() < 0.55]); }
    const play = (rebuy) => { const e = eng({ age: 5 * H }); e.rebuy = rebuy; for (const [tt, m, sol, isBuy] of trades) { swap(e, tt, m, { sol, isBuy, user: "u" + (Math.floor(tt) % 37) }); e.tick(tt + 1.5); } return e; };
    const on = play(true), off = play(false), key = (e) => all(e).flatMap((x) => x.families.filter((f) => !x.again?.[f]).map((f) => `${f}|${x.ag}|${x.decisionT.toFixed(3)}`)).sort();
    assert.deepEqual(key(on), key(off), `run ${run}: same first buys`); assert.equal(all(off).filter((x) => x.again).length, 0, "switched off: no re-buy at all");
    if (run === 0) assert.ok(all(on).some((x) => x.again), "and the market was lively enough to produce re-buys");
    for (const x of all(on)) for (const f of x.families) { const tag = S.clockTag(f); assert.ok(x.versions.some((v) => v.stall === tag), `entry ${x.id}: ${f} has rows`); }
    // no book ever holds the same row twice at once (except a first buy in a NEW age group over a still-open earlier one — older-8 allowed that and still does)
    const byFam = new Map(); for (const x of all(on)) for (const f of x.families) (byFam.get(f) || byFam.set(f, []).get(f)).push(x);
    for (const [f, list] of byFam) for (const x of list) { if (!x.again?.[f] || !x.fill) continue; const tag = S.clockTag(f); for (const v of x.versions.filter((v) => v.stall === tag)) for (const y of list) { if (y === x || !y.fill || y.decisionT > x.decisionT) continue; const u = y.versions.find((z) => z.stall === tag && z.policy === v.policy && z.stop === v.stop); if (!u) continue; assert.ok(u.exit && u.exit.t <= x.decisionT + 1e-6, `${f} re-bought row ${v.policy}|${v.stop} at ${x.decisionT} while entry ${y.id} still held it`); } }
  }
});

import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { bookRows, overlap } from "../src/report.mjs";
import { newEntry, advance, observe } from "../src/book.mjs";
const st = (q, t) => ({ base: 2e8, quote: q, feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003, t }), yes = () => true;
test("1,340 rows (670 pure-trailing + 670 scale-out; VOL100K and DIP15K added in older-11); a shared entry feeds each of its families' rows; CONT-STALL reads only stall versions", () => {
  const a = newEntry({ mint: "M1", pool: "P", ag: "A1", t: 0, families: ["LOW4H", "SLOW-BUILD"], features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1 });
  const b = newEntry({ mint: "M2", pool: "P", ag: "A2", t: 0, families: ["CONT", "CONT-STALL"], features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1, startup: true });
  for (const e of [a, b]) { advance(e, 1.5, st(200, 0), yes); observe(e, st(100, 100), 100); advance(e, 101, st(100, 100), yes); }
  const rows = bookRows([a, b]); assert.equal(rows.length, 1340); assert.equal(rows.filter((r) => r.policy === "trail").length, 670);
  const r = (f, ag, s, p = "trail") => rows.find((x) => x.family === f && x.ag === ag && x.stop === s && x.policy === p);
  assert.equal(r("LOW4H", "A1", 0.2).closed, 1); assert.equal(r("SLOW-BUILD", "A1", 0.2).closed, 1); assert.equal(r("LOW4H", "A2", 0.2).closed, 0);
  assert.equal(r("CONT-STALL", "A2", 0.4).closed, 1); assert.equal(r("CONT", "A2", 0.4, "scale").closed, 1); assert.ok(r("LOW4H", "A1", 0.2).avg_ret < -0.4);
  assert.equal(r("LOW4H", "A1", 0.2).loss40, 1); assert.equal(r("CONT", "A2", 0.2).startup_entries, 1); assert.ok(r("CONT", "A2", 0.2).startup_net_sol < 0);
  const o = overlap([a, b]).find((x) => x.a === "LOW4H" && x.b === "SLOW-BUILD"); assert.equal(o.shared_entries, 1);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/restart.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";

test("restart: one-entry flags and open positions survive; the restart gap blocks fabricated crossings; exits flag the outage", () => {
  const db = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-")), "t.db");
  let st = new Store(db), e = mkEngine({ store: st });
  e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 4 * H }); st.saveCoin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 4 * H });
  path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000); path(e, T0 + 4 * H + 60, T0 + 5 * H, (t) => 50_000 * (1 + (t - T0 - 4 * H) / H * 1.5));
  e.tick(T0 + 5 * H); st.heartbeat(T0 + 5 * H);
  const before = [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H")); assert.equal(before.length, 1); assert.equal(before[0].status, "filled");
  st.db.close();

  // restart 20 minutes later
  st = new Store(db); const e2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(e2);
  const now = T0 + 5 * H + 1200; e2.restartGap(st.lastAlive(), now); e2.feedUp(now);
  assert.equal(e2.entries.size >= 1, true);
  path(e2, now, now + 3 * H, () => 30_000);                                   // price collapses → the open position's stops sell
  const re = [...e2.entries.values()].find((x) => x.id === before[0].id);
  assert.ok(re.versions.every((v) => v.status === "closed")); assert.ok(re.versions.every((v) => v.exit.outage_during));
  path(e2, now + 3 * H, now + 3.5 * H, (t) => 30_000 * (1 + (t - now - 3 * H) / 600)); // a fresh doubling inside A1 again
  assert.equal([...e2.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H") && x.ag === "A1").length + 0, 1 - 0); // only the restored one (no second A1 LOW4H)
  assert.equal(st.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM decisions WHERE family='LOW4H' AND ag='A1'").get().n, 1);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/review.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// Regression tests for the nine blockers of the independent code review of commit d88a250 (20 Nov 2026).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, quoteFor, H, T0, BASE } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { Poller } from "../src/polled.mjs";
import { Series, Coverage } from "../src/series.mjs";
import { newEntry, advance, observe, inventories, COST } from "../src/book.mjs";
import { decodeEvent, applyEvent, reduceTx, DISC, dec } from "../src/decoder.mjs";
import { SPL_TOKEN } from "../src/risk.mjs";

const fastSetup = (eng) => { eng.feedUp(T0); eng.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); path(eng, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10); };
const ev = (t, mc, extra = {}) => ({ t, mint: "MintAAApump", pool: "PoolA", base: BASE, quote: quoteFor(mc), feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u", ...extra });

test("B1 a state observed AFTER the scheduled time can never price it: no back-dated fill, the 5 s timeout applies", () => {
  const e = mkEngine(); fastSetup(e); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 210_000)); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "pending");
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 705, 400_000, { replay: true }));            // recovery/replay delivers a state from 100 s later without running orders
  e.tick(T0 + 706);
  assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled", "not filled from the future state"); assert.match(en.flags.join(), /pricing\/data timeout/); assert.equal(en.fill, null);
  // and in the ordinary case the fill's state is never younger than the fill
  const e2 = mkEngine(); fastSetup(e2); e2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 210_000)); e2.tick(T0 + 606.1); const f = [...e2.entries.values()][0].fill; assert.ok(f.state_age_s >= 0);
});

test("B2 events of one transaction give ONE committed state; equal receive times still order strictly, so FAST-1M is not missed", () => {
  // engine level: $100K → $150K → $205K all stamped with the same receive time
  const e = mkEngine(); fastSetup(e); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 150_000)); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 205_000));
  const fams = [...e.entries.values()].flatMap((x) => x.families); assert.ok(fams.includes("FAST150-1M") && fams.includes("FAST-1M"), fams.join());
  // decoder level: buy then sell inside one transaction → the engine would see only the final state, never the intermediate high
  const u = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigUInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, i = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, pk = (n) => Buffer.alloc(32, n);
  const mk = (buy, B, Q, b) => { const q = buy ? (Q * b) / (B - b) : (Q * b) / (B + b); return decodeEvent(Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(buy ? DISC.BuyEvent : DISC.SellEvent), i(1), u(b), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(B), u(Q), u(q), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(q), u(q), pk(9), pk(2)]).toString("base64")); };
  const B = 200_000_000_000_000n, Q = 200_000_000_000n, b = 20_000_000_000_000n, q1 = (Q * b) / (B - b);
  const p = { vq: 0 }, x = reduceTx(p, [mk(true, B, Q, b), mk(false, B - b, Q + q1, b)]);
  assert.equal(x.buys, 1); assert.equal(x.sells, 1); assert.ok(Math.abs(x.last.state.base - 200_000_000) < 1e-3 && Math.abs(x.last.state.quote - 200) < 1e-6, "committed state = back where it started; the +23% intermediate price is never exposed");
});

test("B3 coverage follows evidence: an empty back-fill extends nothing and repairs nothing; a real low in the boundary minute is kept", () => {
  const cov = new Coverage(); cov.up(1000); const sr = new Series("M", cov); sr.startLive(1000);
  assert.equal(sr.addBars([], { from: 0, to: 1000 }).accepted, false); assert.equal(sr.histFrom, 1000, "empty reply: history NOT extended");
  cov.drop(2000); cov.up(2300); assert.equal(sr.addBars([], { from: 2000, to: 2300, patchGap: true }).accepted, false); assert.equal(sr.covered(1900, 2400), false, "empty reply: outage NOT repaired");
  const r = sr.addBars([{ m: Math.floor(2000 / 60), o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 }, { m: Math.floor(2240 / 60), o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 }], { from: 2000, to: 2300, patchGap: true });
  assert.equal(r.accepted, true); assert.equal(sr.covered(1900, 2400), true, "repaired at bar resolution for hour-scale windows"); assert.equal(sr.covered(1900, 2400, true), false, "but never for a fresh crossing or a seconds-scale window");
  const far = sr.addBars([{ m: 10, o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 }], { from: 0, to: 1000 }); assert.equal(far.accepted, false, "a bar ending 5+ minutes before the known history does not connect to it"); assert.equal(sr.histFrom, 1000);
  const part = sr.addBars([{ m: 12, o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 }], { from: 0, to: 1000 }); assert.equal(part.from, 720); assert.equal(sr.histFrom, 720, "history reaches back only to the first bar actually returned");
  // engine level: FAST never fires across an outage that was 'repaired'
  const e = mkEngine(); fastSetup(e); e.feedDown(T0 + 601); e.feedUp(T0 + 620); e.coins.get("MintAAApump").series.addBars([{ m: Math.floor((T0 + 601) / 60), o: 1e-4, h: 1e-4, l: 1e-4, c: 1e-4 }], { from: T0 + 601, to: T0 + 620, patchGap: true });
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 621, 210_000)); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 630, 215_000)); assert.equal(e.entries.size, 0);
  // raw/bar boundary: the low sits in the minute where the exact buffer begins
  const c2 = new Coverage(); c2.up(0); const s2 = new Series("M", c2); s2.startLive(0);
  s2.add(3605, 0.5); s2.add(3650, 2); s2.raw = s2.raw.filter((x) => x[0] >= 3650);        // the 0.5 print was pruned from the exact buffer; its minute bar remains
  assert.equal(s2.lowIn(3000, 9000).px, 0.5);
});

test("B3 a sample the price provider omits breaks that token's coverage at once (first miss, not the third)", async () => {
  let t = T0; const px = new Map([["TokA", 1e-4]]), omit = new Set();
  const jup = { prices: async (m) => ({ t, req: t, prices: new Map(m.filter((x) => !omit.has(x)).map((x) => [x, { px: px.get(x), liqUsd: 5e4, decimals: 6 }])), omitted: m.filter((x) => omit.has(x)) }), quote: async () => ({ ok: false, why: "n/a", t }), recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async () => [] };
  const eng = mkEngine(), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup, now: () => t });
  po.add({ mint: "TokA", decimals: 6, token_program: SPL_TOKEN, circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: 1e5, first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - 9 * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true }, "t");
  for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) { t += 10; await po.sweep(); }
  omit.add("TokA"); t += 10; await po.sweep(); assert.equal(po.stats.lost, 1, "history restarts on the first omitted sample");
  omit.delete("TokA"); px.set("TokA", 2.1e-4); t += 10; await po.sweep(); t += 10; px.set("TokA", 2.2e-4); await po.sweep(); assert.equal(eng.entries.size, 0);
});

test("B4 open-position peaks are check-pointed: a restart resumes with the same high-water mark and the same stop", () => {
  const db = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-")), "t.db"); let st = new Store(db), e = mkEngine({ store: st });
  fastSetup(e); st.saveCoin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 210_000)); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 607, 212_000));
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 700, 380_000)); e.tick(T0 + 706);                               // peak, no status change — must still reach the database
  const mem = [...e.entries.values()][0], disk = st.allEntries().find((x) => x.id === mem.id);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(disk.versions[0].peak - mem.versions[0].peak) < 1e-12 && mem.versions[0].peak > COST * 1.5, "database holds the in-memory peak");
  st.heartbeat(T0 + 706); st.db.close(); st = new Store(db); const e2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(e2); e2.restartGap(st.lastAlive(), T0 + 720); e2.feedUp(T0 + 720);
  e2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 721, 290_000)); e2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 723, 289_000));              // −24% from the 380K peak: the 20% stop must fire on the restored position
  const r = [...e2.entries.values()].find((x) => x.id === mem.id), v20 = r.versions.find((v) => v.stop === 0.2 && v.policy === "trail");
  assert.equal(v20.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v20.reason, "trail");
});

function world() { const w = { t: T0, px: new Map(), route: true };
  w.tok = (m) => ({ mint: m, symbol: "TKN", decimals: 6, token_program: SPL_TOKEN, circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: (w.px.get(m) || 0) * 1e9, launchpad: "met-dbc", first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - 9 * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true });
  w.jup = { prices: async (ms) => ({ t: w.t, req: w.t, prices: new Map(ms.filter((x) => w.px.has(x)).map((x) => [x, { px: w.px.get(x), liqUsd: 6e4, decimals: 6 }])), omitted: ms.filter((x) => !w.px.has(x)) }),
    quote: async (m, side, raw) => { const req = w.t; w.t += 0.3; if (!w.route) return { ok: false, t: w.t, why: "no route" }; const p = w.px.get(m) / 100; return side === "buy" ? { ok: true, req, t: w.t, out: ((raw / 1e9) * 0.99 / p) * 1e6, route: ["Meteora"] } : { ok: true, req, t: w.t, out: (raw / 1e6) * p * 0.99 * 1e9, route: ["Meteora"] }; },
    recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async (ms) => ms.map(w.tok) };
  return w; }
async function sampledEntry(w, eng, po, M) { w.px.set(M, 1e-4); po.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) { w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); } w.px.set(M, 2.05e-4); w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); w.t += 1.1; await po.quotes(); return [...eng.entries.values()][0]; }

test("B5 restart: a sampled coin comes back as sampled, on the watch list, and its open position keeps receiving quotes", async () => {
  const db = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-")), "t.db"), M = "SampledCoin1"; const w = world(); let st = new Store(db), eng = mkEngine({ store: st }), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t });
  const en = await sampledEntry(w, eng, po, M); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.exec, "routed-quote"); eng.tick(w.t + 6); st.db.close();
  st = new Store(db); const eng2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(eng2); const po2 = new Poller({ engine: eng2, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t });
  assert.equal(eng2.coins.get(M).mode, "polled", "identity and mode restored before the one-entry flags"); assert.equal(po2.restore(), 1); assert.ok(po2.watch.has(M));
  assert.equal(po2.add(w.tok(M), "rediscovery"), true, "rediscovery does not reject it as 'already live'");
  w.t += 20; assert.ok(eng2.quoteNeeds(w.t).length === 1); const before = eng2.stats.quotes; await po2.quotes(); assert.ok(eng2.stats.quotes > before, "quotes flow again after the restart");
});

test("B6 an entry keeps its declared execution method: live events never price a routed-quote entry; a coin moves to live only with no open entry", async () => {
  const M = "SampledCoin2", w = world(), eng = mkEngine(), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, now: () => w.t }); eng.feedUp(T0);
  w.px.set(M, 1e-4); po.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) { w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); } w.px.set(M, 2.05e-4); w.t += 10; await po.sweep();
  const en = [...eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "pending"); assert.equal(en.exec, "routed-quote");
  eng.onSwap({ t: w.t + 1.2, mint: M, pool: "CanonPool", base: BASE, quote: quoteFor(205_000), feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u" }); eng.tick(w.t + 1.5);
  assert.equal(en.status, "pending", "the canonical pool's state did NOT fill the routed-quote entry"); assert.equal(eng.stats.liveIgnoredSampled, 1); assert.equal(eng.coins.get(M).mode, "polled");
  w.t += 1.6; await po.quotes(); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.fill.exec, "routed-quote"); assert.ok(en.fill.quote.route);
  const N = "SampledCoin3"; w.px.set(N, 1e-5); po.add(w.tok(N), "t"); assert.equal(eng.coins.get(N).mode, "polled");
  eng.onSwap({ t: w.t + 5, mint: N, pool: "CanonPool3", base: BASE, quote: quoteFor(10_000), feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u" });
  assert.equal(eng.coins.get(N).mode, "live"); assert.ok(!po.watch.has(N), "with no entry open, ownership moves cleanly to the live adapter and polling stops");
});

test("B7 see exits.test 'a triggered sell survives a data failure': unresolved is never terminal — sampled side: inventory still requested, slowly", async () => {
  const M = "SampledCoin4", w = world(), eng = mkEngine(), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, now: () => w.t }); const en = await sampledEntry(w, eng, po, M); assert.equal(en.status, "filled");
  w.px.set(M, 1e-4); w.t += 11; await po.quotes(); w.route = false; w.t += 86400 + 100; eng.tick(w.t); // stops triggered, then no route for a day
  assert.ok(en.versions.some((v) => v.unresolvedSince && v.status === "selling")); const need = eng.quoteNeeds(w.t)[0]; assert.ok(need && need.unresolved && need.sells.length > 0);
  w.route = true; w.t += 601; await po.quotes(); assert.ok(en.versions.filter((v) => v.policy === "trail").every((v) => v.status === "closed"), "a restored route closes the unresolved sale");
});

test("B8 a break in the reserve chain is reported as a discontinuity and resets trust: the stale state cannot fill a pending order", () => {
  const u = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigUInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, i = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, pk = (n) => Buffer.alloc(32, n);
  const buy = (B, Q, b) => { const q = (Q * b) / (B - b); return decodeEvent(Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(DISC.BuyEvent), i(1), u(b), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(B), u(Q), u(q), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(q), u(q), pk(9), pk(2)]).toString("base64")); };
  const p = { vq: 0 }; assert.equal(applyEvent(p, buy(200_000_000_000_000n, 200_000_000_000n, 10n ** 12n)).discontinuity, false);
  const r = applyEvent(p, buy(150_000_000_000_000n, 260_000_000_000n, 10n ** 12n)); assert.equal(r.discontinuity, true, "reserves jumped between two events: flagged, not silently accepted");
  assert.equal(reduceTx({ base: 1, quote: 1, vq: 0 }, [buy(150_000_000_000_000n, 260_000_000_000n, 10n ** 12n)]).lost, true);
  // engine: after the feed marks the coin lost, the old state is not trusted for an order scheduled before the break
  const e = mkEngine(); fastSetup(e); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 210_000)); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0];
  e.coinLost("MintAAApump", T0 + 605.5); e.tick(T0 + 606.2); assert.equal(en.status, "pending", "not filled from the pre-break state");
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 607, 230_000)); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.fill.quote_sol, quoteFor(230_000)); assert.ok(en.fill.delay_s > 0.9, "filled from the first state after the break, with its real delay");
});

test("B9 each quoted quantity keeps its own request/response time; an order only uses quotes requested after it was scheduled", () => {
  const e = newEntry({ mint: "M", pool: null, ag: "A1", t: 1000, families: ["LOW4H"], features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1 }); e.exec = "routed-quote";
  observe(e, { kind: "quote", reqT: 1001, t: 1001.3, buy: { sol: 0.2, tok: 1000, reqT: 1001, t: 1001.3 }, sells: [] }, 1001.3); assert.equal(e.status, "filled"); const tok = e.fill.tok;
  const Qs = (full, half, tf, th) => ({ kind: "quote", reqT: Math.max(tf, th) - 0.3, t: Math.max(tf, th), buy: null, sells: [{ tok, sol: full, reqT: tf - 0.3, t: tf }, { tok: tok / 2, sol: half, reqT: th - 0.3, t: th }] });
  observe(e, Qs(0.42, 0.21, 1100, 1100.4), 1100.4);                       // full value ≥ 2× → scale trigger at 1100.4, scheduled 1101.1
  const v = e.versions.find((x) => x.policy === "scale" && x.stop === 0.25); assert.equal(v.status, "scaling");
  observe(e, Qs(0.42, 0.21, 1101.0, 1101.5), 1101.5);                     // half-quote requested at 1101.2 (after 1101.1) but the full quote was requested at 1100.7 (before): not eligible yet
  assert.equal(v.status, "scaling", "an older quote for another quantity cannot be borrowed");
  observe(e, Qs(0.40, 0.20, 1102.0, 1101.5), 1102.0);                     // both quantities now quoted after the scheduled time
  assert.equal(v.status, "open"); assert.ok(v.half); assert.equal(v.half.t, 1102.0); assert.deepEqual([v.half.quotes.half.resp_t, v.half.quotes.held_before.resp_t, v.half.quotes.remaining.resp_t], [1101.5, 1102.0, 1101.5], "each quote's own times are recorded, not one merged timestamp"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.half.quote_spread_s - 0.5) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.half.sol - (0.20 - 0.0005)) < 1e-12);
});

test("memory: buyer wallet lists are released after 20 minutes, the count is kept, and the 15-minute features are unchanged", async () => {
  const { Series, Coverage } = await import("../src/series.mjs");
  const sr = new Series("M", new Coverage()); const t0 = 1_800_000_000;
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) sr.add(t0 + i * 60, 1 + i / 100, 10, true, "w" + (i % 7)), sr.add(t0 + i * 60 + 5, 1 + i / 100, 10, true, "x" + i);
  const now = t0 + 40 * 60, m = Math.floor(now / 60);
  const before = sr.buyersIn(m - 15, m);
  sr.prune(now);
  assert.equal(sr.buyersIn(m - 15, m), before);
  const old = sr.bars.get(Math.floor(t0 / 60)), recent = sr.bars.get(m - 5);
  assert.equal(old.buyers.size, 0); assert.equal(old.nbuyers, 2); assert.equal(old.nb, 2);
  assert.equal(recent.buyers.size, 2); assert.equal(recent.nbuyers, undefined);
});

test("measurements: 1/3/5-minute volume, buys, sells, different buyers and top-buyer share are recorded on the entry and never change the decision", async () => {
  const eng = mkEngine(); const t = T0 + 30 * 24 * H; eng.feedUp(t - 3 * H); eng.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: t - 20 * 24 * H });
  path(eng, t - 2 * H, t - 400, () => 60e3, 60, (x) => ({ user: "old" + (x % 3), sol: 0.5 }));
  swap(eng, t - 250, 60e3, { user: "a", sol: 1 }); swap(eng, t - 150, 62e3, { user: "b", sol: 1 }); swap(eng, t - 100, 61e3, { user: "s", sol: 1, isBuy: false });
  swap(eng, t - 40, 70e3, { user: "a", sol: 6 }); swap(eng, t - 20, 90e3, { user: "c", sol: 1 }); swap(eng, t, 125e3, { user: "d", sol: 1 });
  const e = [...eng.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("FAST-5M"));
  assert.ok(e, "a fast-doubling entry was created"); const f = e.features;
  assert.equal(f.w1.buys, 3); assert.equal(f.w1.buyers, 3); assert.equal(f.w1.sells, 0);
  assert.equal(f.w3.buys, 4); assert.equal(f.w3.sells, 1); assert.equal(f.w3.buyers, 4);
  assert.equal(f.w5.buys, 5); assert.equal(f.w5.buyers, 4); assert.equal(f.w5.top_buyer_share, 0.7); // wallet "a": 7 of 10 SOL bought
  assert.equal(f.w5.vol_usd, 1100); assert.ok(f.vol60_usd > f.w5.vol_usd); assert.ok(f.vol_surge5 > 1);
});
