test("measurements: the holder snapshot keeps the provider's numbers as returned, with request and reply times; a missing token is recorded as missing", async () => {
  const J = await import("../src/jupiter.mjs"); const urls = [];
  J.setFetch(async (url) => { urls.push(url); return { status: 200, headers: [], json: async () => [{ id: "MintX", holderCount: 1234, liquidity: "45000", mcap: 90000, organicScore: 12.5, audit: { topHoldersPercentage: 21.4 }, stats5m: { holderChange: 3.1, numTraders: 41, buyVolume: 9000.5, flag: true, note: "x" }, stats1h: { holderChange: 12 } }] }; });
  const r = await J.tokenStats("MintX");
  assert.equal(r.ok, true); assert.equal(r.holders, 1234); assert.equal(r.top_holders_pct, 21.4); assert.deepEqual(r.stats5m, { holderChange: 3.1, numTraders: 41, buyVolume: 9000.5 }); assert.ok(r.t >= r.req); assert.match(urls[0], /tokens\/v2\/search\?query=MintX$/);
  const m = await J.tokenStats("Other"); assert.equal(m.ok, false);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/review10.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// TENTH REVIEW (Codex on older-10 @ 3537ada) — five defects, each reproduced with the PRODUCTION Engine used directly: no
// helpers.mjs, no default fixtures. Every scenario is driven chronologically, one transaction at a time, in the order feed.mjs
// uses: engine.tx(receiveTime, delayBehindChain, () => install this transaction's pool states). Nothing is assumed current.
//   B1 a recovery priced orders on the pool state that the recovering transaction itself replaced
//   B2 the recovery path filled a pending buy without the eligibility gate
//   B3 a silence stopped pricing but left signal history continuous
//   B4 one good measurement ended a late spell
//   B5 a mint account that came back empty was never asked for again
// On 3537ada (driven there as its feed did: noteLag, then the events) the B1–B5 cases fail and the two "control" cases pass;
// the status function used by the diagnostics case did not exist there.
import test from "node:test"; import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { Engine } from "../src/engine.mjs"; import * as R from "../src/risk.mjs"; import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
import { liqValue } from "../src/fills.mjs"; import { bookRows } from "../src/report.mjs";

const T0 = 1_800_000_000, H = 3600, BASE = 2e8, q = (mc) => (mc / 1e11) * BASE, A = "MintAAApump", B = "MintBBBpump", LIM = S.FEED_MAX_LAG_S;
const mk = (info = { token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, mints = [A]) => { const e = new Engine({ solUsd: () => 100 }); e.feedUp(T0); for (const m of mints) Object.assign(e.coin(m, { pool: "Pool" + m[4], createTs: T0 - 9 * H }).info, info); return e; };
const ev = (t, mc, { mint = A, sol = 1 } = {}) => ({ t: T0 + t, mint, pool: "Pool" + mint[4], base: BASE, quote: q(mc), feeBps: 30, sol, isBuy: true, user: "u" });
/** one transaction in feed order; on 3537ada (no engine.tx) the same calls in the order its feed made them */
const tx = (e, t, lag, ...evs) => (e.tx ? e.tx(T0 + t, lag, () => { for (const x of evs) e.onSwap(x); }) : (e.noteLag(T0 + t, lag), evs.forEach((x) => e.onSwap(x))));
const ents = (e) => [...e.entries.values()], filled = (e) => ents(e).filter((x) => x.status === "filled");
/** current stream, a trade every 2 s at $100K for ten minutes, then the jump to $210K: a fresh FAST crossing, buy scheduled 1 s later */
const lead = (e, mints = [A]) => { for (let t = 0; t < 600; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(t, 100_000, { mint: m }))); tx(e, 600, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(600, 210_000, { mint: m, sol: 5 }))); };
const fill = (e, mints = [A]) => { tx(e, 601.2, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(601.2, 210_000, { mint: m }))); e.tick(T0 + 601.3); for (let t = 603; t < 610; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(t, 210_000, { mint: m }))); };
const row = (e, mint = A, stop = 0.2) => filled(e).find((x) => x.mint === mint).versions.find((v) => v.stop === stop && v.policy === "trail");
const mintAcc = (ext = []) => ({ owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1000000000000000", mintAuthority: null, freezeAuthority: null, extensions: ext } } } });
const feeExt = (bps) => ({ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: bps }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: bps } } });

test("B1 the transaction that ends a late spell is applied BEFORE anything is priced: the overdue sale gets its 80 SOL pool, not the 240 SOL one before it", () => {
  const e = mk(); lead(e); fill(e); tx(e, 610, 1, ev(610, 150_000)); const v = row(e); assert.equal(v.status, "selling", "the 20% trail has triggered; sale scheduled at +610.7");
  tx(e, 610.2, 60); for (let t = 612; t <= 630; t += 2) tx(e, t, 60, ev(t, 120_000)); assert.equal(v.status, "selling", "nothing is priced while the stream is late");
  for (const t of [630.001, 631.001, 632.001, 633.001, 634.001]) tx(e, t, 1); assert.equal(v.status, "selling", "five good measurements: 4 s of good evidence, not yet 5");
  tx(e, 635.001, 1, ev(635.001, 40_000)); // completes the recovery AND moves the pool
  assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.pool.q - q(40_000)) < 1e-9, "priced on the recovering transaction's own committed state (80 SOL), not the preceding 240 SOL");
  const want = liqValue({ base: BASE, quote: q(40_000), feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003 }, filled(e)[0].fill.tok);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.sol - want) < 1e-12, `proceeds ${v.exit.sol} must equal the quote on that state ${want}`); assert.ok(v.exit.sol < 0.04 && v.exit.delay_s > 24, "and carry the delay the sale really had");
});
test("B1 a recovering transaction that touches TWO pools: no position is revalued halfway through it", () => {
  const e = mk(undefined, [A, B]); lead(e, [A, B]); fill(e, [A, B]); tx(e, 610, 1, ev(610, 150_000), ev(610, 150_000, { mint: B })); const va = row(e, A), vb = row(e, B); assert.equal(va.status, "selling"); assert.equal(vb.status, "selling");
  tx(e, 610.2, 60); for (let t = 612; t <= 630; t += 2) tx(e, t, 60, ev(t, 120_000), ev(t, 120_000, { mint: B }));
  for (const t of [630.001, 631.001, 632.001, 633.001, 634.001]) tx(e, t, 1);
  tx(e, 635.001, 1, ev(635.001, 40_000), ev(635.001, 30_000, { mint: B })); // pool A first, pool B second, one transaction
  assert.ok(Math.abs(va.exit.pool.q - q(40_000)) < 1e-9); assert.ok(Math.abs(vb.exit.pool.q - q(30_000)) < 1e-9, "the pool installed LAST in the transaction is also priced on its new state");
});
test("B2 the eligibility gate also guards the recovery path: a pending buy on a coin that turned restricted is cancelled, not filled", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc()) }); lead(e); const p = ents(e); assert.ok(p.length > 0 && p.every((x) => x.status === "pending"), "decided at +600, fill scheduled at +601");
  tx(e, 600.1, 60); for (const t of [600.6, 601.6, 602.6, 603.6, 604.6]) tx(e, t, 1); assert.ok(p.every((x) => x.status === "pending"), "delayed by the late spell");
  e.coins.get(A).info.risk = R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc([feeExt(800)])); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 604.6), false);
  tx(e, 605.601, 1, ev(605.601, 100_000, { mint: B })); // another pool's transaction completes the recovery (good evidence since +600.6), inside the buy window that ends at +606
  assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 605.601), true); assert.equal(filled(e).length, 0, "not filled with a zero fee");
  assert.ok(p.every((x) => x.status === "cancelled" && x.flags.some((f) => /cancelled before the buy/.test(f) && /restricted/.test(f))));
  // positive control: same timing, coin still clean → the recovery fills it inside the buy window
  const c = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc()) }); lead(c); tx(c, 600.1, 60); for (const t of [600.6, 601.6, 602.6, 603.6, 604.6]) tx(c, t, 1); tx(c, 605.601, 1, ev(605.601, 100_000, { mint: B }));
  assert.ok(filled(c).length > 0 && filled(c)[0].fill.delay_s > 4, "an eligible buy is still filled by the first current state within the timeout");
});
test("B3 a globally silent stream is a gap in SIGNAL coverage too: no exact window and no fresh crossing spans it", () => {
  const e = mk(); for (let t = 0; t <= 600; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000));
  assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 610), false, "no evidence for 10 s: not current");
  tx(e, 622, 1, ev(622, 210_000, { sol: 5 })); tx(e, 623.2, 1, ev(623.2, 210_000)); e.tick(T0 + 623.3);
  assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0, "the doubling was only seen across 22 unobserved seconds: no FAST or FAST150 buy");
  const c = e.coins.get(A); assert.equal(c.series.continuous(T0 + 560, T0 + 622), false, "exact continuity is broken across the silence");
  for (let t = 624; t <= 630; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 210_000)); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 630), true);
  const g = e.cov.gapsIn(T0 + 590, T0 + 631); assert.equal(g.length, 1); assert.ok(Math.abs(g[0][0] - (T0 + 600)) < 1e-6 && g[0][1] >= T0 + 622 + LIM, "one gap, from the last evidence to the end of the good-evidence period");
  assert.equal(e.streamStatus().interruptions.silence, 1, "counted as a silence, with its provenance");
  // a quiet POOL is not a silent FEED: pool A stops trading, other transactions keep the delay measured → no gap, no interruption
  const k = mk(); for (let t = 0; t <= 600; t += 2) tx(k, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); for (let t = 602; t <= 660; t += 2) tx(k, t, 1); assert.equal(k.cov.gapsIn(T0 + 590, T0 + 660).length, 0); assert.equal(k.freshAt(T0 + 640), true); assert.equal(k.streamStatus().interruptions.silence, 0);
  // and after a clean window the same coin is bought normally
  for (let t = 632; t < 1300; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 210_000)); tx(e, 1300, 1, ev(1300, 445_000, { sol: 5 })); tx(e, 1301.2, 1, ev(1301.2, 445_000)); e.tick(T0 + 1301.3); assert.ok(filled(e).length > 0);
});
test("B4 a late spell ends only after FEED_MAX_LAG_S of continuous good evidence — one good measurement after a quiet stretch does not end it", () => {
  const e = mk(); for (let t = -20; t < 0; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 - 2), true);
  tx(e, 0, 60); tx(e, 30, 1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 30), false, "one measurement, 30 s after the last evidence, proves nothing"); assert.equal(e.streamStatus().state, "recovering");
  tx(e, 32, 1); tx(e, 34, 1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 34), false, "4 s of good evidence"); tx(e, 35, 1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 35), true, "5 s of good evidence, starting at the FIRST good measurement (+30)");
  // bad evidence restarts the period
  const b = mk(); for (let t = -20; t < 0; t += 2) tx(b, t, 1); tx(b, 0, 60); tx(b, 1, 1); tx(b, 3, 1); tx(b, 4, 60); tx(b, 5, 1); tx(b, 7, 1); tx(b, 9, 1); assert.equal(b.freshAt(T0 + 9), false, "only 4 s since the good period restarted at +5"); tx(b, 10, 1); assert.equal(b.freshAt(T0 + 10), true);
  // a silence longer than the limit inside the period restarts it
  const s = mk(); for (let t = -20; t < 0; t += 2) tx(s, t, 1); tx(s, 0, 60); tx(s, 1, 1); tx(s, 3, 1); tx(s, 9.5, 1); assert.equal(s.freshAt(T0 + 9.5), false, "6.5 s without evidence: the period starts again at +9.5"); tx(s, 12, 1); tx(s, 14.5, 1); assert.equal(s.freshAt(T0 + 14.5), true);
  // a disconnect inside a late spell does not shorten it: the new connection still owes the full period
  const d = mk(); for (let t = -20; t < 0; t += 2) tx(d, t, 1); tx(d, 0, 60); d.feedDown(T0 + 1, "close"); d.feedUp(T0 + 3); tx(d, 3.5, 1); assert.equal(d.freshAt(T0 + 3.5), false); tx(d, 6, 1); tx(d, 8.5, 1); assert.equal(d.freshAt(T0 + 8.5), true);
});
test("the written initial-connection policy is kept, explicitly: unknown until measured, current at the first good measurement of a connection", () => {
  const e = new Engine({ solUsd: () => 100 }); if (e.streamStatus) assert.equal(e.streamStatus().state, "unknown (delay not measured on this connection)"); e.feedUp(T0); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0), false);
  tx(e, 0.4, 1.1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 0.4), true); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 0.2), false, "never back-dated before the first evidence");
  for (let t = 2; t <= 20; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1); e.feedDown(T0 + 21, "close"); e.feedUp(T0 + 23); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 23.2), false, "unknown again"); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 20), true, "what was current before the disconnect stays so");
  tx(e, 23.5, 1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 23.5), true); assert.equal(e.cov.gapsIn(T0 + 20, T0 + 24).length, 1, "the disconnect is one coverage gap, closed by the first measurement");
  const f = new Engine({ solUsd: () => 100 }); f.feedUp(T0); tx(f, 0.4, 30); assert.equal(f.freshAt(T0 + 0.4), false, "a connection whose first measurement is late starts late"); tx(f, 1, 1); assert.equal(f.freshAt(T0 + 1), false); tx(f, 3.5, 1); tx(f, 6, 1); assert.equal(f.freshAt(T0 + 6), true);
});
test("B5 a mint account that comes back empty, unparsed or missing from a short answer is retried with a bounded back-off; the rest of the batch is not stranded", async () => {
  const e = mk({}, [A, B]); const C = "MintCCCpump"; e.coin(C, { pool: "PoolC", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); lead(e, [A, B, C]); const [a, b, c] = [A, B, C].map((m) => e.coins.get(m));
  assert.deepEqual(new Set(R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 601).map((x) => x.mint)), new Set([A, B, C]), "three live coins a decision is waiting on");
  const before = { ...R.rk }, order = R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 601).map((x) => x.mint);
  const answer = { [A]: null, [B]: { owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: ["AAAA", "base64"] }, [C]: mintAcc() };
  assert.equal(await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, p) => ({ value: p[0].map((x) => answer[x]) }), T0 + 601), 3);
  assert.equal(R.feeKnown(c.info), true, "the good account in the batch is classified"); assert.equal(c.info.risk.read_at, T0 + 601);
  assert.equal(R.feeKnown(a.info), false); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(b.info), false); assert.equal(a.riskRead, false, "not marked as read"); assert.equal(R.rk.null_account - before.null_account, 1); assert.equal(R.rk.unparsed - before.unparsed, 1);
  assert.equal(R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 620).length, 0, "not hammered"); tx(e, 700, 1, ev(700, 100_000), ev(700, 100_000, { mint: B })); // the next decision asks again
  assert.deepEqual(new Set(R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 701).map((x) => x.mint)), new Set([A, B]), "asked for again after the back-off (was: never, on 3537ada)");
  // short answer: the coins beyond its end are retried, the ones inside it are classified
  const n0 = R.rk.short_response; await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, p) => ({ value: [mintAcc()] }), T0 + 701); const got = [a, b].filter((x) => R.feeKnown(x.info)); assert.equal(got.length, 1); assert.equal(R.rk.short_response - n0, 1);
  const left = [a, b].find((x) => !R.feeKnown(x.info)); assert.equal(left.riskFails, 2); assert.ok(left.riskRetryAt - (T0 + 701) === 120, "back-off doubles: 60 s, then 120 s"); assert.equal(R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 800).length, 0);
  tx(e, 900, 1, ev(900, 100_000, { mint: left.mint })); await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, p) => ({ value: p[0].map(() => mintAcc()) }), T0 + 901); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(left.info), true); assert.equal(left.riskFails, 0);
  // a whole-batch (transport) failure is told apart and bounded too
  const t = mk({}); lead(t); const n1 = R.rk.transport_fail_rounds; for (let i = 0, now = T0 + 601; i < 12; i++) { await R.riskReadOnce(t, null, async () => { throw new Error("rpc down"); }, now); now = t.coins.get(A).riskRetryAt + 1; }
  assert.equal(R.rk.transport_fail_rounds - n1, 12); assert.ok(t.coins.get(A).riskRetryAt - (t.coins.get(A).riskAt) <= 3600, "back-off never exceeds an hour");
});
test("a crossing that was blocked because the fee was not known is LOST: it is not back-dated and not taken late once the classification arrives", async () => {
  const e = mk({}); lead(e); const c = e.coins.get(A); assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0, "fee unknown at the crossing (+600): no entry"); assert.ok(c.feeWantedFirst <= T0 + 4, "asked for at the coin's FIRST evaluation, long before any crossing");
  assert.equal(await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, x) => ({ value: x[0].map(() => mintAcc()) }), T0 + 600.4), 1); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(c.info), true, "classified 0.4 s later");
  for (let t = 601; t < 660; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 210_000)); e.tick(T0 + 660); assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0, "the price is still doubled, but that crossing happened before the classification: no buy");
  assert.ok(e.stats.feeUnknown >= 300 && e.stats.feeUnknownCoins === 1, "every blocked evaluation is counted (an upper bound on lost crossings: the rules are not run on an ineligible coin)"); assert.ok(R.rk.latency_n >= 1 && R.rk.latency_max_s >= 598);
  for (let t = 660; t < 1300; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 210_000)); tx(e, 1300, 1, ev(1300, 445_000, { sol: 5 })); tx(e, 1301.2, 1, ev(1301.2, 445_000)); e.tick(T0 + 1301.3);
  assert.ok(filled(e).length > 0 && filled(e).every((x) => x.fill.t > T0 + 1300), "a NEW crossing after the classification is bought normally"); assert.ok(filled(e)[0].token.risk_age_s >= 699);
});
test("control: on an uninterrupted current stream a stop is still priced at its scheduled time on the state in force then", () => {
  const e = mk(); lead(e); fill(e); tx(e, 610, 1, ev(610, 150_000)); const v = row(e); assert.equal(v.status, "selling");
  tx(e, 612, 1, ev(612, 60_000)); // the next trade, 1.3 s after the scheduled sale, is far lower — it must not price the sale
  assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.exit.delay_s, 0); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.pool.q - q(150_000)) < 1e-9, "scheduled-time pricing unchanged: the 150K state, in force at +610.7");
});
test("control: a quiet pool, driven chronologically — while other transactions keep the delay measured its unchanged state prices the 24-hour sale on time", () => {
  const e = mk(); lead(e); fill(e); const p = filled(e)[0], end = p.fill.t - T0 + S.MAX_HOLD_S; let n = 0;
  for (let t = 611; t <= end + 4; t += 4) { tx(e, t, 1.1); e.tick(T0 + t); n++; } // evidence and clock interleaved, in time order; pool A never trades again
  const rows = p.versions.filter((v) => v.reason === "max-hold"); assert.ok(n > 20_000 && rows.length > 0 && rows.every((v) => v.status === "closed" && v.exit.delay_s === 0 && v.exit.state_age_s > 80_000));
  if (e.streamStatus) { assert.equal(e.streamStatus().interruptions.silence, 0); assert.equal(e.streamStatus().interruptions.late, 0); }
});
test("a coin found fee-bearing after a live buy: the position stays, its fee-free result is reported apart and is in no primary total", async () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: { ...R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc()), read_at: T0 + 600 } }); lead(e); fill(e); const p = filled(e)[0], c = e.coins.get(A); c.riskRead = true; c.riskAt = T0 + 600;
  await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, x) => ({ value: x[0].map(() => mintAcc([feeExt(300)])) }), T0 + 600 + S.RISK_REFRESH_S + 5, S.RISK_REFRESH_S); assert.ok(p.riskAfter && p.flags.some((f) => /found after the buy/.test(f)));
  for (let t = 1206; t < 1216; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1); tx(e, 1216, 1, ev(1216, 100_000)); tx(e, 1218, 1, ev(1218, 100_000)); const closed = p.versions.filter((v) => v.status === "closed"); assert.ok(closed.length > 0, "its stops still work and its sales are still recorded");
  const r = bookRows([p]).find((x) => x.family === p.families[0] && x.ag === p.ag && x.stop === 0.2 && x.policy === "trail");
  assert.equal(r.closed, 0); assert.equal(r.net_sol, 0); assert.equal(r.unsupported_fee_exits, 1); assert.ok(r.unsupported_fee_net_sol < 0, "the loss is shown, apart — not deleted, not zeroed, not inside net_sol");
  assert.equal(p.fill.risk_age_s, 1, "the age of the classification the fill relied on is recorded"); assert.equal(p.token.risk_age_s, 0);
});
test("a failed refresh of a coin we HOLD: what was known stays, the position stays, and the retry never waits longer than the refresh interval", async () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: { ...R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc()), read_at: T0 + 600 } }); lead(e); fill(e); const p = filled(e)[0], c = e.coins.get(A); c.riskRead = true; c.riskAt = T0 + 600;
  let now = T0 + 600 + S.RISK_REFRESH_S + 1; for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { assert.equal(await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, x) => ({ value: x[0].map(() => null) }), now, S.RISK_REFRESH_S), 1, "it is asked for again");
    assert.ok(c.riskRetryAt - now <= S.RISK_REFRESH_S && c.riskRetryAt > now); now = c.riskRetryAt + 1; }
  assert.equal(R.feeKnown(c.info), true, "the earlier reading is kept"); assert.equal(c.info.risk.read_at, T0 + 600, "with its true age"); assert.equal(p.status, "filled"); assert.ok(!p.riskAfter);
  const st = R.riskStatus(e, now, S.RISK_REFRESH_S); assert.equal(st.queue, 1); assert.ok(st.null_account >= 9 && st.backoff_max_s >= 60 && Object.values(st.latency_hist).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) === st.latency_n);
});
test("diagnostics are bounded and sufficient: delay histogram, seconds in each state, interruption counts and lengths", () => {
  const e = mk(); for (let t = 0; t < 100; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1.2); tx(e, 100, 30); for (let t = 102; t < 120; t += 2) tx(e, t, 12); for (let t = 120; t <= 130; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1.4); tx(e, 150, 1); for (let t = 151; t <= 160; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1);
  const s = e.streamStatus(); assert.equal(s.state, "current"); assert.equal(s.interruptions.late, 1); assert.equal(s.interruptions.silence, 1); assert.ok(s.longest_s.late >= 25 && s.longest_s.silence >= 25);
  assert.equal(Object.values(s.lag_hist).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), s.measurements); assert.ok(s.lag_hist["<=30"] >= 10 && s.lag_hist["<=2"] > 50); assert.ok(s.seconds.current > 90 && s.seconds.late > 15 && s.seconds.recovering >= 4 && s.seconds.silence >= 15);
  for (let i = 0; i < 12_000; i++) { const t = 200 + i * 20; tx(e, t, 1); tx(e, t + 1, 1); tx(e, t + 2, 1); tx(e, t + 3, 1); tx(e, t + 4, 1); tx(e, t + 5, 1); } // 12,000 more silences
  assert.ok(e.stream.spans.length <= 4000 && e.streamStatus().recent.length <= 10 && e.stream.diag.recent.length <= 30, "interruption records are capped");
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/review11.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// ELEVENTH REVIEW (Codex on older-10 @ 161d545). Production Engine used directly, driven chronologically; no helpers.mjs.
//   C1 recovery valued positions without first running their time-based deadlines → a timed sale could stay pending
//   C2 a silence in progress was reported as "current"; seconds counters stopped at the last measurement
//   C3 re-buys of coins later found fee-bearing stayed inside the re-buy totals
//   C4 a malformed mint answer threw out of the reader and stranded the whole batch
//   C5 late / silence gaps (and, since older-10, the tail of a reconnect gap) never reached the gap repair
import test from "node:test"; import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { Engine } from "../src/engine.mjs"; import * as R from "../src/risk.mjs"; import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
import { bookRows } from "../src/report.mjs"; import * as BF from "../src/backfill.mjs";
const gapRequests = BF.gapRequests; // did not exist at 161d545 (the scheduler read engine.feedCov.gaps inline in main.mjs)

const T0 = 1_800_000_000, H = 3600, BASE = 2e8, q = (mc) => (mc / 1e11) * BASE, A = "MintAAApump", B = "MintBBBpump", LIM = S.FEED_MAX_LAG_S;
const mk = (info = { token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, mints = [A]) => { const e = new Engine({ solUsd: () => 100 }); e.feedUp(T0); for (const m of mints) Object.assign(e.coin(m, { pool: "Pool" + m[4], createTs: T0 - 9 * H }).info, info); return e; };
const ev = (t, mc, { mint = A, sol = 1 } = {}) => ({ t: T0 + t, mint, pool: "Pool" + mint[4], base: BASE, quote: q(mc), feeBps: 30, sol, isBuy: true, user: "u" });
const tx = (e, t, lag, ...evs) => (e.tx ? e.tx(T0 + t, lag, () => { for (const x of evs) e.onSwap(x); }) : (e.noteLag(T0 + t, lag), evs.forEach((x) => e.onSwap(x))));
const ents = (e) => [...e.entries.values()], filled = (e) => ents(e).filter((x) => x.status === "filled");
const lead = (e, mints = [A]) => { for (let t = 0; t < 600; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(t, 100_000, { mint: m }))); tx(e, 600, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(600, 210_000, { mint: m, sol: 5 }))); };
const fill = (e, mints = [A]) => { tx(e, 601.2, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(601.2, 210_000, { mint: m }))); e.tick(T0 + 601.3); for (let t = 603; t < 610; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ...mints.map((m) => ev(t, 210_000, { mint: m }))); };
const mintAcc = (ext = []) => ({ owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1000000000000000", mintAuthority: null, freezeAuthority: null, extensions: ext } } } });
const feeExt = (bps) => ({ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: bps }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: bps } } });
/** a filled position on a pool that never trades again, with the stream kept measured and the clock running until `until` seconds before its 24-hour deadline E */
const quietToDeadline = (until) => { const e = mk(); lead(e); fill(e); const p = filled(e)[0], E = p.fill.t - T0 + S.MAX_HOLD_S; for (let t = 611; t < E - until; t += 2) { tx(e, t, 1.1); e.tick(T0 + t); } return { e, p, E }; };

test("C1 (Codex's reproduction) a 24-hour deadline that passes inside a late spell while the clock is held up: sold at recovery, not left pending", () => {
  const { e, p, E } = quietToDeadline(4); const rows = () => p.versions.filter((v) => v.policy === "trail");
  tx(e, E - 2, 60);                     // the late spell starts 2 s before the deadline
  e.tick(T0 + E - 1.5);                 // the last clock tick before the deadline
  for (const t of [E - 1, E, E + 1, E + 2, E + 3]) tx(e, t, 1); // good measurements; NO clock ticks: a brief processing stall
  assert.ok(rows().every((v) => v.status === "open"), "nothing has run the deadline yet"); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + E + 3), false);
  tx(e, E + 4, 1);                      // recovery completes (5 s of good evidence since E−1)
  assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + E + 4), true);
  assert.ok(rows().every((v) => v.status === "closed" && v.reason === "max-hold"), "the deadline was run at recovery and the sale priced there (was: still open, then pending for good)");
  for (const v of rows()) { assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.t - (T0 + E + 4)) < 1e-6, "priced at the recovery"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.delay_s - 3.3) < 1e-6, "with the delay it really had: scheduled E+0.7, priced E+4"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.trigT - (T0 + E)) < 1e-6, "triggered at the deadline itself"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.pool.q - q(210_000)) < 1e-9, "on the unchanged pool state in force"); }
  e.tick(T0 + E + 4.1); for (let t = E + 5; t <= E + 30; t += 1) { tx(e, t, 1); e.tick(T0 + t + 0.05); }
  assert.ok(rows().every((v) => v.status === "closed" && Math.abs(v.exit.delay_s - 3.3) < 1e-6), "and nothing changes afterwards");
});
test("C1 control: the same deadline with the clock running through the late spell closes at the same moment with the same delay", () => {
  const { e, p, E } = quietToDeadline(4); const step = (t, lag) => { tx(e, t, lag); e.tick(T0 + t + 0.05); };
  step(E - 2, 60); for (const t of [E - 1, E, E + 1, E + 2, E + 3, E + 4]) step(t, 1);
  for (const v of p.versions.filter((x) => x.policy === "trail")) { assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.reason, "max-hold"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.t - (T0 + E + 4)) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(v.exit.delay_s - 3.3) < 1e-6); }
});
test("C1 a deadline that comes AFTER the recovery is untouched by it: triggered and priced on time by the clock, as always", () => {
  const { e, p, E } = quietToDeadline(12); const rows = () => p.versions.filter((v) => v.policy === "trail");
  tx(e, E - 9, 60); e.tick(T0 + E - 8.5); for (let t = E - 8; t <= E - 4; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1); tx(e, E - 3, 1); // good evidence E−8 … E−3: recovery at E−3, before the deadline
  assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + E - 3), true); assert.ok(rows().every((v) => v.status === "open"), "the deadline has not come yet");
  for (let t = E - 2; t <= E + 3; t += 1) { tx(e, t, 1); e.tick(T0 + t + 0.05); }
  for (const v of rows()) { assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.equal(v.exit.delay_s, 0, "ordinary scheduled-time pricing"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.t - (T0 + E + 0.7)) < 1e-6); }
});
test("C1 the eligibility gate still comes first at recovery: a pending buy on a coin that turned restricted is cancelled before any clock transition", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc()) }); lead(e); const p = ents(e);
  tx(e, 600.1, 60); for (const t of [600.6, 601.6, 602.6, 603.6, 604.6]) tx(e, t, 1); e.coins.get(A).info.risk = R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc([feeExt(800)])); tx(e, 605.601, 1, ev(605.601, 100_000, { mint: B }));
  assert.equal(filled(e).length, 0); assert.ok(p.every((x) => x.status === "cancelled" && x.flags.some((f) => /cancelled before the buy/.test(f))));
});
test("C2 a silence is reported while it is happening: state, signal coverage and the seconds counters are as of NOW", () => {
  const e = mk(); for (let t = 0; t <= 600; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000));
  let s = e.streamStatus(T0 + 630); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 630), false); assert.equal(s.state, "silence", "30 s without a transaction is not current — even before the clock has run (was: \"current\")");
  s = e.streamStatus(T0 + 603); assert.equal(s.state, "current"); assert.equal(s.since_last_measurement_s, 3); assert.ok(Math.abs(s.seconds.current - 603) < 1e-6, "the 3 s since the last measurement are counted"); s = e.streamStatus(T0 + 630);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(s.seconds.silence - 30) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(s.seconds.current - 600) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(s.seconds_total - 630) < 1e-6, "every wall-clock second is in exactly one state"); assert.equal(s.interrupted_since, T0 + 600);
  assert.equal(e.stream.diag.spells.silence, 0, "reading the status changes nothing in the engine");
  e.tick(T0 + 630); // the clock notices it: now it is an interruption with a signal-coverage gap in progress
  s = e.streamStatus(T0 + 630); assert.equal(s.state, "silence"); assert.equal(s.interruptions.silence, 1); assert.equal(e.cov.down, true); assert.deepEqual(e.cov.gapsIn(T0 + 590, T0 + 630).map((g) => g[0]), [T0 + 600], "the gap starts at the last evidence");
  e.tick(T0 + 640); assert.equal(e.streamStatus(T0 + 640).interruptions.silence, 1, "one silence, counted once however often the clock runs");
  tx(e, 650, 1, ev(650, 100_000)); assert.equal(e.streamStatus(T0 + 650).state, "recovering"); assert.equal(e.streamStatus(T0 + 650).interruptions.silence, 1, "and not counted again when the next transaction arrives");
  for (let t = 651; t <= 655; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); s = e.streamStatus(T0 + 655); assert.equal(s.state, "current"); assert.equal(e.cov.down, false);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(s.seconds.silence - 50) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(s.seconds.recovering - 5) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(s.seconds.current - 600) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(s.seconds_total - 655) < 1e-6);
  assert.equal(e.cov.gapsIn(T0 + 590, T0 + 660).length, 1); assert.ok(s.longest_s.silence >= 55 - 1e-6);
  const old = e.streamStatus(); assert.equal(old.as_of, T0 + 655, "without a time the figures stop at the last measurement, and say so");
});
test("C2 the unknown time before a connection's first measurement is counted, and a clock-noticed silence prices nothing", () => {
  const e = new Engine({ solUsd: () => 100 }); e.feedUp(T0); let s = e.streamStatus(T0 + 3); assert.equal(s.state, "unknown (delay not measured on this connection)"); assert.ok(Math.abs(s.seconds.unknown - 3) < 1e-6);
  const { e: g, p, E } = quietToDeadline(40); g.tick(T0 + E - 20); assert.equal(g.streamStatus(T0 + E - 20).state, "silence"); g.tick(T0 + E + 5);
  const v = p.versions.find((x) => x.policy === "trail"); assert.equal(v.status, "selling", "the deadline is triggered but nothing is priced during a silence"); assert.equal(v.reason, "max-hold");
  for (let t = E + 6; t <= E + 11; t += 1) tx(g, t, 1); assert.equal(v.status, "closed"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.exit.delay_s - 10.3) < 1e-6, "priced at the end of the good-evidence period, with its real delay");
});
test("C3 a RE-BUY of a coin later found fee-bearing is counted apart from the re-buy totals, exactly like a first buy; no row is lost", async () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: { ...R.classifyMintAccount(mintAcc()), read_at: T0 + 600 } }); lead(e); fill(e); const p = filled(e)[0], c = e.coins.get(A); c.riskRead = true; c.riskAt = T0 + 600;
  await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, x) => ({ value: x[0].map(() => mintAcc([feeExt(300)])) }), T0 + 600 + S.RISK_REFRESH_S + 5, S.RISK_REFRESH_S); assert.ok(p.riskAfter);
  for (let t = 1206; t < 1216; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1); tx(e, 1216, 1, ev(1216, 100_000)); tx(e, 1218, 1, ev(1218, 100_000)); const fam = p.families[0];
  const pick = (rows) => rows.find((x) => x.family === fam && x.ag === p.ag && x.stop === 0.2 && x.policy === "trail");
  const first = pick(bookRows([p])); assert.equal(first.unsupported_fee_exits, 1); assert.equal(first.closed, 0); assert.equal(first.rebuys_closed, 0);
  const again = pick(bookRows([{ ...p, again: Object.fromEntries(p.families.map((f) => [f, true])) }])); // the same ledger rows, as a re-buy
  assert.equal(again.rebuys_closed, 0, "not inside the ordinary re-buy count (was: 1)"); assert.equal(again.rebuys_net_sol, 0, "nor its P&L"); assert.equal(again.unsupported_fee_rebuys, 1); assert.ok(again.unsupported_fee_rebuys_net_sol < 0, "the modelled result is shown, apart"); assert.equal(first.unsupported_fee_rebuys, 0);
  assert.equal(again.unsupported_fee_exits, 0); assert.equal(again.closed, 0); assert.equal(again.entries, 0, "a re-buy is still not a first buy");
  const clean = pick(bookRows([{ ...p, riskAfter: undefined, again: Object.fromEntries(p.families.map((f) => [f, true])) }])); assert.equal(clean.rebuys_closed, 1); assert.equal(clean.unsupported_fee_rebuys, 0, "control: an ordinary re-buy is counted as before");
});
test("C4 a malformed mint answer fails that ONE coin (and fails closed); the rest of the batch is classified and the bad one is retried", async () => {
  const C = "MintCCCpump", e = mk({}, [A, B]); e.coin(C, { pool: "PoolC", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); lead(e, [A, B, C]); const order = R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 601).map((x) => x.mint), n0 = { ...R.rk };
  const bad = [{ owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1", extensions: "oops" } } } }, { owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1", extensions: [null] } } } },
    { owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: "6", supply: "1", extensions: [] } } } }, { owner: 7, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1", extensions: [] } } } }, { owner: R.TOKEN_2022, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "x", extensions: [] } } } }];
  for (const b of bad) { const k = R.classifyMintAccount(b); assert.equal(k.status, "missing", "never read as \"no extensions\""); assert.equal(k.malformed, true); }
  assert.equal(await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, p) => ({ value: p[0].map((x, i) => (i === 0 ? bad[0] : mintAcc())) }), T0 + 601), 3, "does not throw (was: TypeError, whole batch stranded)");
  const [x, y, z] = order.map((m) => e.coins.get(m)); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(x.info), false); assert.equal(x.riskRead, false); assert.equal(x.riskFailWhy, "malformed"); assert.equal(R.rk.malformed - n0.malformed, 1);
  assert.equal(R.feeKnown(y.info), true); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(z.info), true, "the two good accounts in the same batch are classified");
  assert.deepEqual(R.riskQueue(e, T0 + 601 + 61).map((c) => c.mint), [x.mint], "and the bad one is asked for again after its back-off");
  // an exception while saving one coin does not strand the others either
  const f = mk({}, [A, B]); lead(f, [A, B]); let calls = 0; const store = new Proxy({}, { get: (_, k) => (k === "saveCoin" ? () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error("disk"); } : () => undefined) });
  assert.equal(await R.riskReadOnce(f, store, async (m, p) => ({ value: p[0].map(() => mintAcc()) }), T0 + 601), 2); const cs = [A, B].map((m) => f.coins.get(m)); assert.equal(cs.filter((c) => c.riskRead).length, 1); assert.equal(cs.filter((c) => !c.riskRead && c.riskRetryAt > T0 + 601).length, 1, "the one whose save failed is retried");
});
test("C5 the gap repair is asked for SIGNAL-coverage gaps — reconnect, late spell and silence — merged per minute, with no duplicates", () => {
  assert.equal(typeof gapRequests, "function", "the scheduler's choice of gaps is a tested function");
  const e = mk(); for (let t = 0; t <= 600; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); let seen = e.cov.gaps.length; assert.equal(gapRequests(e.cov.gaps, seen).requests.length, 0);
  tx(e, 602, 60); for (let t = 604; t <= 609; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000));                        // a late spell: 602 → 609
  for (let t = 610; t <= 700; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); e.tick(T0 + 720); for (let t = 721; t <= 726; t += 1) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); // a silence: 700 → 726
  for (let t = 728; t <= 900; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); e.feedDown(T0 + 901, "close"); e.feedUp(T0 + 903); tx(e, 903.4, 1, ev(903.4, 100_000)); // a reconnect: 901 → 903.4
  assert.equal(e.feedCov.gaps.length, 1, "the pool-trust list only ever saw the reconnect (this is the list the scheduler used to watch)"); assert.equal(e.cov.gaps.length, 3);
  assert.ok(e.cov.gaps[2][1] > e.feedCov.gaps[0][1], "and the reconnect's signal gap ends later than its pool-trust gap: at the first delay measurement");
  const r = gapRequests(e.cov.gaps, seen); assert.equal(r.seen, 3); assert.equal(r.requests.length, 2, "the late spell (ends 609) and the silence (starts 700) touch the same minute boundary: one request");
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.requests[0][0] - (T0 + 602)) < 1e-3 && r.requests[0][1] === T0 + 726 && r.requests[1][0] === T0 + 901 && r.requests[1][1] === e.cov.gaps[2][1]);
  assert.equal(gapRequests(e.cov.gaps, r.seen).requests.length, 0, "nothing is asked for twice");
  // gaps whose minute ranges touch are ONE request covering all of them
  const m = gapRequests([[T0 + 5, T0 + 12], [T0 + 30, T0 + 41], [T0 + 70, T0 + 75], [T0 + 400, T0 + 410]], 0).requests; assert.deepEqual(m, [[T0 + 5, T0 + 75], [T0 + 400, T0 + 410]]);
  // the repair has to be recorded for the SIGNAL gap: one recorded for the shorter pool-trust interval does not cover it
  const sr = e.coins.get(A).series, M = (T0 + 900) / 60, bars = [M - 2, M - 1, M, M + 1, M + 2].map((k) => ({ m: k, o: 1e-4, h: 1e-4, l: 1e-4, c: 1e-4, v: 10 }));
  for (let t = 905; t <= 960; t += 2) tx(e, t, 1, ev(t, 100_000)); const win = [T0 + 880, T0 + 950];
  assert.equal(sr.covered(...win), false); sr.addBars(bars, { from: e.feedCov.gaps[0][0], to: e.feedCov.gaps[0][1], patchGap: true }); assert.equal(sr.covered(...win), false, "the old request (pool-trust interval) leaves the window unproven");
  const [a, b] = r.requests[1]; assert.equal(sr.addBars(bars, { from: a, to: b, patchGap: true }).accepted, true); assert.equal(sr.covered(...win), true, "the new one repairs it for hour-scale use");
  assert.equal(sr.continuous(...win), false, "and never for exact windows: minute bars cannot prove a fresh crossing");
  assert.equal(e.feedCov.gaps.length, 1, "pool-state trust is untouched by any of this");
});

// TWELFTH REVIEW (ChatGPT / Codex on older-10-review 2328675 and older-11-review bbe1115, 25 Nov 2026) — each finding
// reproduced on the production Engine, and the owner's decision of 25 Nov 11:30 UK (a buy that got no provider reading does
// not use up the book's one buy; it may buy on a fresh signal later).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { parseInfo as parseRaw, judge, num, athUsd } from "../src/gates.mjs";
const parseInfo = (info, supply = null, pump = true) => parseRaw(info, supply, pump); // the readings in these tests are of a verified pump.fun coin unless a test says otherwise
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
import { Poller } from "../src/polled.mjs"; import { SPL_TOKEN } from "../src/risk.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", fams = (e) => [...e.entries.values()].flatMap((x) => x.families).sort(), tick = () => new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
const GOOD = { ath_price: 0.0004, total_supply: "1000000000", total_fee: "20", link: { twitter_username: "x" }, stat: { top_10_holder_rate: "0.22", top_bundler_trader_percentage: "0.1", top_rat_trader_percentage: "0.05", top70_sniper_hold_rate: "0.01" } };
// a 20-hour-old coin: ten quiet minutes at $100K, then $205K — the FAST books fire at T0 + 610
const quiet = (e, o = {}) => path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20, o);
// manual stream: nothing is assumed current — a measurement every 2 s keeps it so, with a trade every 20 s
const drive = (e, t0, t1, mc) => { for (let t = t0; t < t1; t += 2) { if ((t - t0) % 20 === 0) swap(e, t, mc, { lag: 1 }); else e.noteLag(t, 1); } };
const coin = (e, age = 20) => { e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - age * H }); return e; };

test("1 — a trailing high recovered at the end of a late spell is check-pointed (it used to stay unsaved unless something else changed)", () => {
  const e = coin(mkEngine({ stream: "manual" })); const L = { lag: 1 }; drive(e, T0, T0 + 610, 100_000); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000, L); swap(e, T0 + 612, 205_000, L); swap(e, T0 + 613, 205_000, L);
  for (let t = T0 + 614; t < T0 + 620; t += 2) e.noteLag(t, 1); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); const v = en.versions.find((x) => x.policy === "trail" && x.stop === 0.6 && !x.stall), before = v.peak;
  swap(e, T0 + 620, 300_000, { lag: 12 }); assert.equal(v.peak, before, "the stream is 12 s behind: the state is installed but no peak moves");
  e.dirty.clear(); // everything known so far has been check-pointed
  for (let t = T0 + 621; t <= T0 + 627; t++) e.noteLag(t, 1); // six seconds of good measurements: the late spell ends and positions are valued on the state in force
  assert.ok(v.peak > before * 1.2, `the recovery raised the peak: ${before} → ${v.peak}`); assert.ok(e.dirty.has(en.id), "and the entry is marked for the next checkpoint");
});

test("2 — a transaction whose own delay is unknown creates no signal and no price history; the next measured transaction is judged on its own", () => {
  const e = coin(mkEngine({ stream: "manual" })); drive(e, T0, T0 + 599, 100_000); const obs = e.coins.get(M).series.lastT; assert.equal(obs, T0 + 580);
  const ev = (t, mc) => e.onSwap({ t, mint: M, pool: "PoolA", base: 2e8, quote: (mc / 1e11) * 2e8, feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u" });
  e.tx(T0 + 600, null, () => ev(T0 + 600, 205_000)); // the doubling arrives in a transaction with no block time, 2 s after a measured one
  assert.deepEqual(fams(e), [], "it does not borrow the freshness of the transaction before it"); assert.equal(e.stats.unmeasuredEvents, 1); assert.equal(e.coins.get(M).series.lastT, obs, "no price observation was recorded from it");
  assert.equal(Math.round(e.coins.get(M).state.quote), Math.round((205_000 / 1e11) * 2e8), "its pool state IS installed: the chain of reserves stays continuous");
  swap(e, T0 + 601, 205_000, { lag: 1 }); assert.ok(fams(e).includes("FAST-1M"), "a measured transaction at the same price is a signal on its own evidence"); assert.equal([...e.entries.values()][0].decisionT, T0 + 601);
});

test("3 — with the gates on, a coin observed by sampling is not entered at all: no ungated buy, no reader call (production Poller)", async () => {
  const SM = "MeteoraCoin1111111111111111111111111111111111";
  const run = async (gated) => { const w = { t: T0, px: new Map() }, tok = (mint) => ({ mint, decimals: 6, token_program: SPL_TOKEN, circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: (w.px.get(mint) || 0) * 1e9, launchpad: "meteora-dbc", first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - 9 * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true, is_sus: false });
    const jup = { prices: async (mints) => { const m = new Map(); for (const x of mints) if (w.px.has(x)) m.set(x, { px: w.px.get(x), liqUsd: 60_000, decimals: 6 }); return { t: w.t, req: w.t, prices: m, omitted: [] }; },
      quote: async (mint, side, raw) => { const req = w.t; w.t += 0.3; const px = w.px.get(mint) / 100; return side === "buy" ? { ok: true, req, t: w.t, in: raw, out: ((raw / 1e9) * 0.99 / px) * 1e6, route: ["Meteora DLMM"], router: "metis", feeBps: 0 } : { ok: true, req, t: w.t, in: raw, out: (raw / 1e6) * px * 0.99 * 1e9, route: ["Meteora DLMM"], router: "metis" }; },
      recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async (ms) => ms.map((m) => tok(m)) };
    const e = mkEngine(); let asked = 0; if (gated) { e.setBooks(S.booksOf("older-11")); e.gates = S.GATES_11; e.readGate = async () => { asked++; return GOOD; }; }
    const poll = new Poller({ engine: e, jup, now: () => w.t }), step = async (dt, mc) => { w.t += dt; w.px.set(SM, mc / 1e9); await poll.sweep(); e.tick(w.t); await poll.quotes(); e.tick(w.t); };
    w.px.set(SM, 100_000 / 1e9); poll.add(tok(SM), "test inventory"); for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) await step(10, 100_000); await step(10, 205_000); // the sampled double
    return { e, asked, all: [...e.entries.values()] }; };
  const plain = await run(false); assert.ok(plain.all.length, "control produced no entry: " + JSON.stringify(plain.e.stats).slice(0, 700)); assert.ok(plain.all.some((x) => x.families.includes("FAST-5M") && x.exec === "routed-quote"), "control — without the gates the sampled method buys");
  const g = await run(true); assert.equal(g.all.length, 0, "with the gates on it does not: no entry, so nothing can fill ungated"); assert.ok(g.e.stats.sampledNotGated >= 1); assert.equal(g.asked, 0);
});

test("4 — a pending gated buy restored after a restart is cancelled cleanly (no crash) and can never fill without a passing verdict", async () => {
  const f = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older12-")), "r.db"), st = new Store(f), a = coin(mkEngine({ store: st })); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H, info: {} });
  a.gates = S.GATES_11; a.clock = () => T0 + 610.1; a.readGate = () => new Promise(() => {}); // the answer never comes before the process stops
  quiet(a); swap(a, T0 + 610, 205_000); const id = [...a.entries.values()][0].id; assert.equal(a.entries.get(id).status, "pending"); a.flush(); st.db.close();
  const st2 = new Store(f), b = mkEngine({ store: st2 }); st2.restore(b); b.gates = S.GATES_11; b.readGate = async () => GOOD; b.feedUp(T0 + 611); const en = b.entries.get(id); assert.ok(en && en.gate && !en.gate.verdict, "restored with its request on record and no answer");
  assert.equal(b.stats.gate, undefined, "this process has asked for no reading itself");
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => swap(b, T0 + 612, 205_000)); swap(b, T0 + 613, 205_000); swap(b, T0 + 614, 205_000);
  assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "no reading"); assert.equal(b.stats.gate.no_reading, 1); assert.ok(en.flags.some((x) => /no provider reading had arrived/.test(x)));
  // fail closed: a verdict other than "pass" already on a pending entry (an interrupted call, a restart) can only cancel it
  const c = coin(mkEngine()); c.gates = S.GATES_11; c.readGate = () => new Promise(() => {}); quiet(c); swap(c, T0 + 610, 205_000); const p = [...c.entries.values()][0]; p.gate.verdict = "no reading";
  swap(c, T0 + 612, 205_000); swap(c, T0 + 613, 205_000); assert.equal(p.status, "cancelled", "the old code returned 'permission' for any verdict that was already set");
  st2.db.close();
});

test("5 — the all-time-high market cap needs supply evidence: price × a billion is not assumed; a minted or unknown supply is 'unknown', which fails the $100K gate", () => {
  assert.equal(Math.round(athUsd(0.00011, 1e6, null, true).usd), 110, "a one-million-token coin at $0.00011 was worth $110, not $110,000");
  assert.equal(judge(parseInfo({ ...GOOD, ath_price: 0.00011, total_supply: "1000000" }), S.GATES_11, "FAST-1M").pass, false);
  assert.equal(Math.round(parseInfo(GOOD, 950_000_000).ath_usd), 380_000, "the chain's supply (950M after burns) is used before the provider's figure: a lower bound"); assert.match(parseInfo(GOOD, 950_000_000).ath_basis, /chain/);
  const none = parseInfo({ ...GOOD, total_supply: undefined }); assert.equal(none.ath_usd, null); assert.deepEqual(judge(none, S.GATES_11, "FAST-1M").missing, ["all-time high"]);
  const minted = parseInfo({ ...GOOD, total_supply: "5000000000" }); assert.equal(minted.ath_usd, null); assert.match(minted.ath_basis, /minted/);
  assert.equal(judge(none, S.GATES_11, "RUG450").missing.includes("all-time high"), false, "the rug books do not ask for it");
});

test("a reading that arrives after the time of the buy is labelled late even when it arrives before the verdict; a malformed figure is unknown, never zero", async () => {
  const e = coin(mkEngine()); e.gates = S.GATES_11; let now = T0; e.clock = () => now; let give; e.readGate = () => new Promise((r) => { give = r; });
  quiet(e); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; now = en.schedT + 0.3; give(GOOD); await tick(); // 0.3 s after the buy time, before any other event
  swap(e, T0 + 613, 205_000); assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "no reading"); assert.equal(en.gate.late, true); assert.equal(e.stats.gate.late, 1); assert.ok(en.gate.reading, "what it said is kept");
  for (const bad of [[], [5], {}, true, false, "abc", "0x10", " ", NaN, Infinity]) assert.equal(num(bad), null, JSON.stringify(bad) + " is not a figure"); assert.equal(num("0.05"), 0.05); assert.equal(num(0), 0); assert.equal(num("1e-3"), 0.001);
  const r = parseInfo({ ...GOOD, stat: { ...GOOD.stat, top70_sniper_hold_rate: [] } }); assert.equal(r.snipers, null); assert.deepEqual(judge(r, S.GATES_11, "FAST-1M").missing, ["snipers"], "an empty list used to read as 0% snipers and pass");
});

test("owner 25 Nov: a buy that got NO READING does not use up the book's one buy — the book buys on a fresh signal later; a coin that FAILED the gates stays used up", async () => {
  const mk = () => { const e = coin(mkEngine()); e.rebuy = false; e.setBooks(["FAST-1M"]); e.gates = S.GATES_11; let now = T0; e.clock = () => now; e.next = null; e.readGate = async () => { if (e.next instanceof Error) throw e.next; return e.next; }; e.at = async (t) => { now = t; await tick(); }; return e; };
  const cycle = async (e, t, answer) => { e.next = answer; swap(e, t, 205_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()].at(-1); await e.at(t + 0.2); swap(e, t + 2, 205_000); swap(e, t + 3, 205_000); return en; };
  // (a) refused by the provider → cancelled, attempt given back
  const e = mk(); quiet(e); const first = await cycle(e, T0 + 610, new Error("GMGN rate limited")); assert.equal(first.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(first.gate.verdict, "no reading"); assert.equal(first.attempt_returned, true);
  assert.equal(e.stats.gate.attempts_returned, 1); assert.ok(first.flags.some((x) => /may buy on a fresh signal later/.test(x))); assert.ok(!e.coins.get(M).groups.A2.done["FAST-1M"], "the book's mark for this coin is taken back");
  // the price falls back and doubles again an hour later: a FRESH signal → bought, as a first buy
  path(e, T0 + 700, T0 + 4300, () => 100_000, 20); const second = await cycle(e, T0 + 4310, GOOD); assert.notEqual(second.id, first.id); assert.equal(second.status, "filled"); assert.equal(second.again, undefined); assert.ok(second.families.includes("FAST-1M"));
  // (b) the coin FAILED the gates → that book's one buy is used: the same fresh signal later buys nothing
  const f = mk(); quiet(f); const bad = await cycle(f, T0 + 610, { ...GOOD, stat: { ...GOOD.stat, top_10_holder_rate: "0.5" } }); assert.equal(bad.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(bad.gate.verdict, "fail"); assert.equal(bad.attempt_returned, undefined);
  path(f, T0 + 700, T0 + 4300, () => 100_000, 20); const n = f.stats.entries; await cycle(f, T0 + 4310, GOOD); assert.equal(f.stats.entries, n, "no second attempt after a genuine fail");
});

test("fresh means fresh: a rule that stays true from trade to trade (5-minute volume) does not re-ask on every trade after a refusal — only once it has stopped firing and fires again", async () => {
  const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); e.rebuy = false; e.setBooks(["VOL300K"]); e.gates = S.GATES_11; let now = T0, asked = 0, ans = new Error("GMGN rate limited"); e.clock = () => now; e.readGate = async () => { asked++; if (ans instanceof Error) throw ans; return ans; };
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 10 }); for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) swap(e, T0 + 600 + k * 30, 100_000, { sol: 800 }); // $320K in 90 s → VOL300K fires
  assert.equal(asked, 1); now = T0 + 690.2; await tick(); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0];
  for (let k = 0; k < 20; k++) swap(e, T0 + 700 + k * 5, 100_000, { sol: 50 }); // the 5-minute volume stays above $300K on every one of these trades
  assert.equal(en.status, "cancelled"); assert.equal(en.attempt_returned, true); assert.equal(asked, 1, "no new request while the same volume burst is still in the window"); assert.ok(e.stats.gate.held >= 1);
  path(e, T0 + 1200, T0 + 1800, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 1 }); ans = GOOD; // the burst has left the 5-minute window: the rule stops firing → the hold is released
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) swap(e, T0 + 1800 + k * 30, 100_000, { sol: 800 }); assert.equal(asked, 2, "a new burst is a new signal"); now = T0 + 1890.2; await tick(); swap(e, T0 + 1895, 100_000, { sol: 1 }); swap(e, T0 + 1897, 100_000, { sol: 1 });
  const again = [...e.entries.values()].at(-1); assert.equal(again.status, "filled"); assert.deepEqual(again.families, ["VOL300K"]);
});

test("the returned attempt survives a restart: the cancelled entry's one-buy marks are not put back from the database", async () => {
  const f = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older12-")), "g.db"), st = new Store(f), a = coin(mkEngine({ store: st })); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H, info: {} }); a.gates = S.GATES_11; a.clock = () => T0 + 610.2; a.readGate = async () => { throw new Error("GMGN rate limited"); };
  quiet(a); swap(a, T0 + 610, 205_000); await tick(); swap(a, T0 + 612, 205_000); swap(a, T0 + 613, 205_000); a.flush();
  assert.equal(st.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM decisions WHERE family LIKE 'FAST-%'").get().n, 0, "the marks left the database with the cancellation"); assert.equal(st.allEntries()[0].attempt_returned, true); st.db.close();
  const st2 = new Store(f), b = mkEngine({ store: st2 }); st2.restore(b); assert.ok(!b.coins.get(M).groups?.A2?.done?.["FAST-1M"], "and the restart's reconciliation does not write them back"); st2.db.close();
});

// Regression tests for the SECOND independent review (ChatGPT, 20 Nov 2026, on commit 5963512) and the Grok review of the same
// commit: C1–C5 = that review's remaining blockers; M1–M3 = its points on the background measurements; G1 = save on stop.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, quoteFor, H, T0, BASE } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { Poller, validDec } from "../src/polled.mjs";
import { Series, Coverage } from "../src/series.mjs";
import { newEntry, observe, COST } from "../src/book.mjs";
import { decodeEvent, applyEvent, reduceTx, DISC, dec } from "../src/decoder.mjs";
import { features } from "../src/signals.mjs";
import { SPL_TOKEN } from "../src/risk.mjs";

const ev = (t, mc, mint = "MintAAApump", pool = "PoolA", extra = {}) => ({ t, mint, pool, base: BASE, quote: quoteFor(mc), feeBps: 30, sol: 1, isBuy: true, user: "u", ...extra });
const tmpDb = () => join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-")), "t.db");
function world() { const w = { t: T0, px: new Map(), route: true, meta: true };
  w.tok = (m) => ({ mint: m, symbol: "TKN", decimals: 6, token_program: SPL_TOKEN, circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: (w.px.get(m) || 0) * 1e9, launchpad: "met-dbc", first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - 9 * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true });
  w.jup = { prices: async (ms) => ({ t: w.t, req: w.t, prices: new Map(ms.filter((x) => w.px.has(x)).map((x) => [x, { px: w.px.get(x), liqUsd: 6e4, decimals: 6 }])), omitted: ms.filter((x) => !w.px.has(x)) }),
    quote: async (m, side, raw) => { const req = w.t; w.t += 0.3; if (!w.route || (w.failSize && Math.abs(raw - w.failSize) < 1)) return { ok: false, t: w.t, why: "no route" }; const p = w.px.get(m) / 100; return side === "buy" ? { ok: true, req, t: w.t, out: ((raw / 1e9) * 0.99 / p) * 1e6, route: ["Meteora"] } : { ok: true, req, t: w.t, out: (raw / 1e6) * p * 0.99 * 1e9, route: ["Meteora"] }; },
    recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async (ms) => (w.meta ? ms.map(w.tok) : []) };
  return w; }

test("C1 a coin promoted from sampled to live keeps its live position after a restart (the ownership change is saved)", async () => {
  const db = tmpDb(), M = "PromotedCoin1", w = world(); let st = new Store(db), eng = mkEngine({ store: st }), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t });
  eng.feedUp(T0); w.px.set(M, 1e-4); po.add(w.tok(M), "t"); assert.equal(eng.coins.get(M).mode, "polled");
  // its canonical pool starts trading: no entry is open, so the coin moves to live observation
  for (let t = T0 + 10; t < T0 + 610; t += 10) eng.onSwap(ev(t, 100_000, M, "CanonPool")); assert.equal(eng.coins.get(M).mode, "live");
  eng.onSwap(ev(T0 + 615, 210_000, M, "CanonPool")); eng.onSwap(ev(T0 + 617, 212_000, M, "CanonPool")); eng.tick(T0 + 618);
  const en = [...eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.exec, "reconstructed-pool-state");
  st.heartbeat(T0 + 618); st.db.close();
  // restart
  st = new Store(db); const eng2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(eng2); eng2.restartGap(st.lastAlive(), T0 + 640); eng2.feedUp(T0 + 640);
  const po2 = new Poller({ engine: eng2, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t }); po2.restore();
  assert.equal(eng2.coins.get(M).mode, "live", "restored as live, not as sampled"); assert.ok(!po2.watch.has(M));
  eng2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 641, 75_000, M, "CanonPool")); eng2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 643, 74_000, M, "CanonPool")); // a 63% fall: through every stop, the 60% one included
  const r = [...eng2.entries.values()].find((x) => x.id === en.id) ?? st.allEntries().find((x) => x.id === en.id);
  assert.ok(r.versions.filter((v) => v.policy === "trail").every((v) => v.status === "closed"), "the fall was seen and every trailing stop sold"); assert.equal(eng2.stats.liveIgnoredSampled ?? 0, 0);
});

test("C1 a database written before the fix (coin stored as sampled, position opened live) is corrected on restore: the entry's method wins", () => {
  const db = tmpDb(), M = "OldRecord1"; let st = new Store(db), eng = mkEngine({ store: st }); eng.feedUp(T0);
  eng.coin(M, { pool: "CanonPool", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: null, createTs: T0 - 9 * H, info: { monitoring_mode: "polled", decimals: 6 } }); // the stale record
  path(eng, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10, { mint: M, pool: "CanonPool" }); eng.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 210_000, M, "CanonPool")); eng.onSwap(ev(T0 + 607, 212_000, M, "CanonPool")); eng.tick(T0 + 608);
  const en = [...eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); st.heartbeat(T0 + 608); st.db.close();
  st = new Store(db); const eng2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(eng2); eng2.restartGap(st.lastAlive(), T0 + 640); eng2.feedUp(T0 + 640);
  assert.equal(eng2.coins.get(M).mode, "live"); assert.equal(eng2.stats.modeForced, 1); assert.equal(eng2.coins.get(M).pool, "CanonPool");
  eng2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 641, 120_000, M, "CanonPool")); eng2.onSwap(ev(T0 + 643, 119_000, M, "CanonPool"));
  const r = [...eng2.entries.values()].find((x) => x.id === en.id) ?? st.allEntries().find((x) => x.id === en.id);
  assert.ok(r.versions.some((v) => v.status === "closed" && v.reason === "trail"), "the position is managed again");
  st.db.close(); st = new Store(db); const eng3 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(eng3); assert.equal(eng3.stats.modeForced ?? 0, 0, "and the correction itself was saved");
});

test("C2 sparse back-fill proves nothing about the minutes it leaves out: one bar does not repair an hour-long outage, and CONT does not fire on it", () => {
  const cov = new Coverage(); cov.up(0); const sr = new Series("M", cov); sr.startLive(0); cov.drop(10_000); cov.up(13_600);
  const one = sr.addBars([{ m: Math.floor(10_000 / 60), o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 }], { from: 10_000, to: 13_600, patchGap: true });
  assert.equal(one.accepted, false); assert.match(one.why, /stop before the end/); assert.equal(sr.covered(9_000, 14_000), false);
  const holed = []; for (let m = Math.floor(10_000 / 60); m <= Math.floor(13_600 / 60); m++) if (m < 180 || m > 190) holed.push({ m, o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 });
  const h = sr.addBars(holed, { from: 10_000, to: 13_600, patchGap: true }); assert.equal(h.accepted, false); assert.match(h.why, /hole of 12 minutes/);
  const full = []; for (let m = Math.floor(10_000 / 60); m <= Math.floor(13_600 / 60); m++) full.push({ m, o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 });
  assert.equal(sr.addBars(full, { from: 10_000, to: 13_600, patchGap: true }).accepted, true); assert.equal(sr.covered(9_000, 14_000), true);
  // history before the live start: only the unbroken run that reaches it counts
  const c2 = new Coverage(); c2.up(20_000); const s2 = new Series("M", c2); s2.startLive(20_000);
  const bars = [200, 201, 202, 320, 321, 322, 323, 324, 325, 326, 327, 328, 329, 330, 331, 332].map((m) => ({ m, o: 1, h: 1, l: 1, c: 1 }));
  const r = s2.addBars(bars, { from: 12_000, to: 20_000 }); assert.equal(r.accepted, true); assert.equal(s2.histFrom, 320 * 60, "history goes back to minute 320, not across the two-hour hole to minute 200");
  // engine: an hour-long outage 'repaired' by a single bar gives no CONT entry
  const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 60_000, 10);
  e.feedDown(T0 + 601); e.feedUp(T0 + 4200); e.coins.get("MintAAApump").series.addBars([{ m: Math.floor((T0 + 601) / 60), o: 6e-5, h: 6e-5, l: 6e-5, c: 6e-5 }], { from: T0 + 601, to: T0 + 4200, patchGap: true });
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 4201, 130_000)); e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 4210, 131_000)); assert.equal([...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("CONT")).length, 0);
});

test("C2 a newer back-filled close is used in preference to an older exact observation (15-minute rise measured from the right price)", () => {
  const cov = new Coverage(); cov.up(0); const sr = new Series("M", cov); sr.startLive(0);
  sr.add(1000, 1.0); cov.drop(1001); cov.up(1900);                                       // last exact print 1.00, then an outage
  const bars = []; for (let m = 16; m <= 31; m++) bars.push({ m, o: 1.6, h: 1.6, l: 1.6, c: 1.6 }); // back-fill: the price was 1.60 during the outage
  assert.equal(sr.addBars(bars, { from: 1001, to: 1900, patchGap: true }).accepted, true);
  sr.add(2100, 2.0);
  const p = sr.priceAt(2100 - 900); assert.equal(p.px, 1.6); assert.equal(p.src, "backfill"); assert.ok(2.0 / p.px < 1.3, "the real 15-minute rise is 25%, not the 100% an old observation would suggest");
  assert.equal(sr.priceAt(1005).px, 1.0, "before any back-filled minute has completed, the last exact print is still the answer");
  assert.equal(sr.highIn(1100, 1900).px, 1.6, "highs and lows inside a repaired outage come from its bars");
  const c2 = new Coverage(); c2.up(0); const s2 = new Series("M", c2); s2.startLive(0); s2.add(1000, 1.0); s2.add(1100, 1.1); assert.equal(s2.priceAt(1200).src, "obs", "without back-fill nothing changes: the exact observation is the answer");
});

const u = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigUInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, i64 = (v) => { const x = Buffer.alloc(8); x.writeBigInt64LE(BigInt(v)); return x; }, pk = (n) => Buffer.alloc(32, n);
const buyEv = (B, Q, b, { paid = null, ix = null } = {}) => { const q = (Q * b) / (B - b); const e = decodeEvent(Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(DISC.BuyEvent), i64(1), u(b), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(B), u(Q), u(q), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(0), u(q), u(paid ?? q + q / 100n), pk(9), pk(2)]).toString("base64")); e.ix = ix; return { e, q }; };

test("C3 a quote-side break in the reserve chain is a discontinuity; fee dust under 0.1% is counted and tolerated", () => {
  const B = 200_000_000_000_000n, Q = 200_000_000_000n, b = 10n ** 12n;
  const p = { vq: 0 }, a = buyEv(B, Q, b); assert.equal(applyEvent(p, a.e).discontinuity, false);
  const n0 = dec.chainBadQuote, r = applyEvent(p, buyEv(B - b, (Q + a.q) / 2n, b).e);        // base chains exactly, quote reserves halved
  assert.equal(r.discontinuity, true); assert.equal(dec.chainBadQuote, n0 + 1); assert.equal(reduceTx({ ...p }, [buyEv(BigInt(Math.round(p.base * 1e6)), 50_000_000_000n, b).e]).lost, true);
  const p2 = { vq: 0 }, a2 = buyEv(B, Q, b); applyEvent(p2, a2.e); const m0 = dec.chainQuoteMinor;
  const r2 = applyEvent(p2, buyEv(B - b, Q + a2.q + (Q / 100_000n), b).e);                  // 0.001% off: dust
  assert.equal(r2.discontinuity, false); assert.equal(dec.chainQuoteMinor, m0 + 1);
});

test("C3 a multi_hop_swap that happens to fit the curve gives a state but never a fee; a nonsense rate never prices a fill", () => {
  const B = 200_000_000_000_000n, Q = 200_000_000_000n, b = 10n ** 12n, p = { vq: 0 };
  applyEvent(p, buyEv(B, Q, b, { ix: "buy" }).e); assert.ok(Math.abs(p.feeBuy - 0.01) < 1e-6);
  const mh = buyEv(BigInt(Math.round(p.base * 1e6)), BigInt(Math.round(p.quote * 1e9)), b, { ix: "multi_hop_swap" }); mh.e.userQuote = mh.q / 2n; // "paid" half the curve amount → −50%
  const k0 = dec.feeNotMeasured, r = applyEvent(p, mh.e); assert.ok(r && !r.discontinuity, "its reserves are a valid observation"); assert.equal(dec.feeNotMeasured, k0 + 1);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.state.feeBuy - 0.01) < 1e-6, "the −50% rate was not installed");
  const inRange = buyEv(BigInt(Math.round(p.base * 1e6)), BigInt(Math.round(p.quote * 1e9)), b, { ix: "multi_hop_swap" }); inRange.e.userQuote = inRange.q + inRange.q / 20n; // 5%: plausible-looking, still not a validated instruction
  applyEvent(p, inRange.e); assert.ok(Math.abs(p.feeBuy - 0.01) < 1e-6, "an unvalidated instruction never sets the fee, even with a plausible number");
});

test("C3 a state rebuilt from a boost event is quarantined: no fill, no peak, no signal until the next swap confirms it", () => {
  const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 10);
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 605, 210_000)); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "pending");
  e.onEvent({ t: T0 + 605.5, mint: "MintAAApump", pool: "PoolA", kind: "boost", state: { base: BASE, quote: quoteFor(400_000), feeBuy: 0.003, feeSell: 0.003, supply: 1e9, approx: "boost" }, sol: 0, isBuy: null });
  e.tick(T0 + 606.5); assert.equal(en.status, "pending", "the boost state priced nothing"); assert.equal(e.stats.approxStates, 1); assert.equal(e.entries.size, 1, "and created no second signal");
  e.onSwap(ev(T0 + 607, 230_000)); assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); assert.equal(en.fill.quote_sol, quoteFor(230_000));
});

test("C4 a half-sale is never executed on routed quotes taken at different market moments; a failed quote is dropped, not carried forward", async () => {
  const e = newEntry({ mint: "M", pool: null, ag: "A1", t: 1000, families: ["LOW4H"], features: {}, mc: 1e5, px: 1 }); e.exec = "routed-quote";
  observe(e, { kind: "quote", reqT: 1001, t: 1001.3, buy: { sol: 0.2, tok: 1000, reqT: 1001, t: 1001.3 }, sells: [] }, 1001.3); const tok = e.fill.tok;
  const Qs = (full, half, rf, rh) => ({ kind: "quote", reqT: Math.max(rf, rh), t: Math.max(rf, rh) + 0.3, buy: null, sells: [{ tok, sol: full, reqT: rf, t: rf + 0.3 }, { tok: tok / 2, sol: half, reqT: rh, t: rh + 0.3 }] });
  observe(e, Qs(0.42, 0.21, 1100, 1100.2), 1100.5); const v = e.versions.find((x) => x.policy === "scale" && x.stop === 0.4); assert.equal(v.status, "scaling");
  observe(e, Qs(0.60, 0.21, 1702, 1102), 1702.3);                        // half quoted at 1102, the full holding ten minutes later
  assert.equal(v.status, "scaling", "quotes ten minutes apart are not one pricing state"); assert.equal(v.half, null); assert.equal(v.scaleWaits, 1);
  observe(e, Qs(0.60, 0.30, 1703, 1704), 1704.3);                        // a fresh, coherent pair
  assert.equal(v.status, "open"); assert.ok(Math.abs(v.half.sol - (0.30 - 0.0005)) < 1e-12); assert.equal(v.half.quote_spread_s, 1); assert.equal(v.half.waited_for_coherent_quotes, 1);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.peak - (0.60 - 0.0005) * ((0.30 - 0.0005) / (0.60 - 0.0005))) < 1e-9, "the trail is rescaled from quotes of one moment");
  // poller: when the quote for one size fails, the old quote for that size is not offered again
  const M = "SampledCoin9", w = world(), eng = mkEngine(), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, now: () => w.t });
  w.px.set(M, 1e-4); po.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let k = 0; k < 31; k++) { w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); } w.px.set(M, 2.05e-4); w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); w.t += 1.1; await po.quotes();
  const en = [...eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); w.t += 11; await po.quotes(); const held = po.held.get(en.id); assert.ok(held.size >= 2);
  w.failSize = Math.floor(en.fill.tok * 1e6); w.t += 11; await po.quotes(); assert.ok(![...held.keys()].some((k) => Math.abs(k - en.fill.tok) < 1e-9), "the failed size holds no stale quote");
});

test("C5 a sampled token without supply or market cap is not valued from a default, and missing decimals are not accepted", async () => {
  assert.equal(validDec(null), false); assert.equal(validDec(undefined), false); assert.equal(validDec(6), true); assert.equal(validDec(6.5), false);
  const M = "NoSupply1", w = world(), eng = mkEngine(), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, now: () => w.t });
  assert.equal(po.add({ ...w.tok("NoDec"), decimals: null }, "t"), false, "decimals: null is rejected"); assert.equal(po.stats.noMeta, 1);
  w.px.set(M, 1e-4); po.add({ ...w.tok(M), circ_supply: null, mcap: null }, "t");
  for (let k = 0; k < 31; k++) { w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); } w.px.set(M, 2.1e-4); w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); // would be "$210,000" at an assumed billion tokens
  assert.equal(eng.entries.size, 0); assert.ok(eng.stats.noValuation > 0); assert.equal(eng.stats.evals ?? 0, 0);
});

test("C5 / B5 a restored sampled position whose stored record lacks decimals is not skipped silently: metadata is fetched again, or the position is flagged", async () => {
  const db = tmpDb(), M = "OldSampled1", w = world(); let st = new Store(db), eng = mkEngine({ store: st }), po = new Poller({ engine: eng, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t });
  w.px.set(M, 1e-4); po.add(w.tok(M), "t"); for (let k = 0; k < 31; k++) { w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); } w.px.set(M, 2.05e-4); w.t += 10; await po.sweep(); w.t += 1.1; await po.quotes();
  const en = [...eng.entries.values()][0]; assert.equal(en.status, "filled"); eng.tick(w.t + 2);
  const info = { ...eng.coins.get(M).info }; delete info.decimals; st.setCoinMode(M, null, info); st.db.close();           // the record as an earlier version wrote it
  st = new Store(db); let eng2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(eng2); let po2 = new Poller({ engine: eng2, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t });
  assert.equal(po2.restore(), 0); assert.equal(po2.stats.restoreNoMeta, 1); assert.equal(await po2.repair(), 1); assert.ok(po2.watch.has(M));
  w.t += 20; const before = eng2.stats.quotes; await po2.quotes(); assert.ok(eng2.stats.quotes > before, "quotes flow again");
  // and when the provider has no metadata either, the position stays visible and flagged
  st.setCoinMode(M, null, info); st.db.close(); st = new Store(db); eng2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(eng2); w.meta = false; po2 = new Poller({ engine: eng2, jup: w.jup, store: st, now: () => w.t }); po2.restore();
  assert.equal(await po2.repair(), 0); assert.match([...eng2.entries.values()][0].flags.join(), /restored without token metadata/);
});

  J.setFetch(async (url) => { order.push(/order/.test(url) ? "order" : /price/.test(url) ? "price" : "background"); return { status: 200, headers: [], json: async () => (/order/.test(url) ? { outAmount: "5", inAmount: "1" } : /price/.test(url) ? {} : []) }; });
  const bg = []; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) bg.push(J.tokenStats("Mint" + k)); const pr = J.prices(["A"]); const q = J.quote("MintQ", "buy", 2e8);
  await Promise.all([...bg, pr, q]); delete process.env.JUP_RPS;
  assert.ok(order.indexOf("order") <= 1, "the quote went out first (at most one request was already in flight): " + order.join()); assert.ok(order.indexOf("price") <= 2); assert.equal(order.filter((x) => x === "background").length, 8);
