// NINTH REVIEW (Codex on the full export of 23 Nov 2026, code 834ca71 / 982ecde) — two execution defects, each reproduced here.
//   A. a LIVE coin whose token program or Token-2022 extensions were never read was bought as if it had no transfer fee;
//      the mint-account reader only ran for sampled coins and only when the sampled adapter was switched on; a pending
//      live buy was not checked again when the coin's restrictions changed.
//   B. a stream that stays connected but runs behind the chain priced orders and created signals as if it were current
//      (receipt time said "1 s old"; the underlying events were up to 112 s old on 23 Nov).
// A2, A5–A7 and B1–B5 fail on 982ecde and pass after the correction; A1, A3 and A4 hold behaviour that must not change.
import test from "node:test"; import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import * as R from "../src/risk.mjs"; import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";

const M = "MintAAApump", ents = (e) => [...e.entries.values()], filled = (e) => ents(e).filter((x) => x.status === "filled");
/** a live coin 9 hours old; `info` is what is known about its token (nothing is assumed: tokens: null) */
const mk = (info = {}, opts = {}) => { const e = mkEngine({ tokens: null, ...opts }); e.feedUp(T0); Object.assign(e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }).info, info); return e; };
/** ten flat minutes at $100K, then a jump to $210K: a fresh FAST crossing → buys scheduled 1 s later. Returns the jump time. */
const jump = (e, t0 = T0, o = {}) => { path(e, t0, t0 + 600, () => 100_000, o.lag != null ? 2 : 10, o); swap(e, t0 + 600, 210_000, { sol: 5, ...o }); return t0 + 600; }; // a test that feeds the stream's delay itself must keep it measured: every 2 s
const settle = (e, t, o = {}) => { swap(e, t + 1.2, 210_000, o); e.tick(t + 1.3); };
/** getAccountInfo / getMultipleAccounts value of a mint account (jsonParsed) */
const mint = (program, extensions = []) => ({ owner: program, data: { parsed: { info: { decimals: 6, supply: "1000000000000000", mintAuthority: null, freezeAuthority: null, extensions } } } });
const feeExt = (bps) => ({ extension: "transferFeeConfig", state: { newerTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: bps }, olderTransferFee: { transferFeeBasisPoints: bps } } });

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- A. live Token-2022 eligibility
test("A1 a classic SPL token (known program, no transfer fee possible) is still bought on the live method", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }), t = jump(e); settle(e, t);
  assert.ok(filled(e).length > 0, "supported execution: the buy fills"); assert.equal(filled(e)[0].token.xfer_bps, 0);
});
test("A2 a live Token-2022 coin whose extensions were never read is NOT bought as fee-free; neither is a coin whose token program is unknown", () => {
  for (const info of [{ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: { status: "missing", why: "Token-2022 mint: extensions not yet read from the chain" } }, {}]) {
    const e = mk(info), t = jump(e); settle(e, t);
    assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0, "no buy is created while the fee is not known");
    assert.ok(e.stats.feeUnknown > 0, "counted"); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(e.coins.get(M).info), false);
  }
});
test("A3 a Token-2022 coin whose mint account was read and shows no fee and no restriction follows the supported path", () => {
  const k = R.classifyMintAccount(mint(R.TOKEN_2022, [{ extension: "metadataPointer", state: {} }])); assert.equal(k.status, "clean"); assert.equal(k.fee_bps, 0);
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: k }), t = jump(e); settle(e, t);
  assert.ok(filled(e).length > 0); assert.equal(filled(e)[0].token.token_program, R.TOKEN_2022); assert.equal(filled(e)[0].token.risk_status, "clean");
});
test("A4 a live coin known to charge a transfer fee is not bought (fee coins are supported on the sampled method only)", () => {
  const k = R.classifyMintAccount(mint(R.TOKEN_2022, [feeExt(300)])); assert.equal(k.fee_bps, 300); assert.notEqual(k.status, "restricted");
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: k }), t = jump(e); settle(e, t);
  assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0);
});
test("A5 a restriction that appears between the decision and the scheduled fill cancels the pending live buy", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: R.classifyMintAccount(mint(R.TOKEN_2022)) }), t = jump(e);
  const pend = ents(e); assert.ok(pend.length > 0 && pend.every((x) => x.status === "pending"), "decided, not yet filled");
  const k = R.classifyMintAccount(mint(R.TOKEN_2022, [feeExt(800)])); assert.equal(k.status, "restricted"); e.coins.get(M).info.risk = k; // read again: now 8%
  settle(e, t);
  assert.equal(filled(e).length, 0, "never filled under the old zero-fee classification");
  assert.ok(pend.every((x) => x.status === "cancelled" && x.flags.some((f) => /cancelled before the buy/.test(f) && /restricted/.test(f))), "cancelled, with the reason on the record");
});
test("A6 the mint-account reader serves LIVE coins and does not depend on the sampled adapter", async () => {
  const e = mk({}), t = jump(e); settle(e, t); // unknown program: not bought, and now wanted
  const c = e.coins.get(M); assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0);
  assert.equal(R.nextRiskRead(e, t + 2), c, "the live coin a decision was waiting on is the next mint account to read — no poller exists in this test");
  const calls = [], rpc = async (method, params) => { calls.push([method, params[0]]); return { value: params[0].map(() => mint(R.TOKEN_2022)) }; };
  assert.ok(typeof R.riskReadOnce === "function", "one reader for both methods"); assert.equal(await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, rpc, t + 2), 1);
  assert.deepEqual(calls[0][1], [M]); assert.equal(c.info.token_program, R.TOKEN_2022); assert.equal(c.info.risk.status, "clean"); assert.equal(R.feeKnown(c.info), true);
  const t2 = jump(e, t + 3600); settle(e, t2); // a NEW fresh crossing after the read
  assert.ok(filled(e).length > 0, "once its fee is known the coin is evaluated and bought like any other");
  // the production loop that calls the reader is not inside the sampled-adapter switch
  const src = readFileSync(new URL("../src/main.mjs", import.meta.url), "utf8"), a = src.indexOf('if (process.env.OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE === "1") {'), b = src.indexOf("Solana-wide adapter ON");
  assert.ok(a > 0 && b > a && src.includes("riskReadOnce(engine") && !src.slice(a, b).includes("riskReadOnce"), "main.mjs starts the reader unconditionally");
  // a failed read is retried and leaves the coin unknown
  const e2 = mk({}), tb = jump(e2); await R.riskReadOnce(e2, null, async () => { throw new Error("rpc down"); }, tb + 2);
  assert.equal(R.feeKnown(e2.coins.get(M).info), false); assert.equal(R.nextRiskRead(e2, tb + 3), null, "not hammered"); assert.equal(R.nextRiskRead(e2, tb + 70), e2.coins.get(M), "tried again after a minute");
});
test("A7 a fee found on a coin already held on the live method is recorded on the position; the position and its exits stay", async () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.TOKEN_2022, risk: R.classifyMintAccount(mint(R.TOKEN_2022)) }), t = jump(e); settle(e, t);
  const pos = filled(e); assert.ok(pos.length > 0); const c = e.coins.get(M); c.riskRead = true; c.riskAt = t;
  await R.riskReadOnce(e, null, async (m, p) => ({ value: p[0].map(() => mint(R.TOKEN_2022, [feeExt(300)])) }), t + S.RISK_REFRESH_S + 5, S.RISK_REFRESH_S);
  assert.equal(c.info.risk.fee_bps, 300);
  assert.ok(pos.every((x) => x.status === "filled" && x.flags.some((f) => /found after the buy/.test(f))), "still held, now flagged");
  assert.ok(pos.every((x) => x.versions.every((v) => v.status === "open" || v.exit)), "no row was deleted or closed at an invented value");
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- B. a connected stream that runs behind the chain
const S5 = () => S.FEED_MAX_LAG_S;
test("B1 a stream running 60 s behind creates no signal and fills no buy, although every state is 1 s old by receive time", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 2, { lag: 1 }); // current
  swap(e, T0 + 600, 210_000, { sol: 5, lag: 60 }); swap(e, T0 + 601.2, 210_000, { lag: 60 }); e.tick(T0 + 601.3); // the doubling arrives 60 s late
  for (let t = T0 + 602; t < T0 + 620; t += 2) { swap(e, t, 215_000, { lag: 60 }); e.tick(t + 0.5); }
  assert.equal(filled(e).length, 0, "nothing is priced from a backlogged state"); assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0, "and no signal is created from it");
  assert.ok(e.stats.lateEvents > 0, "counted"); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 610), false);
});
test("B2 after the stream is current again, catch-up observations cannot manufacture a fresh crossing; a clean window fires normally", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 2, { lag: 1 });
  for (let t = T0 + 600; t < T0 + 640; t += 2) swap(e, t, t < T0 + 620 ? 100_000 : 210_000, { lag: 60 }); // the move happens while the stream is late
  for (let t = T0 + 640; t < T0 + 652; t += 1) swap(e, t, 210_000, { lag: 1 }); // current again (and stays so for FEED_MAX_LAG_S)
  assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 651), true, "freshness re-established by measurements, not by a lifetime average");
  swap(e, T0 + 653, 212_000, { lag: 1 }); e.tick(T0 + 655);
  assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0, "the 100K → 210K move was only seen through late observations: no FAST / FAST150 buy");
  path(e, T0 + 656, T0 + 1300, () => 210_000, 2, { lag: 1 }); swap(e, T0 + 1300, 445_000, { sol: 5, lag: 1 }); swap(e, T0 + 1301.2, 445_000, { lag: 1 }); e.tick(T0 + 1301.3);
  assert.ok(filled(e).length > 0, "a doubling observed on a current stream in a clean window is bought as before");
});
test("B3 orders pending when the stream falls behind: the buy is cancelled by the pricing timeout, a triggered sale waits and is priced with its real delay", () => {
  // (a) pending buy
  const a = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }); path(a, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 2, { lag: 1 }); swap(a, T0 + 600, 210_000, { sol: 5, lag: 1 });
  const pend = ents(a); assert.ok(pend.length > 0 && pend.every((x) => x.status === "pending"));
  for (let t = T0 + 600.5; t < T0 + 612; t += 1) { swap(a, t, 212_000, { lag: 60 }); a.tick(t + 0.2); }
  assert.ok(pend.every((x) => x.status === "cancelled" && x.flags.some((f) => /pricing\/data timeout/.test(f))), "not filled from a late state: the existing 5 s data timeout applies");
  // (b) pending sale
  const b = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }), t = jump(b, T0, { lag: 1 }); settle(b, t, { lag: 1 }); const pos = filled(b); assert.ok(pos.length > 0);
  for (let x = t + 3; x < t + 10; x += 2) b.noteLag(x, 1); // other pools keep the stream measured
  swap(b, t + 10, 150_000, { lag: 1 }); // −29%: the 20% and 25% trailing rows trigger, sale scheduled 0.7 s later
  const sell = pos[0].versions.filter((v) => v.status === "selling"); assert.ok(sell.length > 0);
  for (let x = t + 10.3; x < t + 40; x += 1) { swap(b, x, 120_000, { lag: 60 }); b.tick(x + 0.2); } // the stream is now 60 s behind
  assert.ok(sell.every((v) => v.status === "selling"), "the sale is NOT priced from backlogged states — it stays pending, visible");
  for (let x = t + 40; x < t + 40 + S5() + 2; x += 1) swap(b, x, 120_000, { lag: 1 }); b.tick(t + 50);
  assert.ok(sell.every((v) => v.status === "closed" && v.exit.delay_s > 20), "priced by the first current state, with the delay it really had");
  assert.ok(sell.every((v) => v.exit.sol < 0.2005 * 0.7), "at the price then in force (120K), not at the trigger's");
});
test("B4 a QUIET pool is still trusted while the stream as a whole is current; a silent stream is not proof of anything", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }), t = jump(e, T0, { lag: 1 }); settle(e, t, { lag: 1 }); const pos = filled(e); assert.ok(pos.length > 0);
  const end = pos[0].fill.t + S.MAX_HOLD_S; // nothing trades on this pool for 24 h; other pools keep the stream's delay measured
  for (let x = t + 4; x < end - 30; x += 4) e.noteLag(x, 1.1);
  e.tick(end - 30); const open = pos[0].versions.filter((v) => v.status === "open"); assert.ok(open.length > 0);
  // the stream goes silent for the last 30 s before the 24-hour limit and stays silent: freshness is unknown
  e.tick(end + 1); assert.equal(e.freshAt(end), false);
  assert.ok(open.every((v) => v.status === "selling"), "the 24-hour sale is due but not priced on an unverified stream");
  // same position, stream measured current across the limit: the old, unchanged state prices the sale at the scheduled time
  const f = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }), tf = jump(f, T0, { lag: 1 }); settle(f, tf, { lag: 1 }); const pf = filled(f), endF = pf[0].fill.t + S.MAX_HOLD_S;
  for (let x = tf + 4; x < endF + 6; x += 4) f.noteLag(x, 1.1);
  f.tick(endF + 5);
  const rows = pf[0].versions.filter((v) => v.reason === "max-hold"); assert.ok(rows.length > 0 && rows.every((v) => v.status === "closed"));
  assert.ok(rows.every((v) => v.exit.delay_s === 0 && v.exit.state_age_s > 80_000), "an old unchanged pool state is valid when the feed is current");
});
test("B5 freshness that has not been established is not assumed: after a (re)connect nothing is evaluated until the delay is measured", () => {
  const e = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }); assert.equal(e.freshAt(T0 + 1), false);
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 2); swap(e, T0 + 600, 210_000, { sol: 5 }); swap(e, T0 + 601.2, 210_000); e.tick(T0 + 601.3); // no delay measurement at all
  assert.equal(ents(e).length, 0); assert.ok(e.stats.lateEvents > 0);
  // a reconnect resets the evidence
  const g = mk({ token_program: R.SPL_TOKEN }, { stream: "manual" }); path(g, T0, T0 + 100, () => 100_000, 2, { lag: 1 }); assert.equal(g.freshAt(T0 + 99), true);
  g.feedDown(T0 + 100, "close"); g.feedUp(T0 + 102); assert.equal(g.freshAt(T0 + 102.5), false, "unknown again until the first measurement on the new connection");
  swap(g, T0 + 103, 100_000, { lag: 1 }); assert.equal(g.freshAt(T0 + 103), true);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/run11.test.mjs
====================================================================================================
// older-11 — STARTING THE RUN: the site shows only the run's books, the 8-hour books can start at 6 hours, a new run gets a
// new database prepared from the previous one (identities only, nothing deleted, no buys carried), the previous run's price
// memory is carried in without its decisions, and the start script fixes a run's settings at its first start.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, mkdirSync, chmodSync, statSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join, dirname } from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process"; import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { booksApi } from "../src/page.mjs";
import { bookRows } from "../src/report.mjs";
import { saveWarm, loadWarm } from "../src/warm.mjs";
import { seed } from "../scripts/seed-db.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", dir = () => mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older11-")), HERE = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const fams = (e) => [...e.entries.values()].flatMap((x) => x.families).sort();
// a coin `ageH` hours old: ten quiet minutes at $100K, then a double inside one minute (FAST-1M / 3M / 5M fire), then the fill
const doubled = (ageH, set = () => {}) => { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - ageH * H }); set(e); path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 612, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 615, 205_000); return e; };

test("the site of a run that chose its books shows ONLY those books; FEE-COINS only when it holds a buy; without a run everything is as before", () => {
  const e = doubled(20), all = [...e.entries.values()]; assert.ok(fams(e).includes("FAST-1M"), "the legacy run bought: " + fams(e));
  const old = booksApi(all, { stop: 0.25 }); assert.equal(old.order.length, S.FAMILIES.length + 1); assert.equal(old.order.at(-1), S.FEE_BOOK); assert.equal(old.run, null); assert.equal(old.rows_total, 1340); assert.match(old.rules.VOL100K, /buys only in a run/);
  const run = { name: "older11", books: S.booksOf("older-11"), gates: true, rebuy: false, min_age_h: 6, sampled: false }, j = booksApi(all, { stop: 0.25, run });
  assert.deepEqual(j.order, S.BOOKS_11, "the run's 13 books, nothing else — no empty FEE-COINS card"); assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(j.books).sort(), [...S.BOOKS_11].sort());
  assert.ok(j.books["FAST-1M"].length >= 1); assert.equal(j.books["FAST-10M"], undefined, "a book outside the run is not on the page even though the legacy engine bought for it");
  assert.deepEqual(j.run, { name: "older11", books: S.BOOKS_11, gates: true, gate_mode: "cancel", checks: "all", rebuy: false, min_age_h: 6, sampled: false }); assert.doesNotMatch(j.rules.VOL100K + j.rules.DIP15K, /buys only in a run/);
  assert.equal(j.rows_total, (7 * 4 + 6 * 5) * 5 * 2, "7 books in 4 age groups + 6 any-age books in 5, × 5 stops × 2 exits");
  const fee = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(all[0])); fee.token.xfer_bps = 300; const jf = booksApi([fee], { stop: 0.25, run }); assert.equal(jf.order.at(-1), S.FEE_BOOK, "a fee-coin buy is never dropped from the page: its book appears when it has one"); assert.equal(jf.books[S.FEE_BOOK].length, 1);
});

test("the provider reading a buy was judged on is on its row of the page", () => {
  const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); e.setBooks(S.booksOf("older-11")); e.gates = S.GATES_11; e.clock = () => T0 + 610.2;
  e.readGate = async () => ({ stat: { top_10_holder_rate: 0.22, top_bundler_trader_percentage: 0.06, top_rat_trader_percentage: 0.03, top70_sniper_hold_rate: 0.02 }, total_fee: 64.5, link: { twitter_username: "x" }, ath_price: 0.0005, total_supply: 1e9, holder_count: 1234 });
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); const en = [...e.entries.values()][0]; assert.ok(en && en.status === "pending", "the signal opened a pending buy");
  return new Promise((res) => setImmediate(() => { swap(e, T0 + 612, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 615, 205_000); // the reading arrived 0.2 s after the signal; the buy is 1 s after it
    assert.equal(en.status, "filled", en.flags.join("; ")); assert.equal(en.gate.verdict, "pass");
    const r = booksApi([en], { stop: 0.25, run: { books: S.booksOf("older-11"), gates: true } }).books["FAST-1M"][0];
    assert.deepEqual(r.gate, { v: "pass", ms: 200, late: undefined, top10: 0.22, bund: 0.06, ins: 0.03, snip: 0.02, fees: 64.5, tw: true, ath: 500_000, holders: 1234, opt: { bundlers_main: false, bundlers_second: true, insiders_main: true, insiders_second: true } });
    res(); }));
});

test("the 8-hour books from 6 hours: off by default (a 7-hour-old coin is not bought), on with the run's setting — in age group A0, never under the run's own age, and the table keeps the buy", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(fams(doubled(7)), [], "default: nothing under 8 hours for the FAST books");
  const on = (x) => { x.minAgeS = S.MIN_AGE_11_S; }, e = doubled(7, on), en = [...e.entries.values()];
  assert.ok(fams(e).includes("FAST-1M") && fams(e).includes("FAST-5M"), "bought at 7 hours: " + fams(e)); assert.ok(en.every((x) => x.ag === "A0")); assert.ok(e.stats.evalsEarly > 0);
  assert.ok(!fams(e).includes("AGE-DOUBLE"), "AGE-DOUBLE needs an age-group start and has none under 8 hours");
  assert.deepEqual(fams(doubled(5, on)), [], "5 hours old is under the run's 6 hours");
  assert.deepEqual(fams(doubled(20, on)), fams(doubled(20)), "a coin over 8 hours old is decided exactly as without the setting");
  swap(e, T0 + 700, 80_000); swap(e, T0 + 702, 80_000); swap(e, T0 + 704, 80_000); // −60%: every stop up to 40% sells
  const rows = bookRows(en), r = rows.find((x) => x.family === "FAST-1M" && x.ag === "A0" && x.stop === 0.25 && x.policy === "trail");
  assert.ok(r && r.filled === 1 && r.closed === 1, "the under-8-hour buy has its own row"); assert.equal(bookRows([]).length, 1340, "and no row is added when there is no such buy");
});

test("a new run's database is prepared from the previous one: coin identities and pools only — no buys, decisions or bars — and the previous database is not changed", () => {
  const d = dir(), A = join(d, "older-a.db"), B = join(d, "older-b.db"), st = new Store(A), e = mkEngine({ store: st }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H });
  st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H, createSrc: "gmgn", info: { monitoring_mode: "live", token_program: "T" } }); st.saveCoin("Sampled1", { createTs: T0 - 30 * H, info: { monitoring_mode: "polled" } }); st.savePool("PoolA", { mint: M, quote: "SOL", creator: "c" });
  st.db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta VALUES ('inventory', ?)").run(JSON.stringify({ at: "2026-11-24T20:19:00Z" })); st.firstStart(T0); st.heartbeat(T0 + 900);
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 100_000, 20); swap(e, T0 + 610, 205_000); swap(e, T0 + 612, 205_000); e.prune(T0 + 700); e.flush();
  const n = (db, t) => db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM ${t}`).get().n, before = ["coins", "pools", "entries", "decisions", "bars"].map((t) => n(st.db, t)); assert.ok(before[2] >= 1 && before[3] >= 1, "the previous run has buys and one-buy marks: " + before); st.db.close();
  const r = seed(A, B, { liveOnly: true }); assert.deepEqual({ seeded: r.seeded, coins: r.coins, skipped: r.skipped_sampled, pools: r.pools, entries: r.entries, decisions: r.decisions, bars: r.bars }, { seeded: true, coins: 1, skipped: 1, pools: 1, entries: 0, decisions: 0, bars: 0 });
  const nb = new Store(B), c = nb.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM coins WHERE mint=?").get(M); assert.equal(c.create_ts, T0 - 20 * H); assert.equal(c.pool, "PoolA"); assert.equal(c.create_src, "gmgn"); assert.equal(JSON.parse(c.info).token_program, "T"); assert.equal(c.last_px, null);
  assert.equal(nb.lastAlive(), null, "the new database has never been alive"); assert.equal(nb.db.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k='first_start'").get(), undefined, "its first start is its own"); assert.ok(nb.db.prepare("SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k='inventory'").get(), "the daily pool inventory is not run again at once");
  const e2 = mkEngine({ store: nb }); nb.restore(e2); assert.equal(e2.coins.get(M).createTs, T0 - 20 * H, "the age is known without asking a provider"); assert.equal(e2.entries.size, 0); assert.deepEqual(e2.coins.get(M).groups ?? {}, {}, "no one-buy mark came across"); nb.db.close();
  assert.equal(seed(A, B).seeded, false, "repeating it leaves the new database alone");
  const again = new Store(A); assert.deepEqual(["coins", "pools", "entries", "decisions", "bars"].map((t) => n(again.db, t)), before, "the previous database is exactly as it was"); again.db.close();
  assert.throws(() => seed(join(d, "nope.db"), join(d, "c.db")), /not found/); assert.throws(() => seed(A, A), /different file/);
});

test("price memory carried into a new run: what was observed comes across, what the previous run decided does not — the new run's first buy of the coin is a first buy", () => {
  const d = dir(), stop = T0 + 5 * H, st = new Store(join(d, "older-a.db")), a = mkEngine({ store: st }); a.feedUp(T0); a.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H, info: {} });
  path(a, T0, T0 + 2 * H, () => 100_000, 60); path(a, T0 + 2 * H, stop - 600, () => 60_000, 60); swap(a, stop - 590, 125_000); swap(a, stop - 585, 125_000); swap(a, stop - 580, 125_000); // LOW4H bought in the previous run (2× the $60K low)
  assert.ok(fams(a).includes("LOW4H"), "previous run: " + fams(a)); path(a, stop - 500, stop, () => 60_000, 60); a.flush(); st.heartbeat(stop);
  const W = join(d, "older-a.db.warm"); saveWarm(W, a, stop); writeFileSync(W + ".copy", readFileSync(W)); st.db.close();
  const start = (fresh) => { const B = join(d, `older-b${fresh ? 1 : 0}.db`); seed(join(d, "older-a.db"), B); const nb = new Store(B), b = mkEngine({ store: nb }); b.rebuy = false; /* the older-11 run: buying again is off */ nb.restore(b); writeFileSync(W, readFileSync(W + ".copy"));
    const r = loadWarm(W, b, stop + 5, { fresh }); assert.ok(r.loaded && r.coins === 1, JSON.stringify(r)); b.restartGap(nb.lastAlive(), stop + 5, { warm: r.loaded }); b.feedUp(stop + 10);
    swap(b, stop + 20, 60_000); swap(b, stop + 40, 125_000); swap(b, stop + 42, 125_000); swap(b, stop + 45, 125_000); return b; };
  const kept = start(false); assert.ok(!fams(kept).includes("LOW4H"), "with the previous run's marks the book would count the coin as already bought");
  const fresh = start(true), en = [...fresh.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H")); assert.ok(en, "carried as a new run: LOW4H buys — the 4-hour low was known from the first minute"); assert.equal(en.again, undefined, "and it is a first buy");
  assert.ok(en.warm_gap_s > 0, "the seconds between the two runs that were not watched are recorded on it"); assert.equal(fresh.stats.warm.carried_from_another_run, true);
});

test("start script: a run's settings are fixed at its first start, an unknown or changed rule set is refused before anything is stopped, and the older-11 settings reach the bot", () => {
  const d = dir(), out = join(d, "out"), bin = join(d, "bin"), logf = join(d, "pm2.log"); mkdirSync(out); mkdirSync(bin);
  writeFileSync(join(bin, "pm2"), `#!/usr/bin/env bash\nif [ "$1" = start ] && [ "$2" = src/main.mjs ]; then echo "START books=$OLDER_BOOKS gates=$OLDER_GATES rebuy=$OLDER_REBUY minage=$OLDER_MIN_AGE_H wide=$OLDER_SOLANA_WIDE db=$(basename "$OLDER_DB") run=$OLDER_RUN_NAME warm=$(basename "$OLDER_WARM_FROM" 2>/dev/null)" >> "${logf}"; touch "$OLDER_DB"; else echo "$1 $2" >> "${logf}"; fi\n`); chmodSync(join(bin, "pm2"), 0o755);
  const run = (...a) => spawnSync("bash", [join(HERE, "../scripts/start.sh"), ...a], { env: { ...process.env, PATH: bin + ":" + process.env.PATH, OLDER_OUT: out }, encoding: "utf8" }), log = () => (existsSync(logf) ? readFileSync(logf, "utf8") : "");
  let r = run("x", "", "older-99"); assert.equal(r.status, 1); assert.match(r.stdout, /unknown settings/); assert.equal(log(), "", "nothing was stopped or started");
  r = run("old"); assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stderr); assert.match(log(), /START books= gates= rebuy= minage= wide=1 db=older-old\.db run=old warm=\n/, "no settings = the run of older-10: " + log());
  r = run("old", "", "older-11"); assert.equal(r.status, 1); assert.match(r.stdout, /already has a database/); assert.ok(!existsSync(join(out, "old.profile")));
  r = run("new11", "", "older-11", "missing"); assert.equal(r.status, 1); assert.match(r.stdout, /has no database/);
  const st = new Store(join(out, "older-old.db")); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0, info: {} }); st.db.close(); const size = statSync(join(out, "older-old.db")).size; writeFileSync(logf, "");
  r = run("new11", "", "older-11", "old"); assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr); assert.match(r.stdout, /new database prepared: 1 coins/); assert.match(r.stdout, /run 'new11' \(settings older-11\)/);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /cold start: run 'old' had other settings/); assert.match(log(), /START books=older-11 gates=older-11 rebuy=0 minage=6 wide=0 db=older-new11\.db run=new11 warm=\n/, "from the old bot: identities only, NO price memory — " + log()); assert.equal(readFileSync(join(out, "new11.profile"), "utf8").trim(), "older-11");
  assert.ok(log().indexOf("delete older") < log().indexOf("START"), "the previous bot is stopped (and writes its price memory) before the new one starts"); assert.ok(statSync(join(out, "older-old.db")).size >= size, "the previous database is still there");
  writeFileSync(logf, ""); r = run("new11"); assert.equal(r.status, 0); assert.match(log(), /START books=older-11 gates=older-11 rebuy=0 minage=6 wide=0 db=older-new11\.db run=new11 warm=\n/, "a plain restart of the name keeps its settings and carries nothing in again: " + log());
  writeFileSync(logf, ""); r = run("score11", "7", "older-11", "new11"); assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr); assert.match(r.stdout, /price memory will be carried from run 'new11'/);
  assert.match(log(), /START books=older-11 gates=older-11 rebuy=0 minage=6 wide=0 db=older-score11\.db run=score11 warm=older-new11\.db\.warm\n/, "validation run → scored run, same settings: a new database with the validated price memory carried in — " + log()); assert.ok(existsSync(join(out, "score11.until")), "and its seven-day entry window is fixed");
  r = run("new11", "", ""); assert.equal(r.status, 0); r = run("old", "", "older-11"); assert.equal(r.status, 1, "and the old name still cannot be turned into the new rule set");
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: older/test/signals.test.mjs

import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";

// coin created 4 h before the feed starts: at 8 h old it has exactly 4 h of covered history
function setup(createAgo = 4 * H) { const e = mkEngine(); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - createAgo }); return e; }
// entries that include LOW4H (other families may also fire on the same synthetic path; each test checks its own family)
const fams = (e) => [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H")).map((x) => x.families.slice().sort().join("+"));

test("LOW4H: a continuous $50K → $100K rise qualifies (no pullback needed); SLOW-BUILD and LIQ25K share the entry", () => {
  const e = setup();
  path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000);                    // flat $50K for 4 h (coin reaches 8 h at T0 + 4 h)
  path(e, T0 + 4 * H + 60, T0 + 5 * H + 60, (t) => 50_000 * (1 + (t - (T0 + 4 * H)) / H * 1.2)); // steady climb, ~2% a minute
  const es = [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H"));
  assert.equal(es.length, 1);
  assert.ok(es[0].families.includes("LOW4H") && es[0].families.includes("SLOW-BUILD") && es[0].families.includes("LOW4H-LIQ25K"), es[0].families.join());
  assert.ok(es[0].signalMc >= 99_999 && es[0].signalMc < 103_000, "enters at the first observation at/above 2× the 4 h low: " + es[0].signalMc);
  assert.equal(es[0].ag, "A1");
});

test("SLOW-BUILD rejects a candle above +20%, records the rejection, and never enters later in that age group", () => {
  const e = setup(); const notes = []; e.store = { saveNote: (m, n) => notes.push(n), saveEntry() {}, saveDecision() {}, saveBar() {}, feedEvent() {} };
  path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000);
  // 40 min later: one minute jumps +30% (open 60K → close 78K), then on to $100K
  const t1 = T0 + 4 * H + 60; path(e, t1, t1 + 35 * 60, (t) => 50_000 + (t - t1) / 60 * 300);
  swap(e, t1 + 35 * 60 + 1, 61_000); swap(e, t1 + 35 * 60 + 50, 79_000);
  path(e, t1 + 37 * 60, t1 + 60 * 60, (t) => 79_000 + (t - t1 - 37 * 60) / 60 * 1_000);
  const fs = fams(e); assert.equal(fs.length, 1); assert.ok(!fs[0].includes("SLOW-BUILD") && fs[0].includes("LOW4H"));
  assert.ok(notes.some((n) => n.family === "SLOW-BUILD" && /above \+20%/.test(n.reason)));
});

test("SLOW-BUILD: a candle of exactly +20% is allowed", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000);
  const t1 = T0 + 4 * H + 120;
  swap(e, t1, 70_000); swap(e, t1 + 30, 84_000);                 // one minute: open 70K close 84K = exactly +20%
  path(e, t1 + 60, t1 + 60 * 60, (t) => 84_000 + (t - t1) / 60 * 400);
  assert.ok(fams(e)[0].includes("SLOW-BUILD"), fams(e).join());
});

test("SLOW-BUILD: under 30 min from the low is rejected", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000);
  const t1 = T0 + 4 * H + 60; swap(e, t1, 45_000); path(e, t1 + 60, t1 + 20 * 60, (t) => 45_000 * (1 + (t - t1) / 60 * 0.08)); // low 45K, doubles in ~13 min
  const f = fams(e); assert.equal(f.length, 1); assert.ok(f[0].includes("LOW4H") && !f[0].includes("SLOW-BUILD"));
});

test("no fabricated crossing after a feed outage: the window is not covered, so nothing enters", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000);
  e.feedDown(T0 + 4 * H + 61, "test"); e.feedUp(T0 + 4 * H + 600);
  swap(e, T0 + 4 * H + 601, 120_000); swap(e, T0 + 4 * H + 660, 125_000);
  assert.equal(e.entries.size, 0);
});

test("a crossing outside the $40K–$1M band is not an entry", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 10_000);
  path(e, T0 + 4 * H + 60, T0 + 5 * H, (t) => 10_000 + (t - T0 - 4 * H) * 5); // crosses $20K well below the band
  assert.equal(e.entries.size, 0);
});

test("LOW4H window boundary: a low older than 4 h is outside the window", () => {
  const e = setup(6 * H); // coin 8 h old at T0 + 2 h
  swap(e, T0, 30_000); path(e, T0 + 60, T0 + 6 * H, () => 70_000); // the $30K low is > 4 h old at T0 + 4 h + 1 min
  swap(e, T0 + 6 * H, 75_000);                                      // 75K is not 2× the 4 h low (70K)
  assert.equal(e.entries.size, 0);
});

test("CONT: +30% over 15 min and within 5% of the 1 h high; CONT-STALL shares the entry", () => {
  const e = setup(7 * H); // 8 h old at T0 + 1 h → needs 1 h covered history
  path(e, T0, T0 + H + 60, () => 100_000);
  path(e, T0 + H + 60, T0 + H + 20 * 60, (t) => 100_000 * (1 + (t - T0 - H - 60) / 600 * 0.5)); // +50% in 10 min
  const es = [...e.entries.values()]; assert.ok(es.length >= 1);
  const c = es.find((x) => x.families.includes("CONT")); assert.ok(c && c.families.includes("CONT-STALL"));
  assert.ok(c.signalMc >= 130_000 && c.signalMc < 136_000, String(c.signalMc));
  assert.equal(c.versions.length, 20); // (plain + stall) × (trail + scale) × 5 stops
});

test("CONT: can qualify immediately at 8 h old when history exists", () => {
  const e = setup(7 * H); path(e, T0, T0 + H - 600, () => 100_000); path(e, T0 + H - 600, T0 + H, (t) => 100_000 * (1 + (t - (T0 + H - 600)) / 600 * 0.4));
  swap(e, T0 + H, 140_000); // first observation at exactly 8 h
  assert.ok([...e.entries.values()].some((x) => x.families.includes("CONT") && x.decisionT === T0 + H));
});

test("HH-BREAK: zero volume baseline is recorded and not entered; a real 2× volume break enters", () => {
  const notes = []; const st = { saveNote: (m, n) => notes.push(n), saveEntry() {}, saveDecision() {}, saveBar() {}, feedEvent() {} };
  const e = setup(3.5 * H); e.store = st;
  path(e, T0, T0 + 4.6 * H, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 0 });            // trades with no volume
  swap(e, T0 + 4.6 * H, 110_000, { sol: 5 });
  assert.ok(notes.some((n) => n.family === "HH-BREAK" && /zero volume/.test(n.reason)));
  assert.ok(![...e.entries.values()].some((x) => x.families.includes("HH-BREAK")));

  const e2 = setup(3.5 * H);
  path(e2, T0, T0 + 4.5 * H, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 1 });           // 1 SOL a minute baseline
  const t1 = T0 + 4.5 * H; path(e2, t1, t1 + 14 * 60, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 3 }); // 3 SOL a minute, flat
  swap(e2, t1 + 14 * 60 + 5, 101_000, { sol: 3 });                    // strictly above the 60-min high
  const h = [...e2.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("HH-BREAK")); assert.ok(h, "HH-BREAK entry");
  assert.equal(h.cands.find((c) => c.family === "HH-BREAK").detail.windows.length, 16);
});

test("AGE-DOUBLE: reference MISSING when history starts after the age-group start; OK and crossing when covered", () => {
  const notes = []; const e = mkEngine(); e.store = { saveNote: (m, n) => notes.push(n), saveEntry() {}, saveDecision() {}, saveBar() {}, feedEvent() {} };
  e.feedUp(T0); e.coin("MintAAApump", { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 9 * H }); // already 9 h old: no observation at 8 h
  path(e, T0, T0 + 600, () => 50_000);
  assert.ok(notes.some((n) => n.family === "AGE-DOUBLE" && n.reason === "reference_status=MISSING"));

  const e2 = setup(7.5 * H); path(e2, T0, T0 + H, () => 60_000);     // at 8 h (T0 + 30 min) the cap is $60K → line $120K
  path(e2, T0 + H, T0 + 2 * H, (t) => 60_000 + (t - T0 - H) * 20);
  const a = [...e2.entries.values()].find((x) => x.families.includes("AGE-DOUBLE")); assert.ok(a);
  assert.ok(a.signalMc >= 119_999 && a.signalMc < 122_000, String(a.signalMc));
  assert.equal(a.cands.find((c) => c.family === "AGE-DOUBLE").detail.ref_mc, 60_000);
});

test("one FIRST buy per mint, family and age group; a new age group allows a new first buy; a later buy in the same age group is a marked re-buy (older-9)", () => {
  const e = setup(); path(e, T0, T0 + 4 * H + 60, () => 50_000);
  path(e, T0 + 4 * H + 60, T0 + 5 * H, (t) => 50_000 * (1 + (t - T0 - 4 * H) / H * 1.5));
  const n1 = [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H")).length; assert.equal(n1, 1);
  path(e, T0 + 5 * H, T0 + 11 * H, () => 40_000);                        // back down; still A1 until 16 h
  path(e, T0 + 11 * H, T0 + 12 * H, (t) => 40_000 * (1 + (t - T0 - 11 * H) / H * 2)); // doubles again inside A1 → no new FIRST buy; the first position was sold on every row hours ago, so this is a re-buy
  const a1 = [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H")); assert.equal(a1.filter((x) => !x.again?.LOW4H).length, 1, "still one first buy in A1"); assert.equal(a1.filter((x) => x.again?.LOW4H).length, 1, "and one marked re-buy");
  path(e, T0 + 12 * H, T0 + 16 * H + 60, () => 40_000);                 // into A2
  path(e, T0 + 16 * H + 60, T0 + 17 * H, (t) => 40_000 * (1 + (t - T0 - 16 * H) / H * 3));
  const l = [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H") && !x.again?.LOW4H).map((x) => x.ag); assert.deepEqual(l, ["A1", "A2"], "first buys: one per age group, exactly as before");
});

// ---- older-6 (21 Nov 2026, owner's decision): HH30-5M, HH30-10M, HH15-5M — faster volume breakouts, no dollar floor ----
const hasF = (e, f) => [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes(f));
test("older-6 HH15-5M: a new 15-minute high with the last 5 completed minutes at 2× the 1-hour norm enters after 1 h 5 min of history; the 2-hour books are not ready yet", () => {
  const e = setup(9 * H);
  path(e, T0, T0 + 70 * 60, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 1 });                 // 70 minutes at 1 SOL a minute
  const t1 = T0 + 70 * 60; path(e, t1, t1 + 5 * 60, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 3 }); // 5 completed minutes at 3 SOL a minute, price flat
  swap(e, t1 + 5 * 60 + 5, 101_000, { sol: 3 });                            // strictly above the 15-minute high
  const h = hasF(e, "HH15-5M"); assert.equal(h.length, 1, "HH15-5M entry");
  const d = h[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "HH15-5M").detail; assert.equal(d.windows.length, 12); assert.equal(d.high_window_s, 900);
  assert.deepEqual(d.signal_window, [d.B - 300, d.B]); assert.deepEqual(d.baseline_window, [d.B - 65 * 60, d.B - 300]); assert.ok(d.vol >= 2 * d.base_median && d.base_median > 0);
  assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH30-5M").length, 0, "needs 2 h 5 min of history"); assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH30-10M").length, 0); assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH-BREAK").length, 0, "needs 4 h 15 min");
});
test("older-6 HH30-5M and HH30-10M: with 2 h 10 min of history a 30-minute high on 2× the 2-hour norm enters both; volume that is not 2× the norm enters neither", () => {
  const run = (sol) => { const e = setup(9 * H); path(e, T0, T0 + 135 * 60, () => 100_000, 60, { sol: 1 });
    const t1 = T0 + 135 * 60; path(e, t1, t1 + 10 * 60, () => 100_000, 60, { sol }); swap(e, t1 + 10 * 60 + 5, 101_000, { sol }); return e; };
  const e = run(3);
  for (const [f, n, K] of [["HH30-5M", 5, 24], ["HH30-10M", 10, 12]]) { const h = hasF(e, f); assert.equal(h.length, 1, f); const d = h[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === f).detail;
    assert.equal(d.windows.length, K); assert.equal(d.high_window_s, 1800); assert.deepEqual(d.signal_window, [d.B - n * 60, d.B]); assert.deepEqual(d.baseline_window, [d.B - (K + 1) * n * 60, d.B - n * 60]); }
  assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH15-5M").length, 1); assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH30-5M")[0].id, hasF(e, "HH15-5M")[0].id, "one shared buy, three books");
  const quiet = run(1.5); for (const f of ["HH30-5M", "HH30-10M", "HH15-5M"]) assert.equal(hasF(quiet, f).length, 0, f + ": 1.5× the norm is not a volume break");
});
test("older-6 no dollar floor, but a quiet coin still does not enter: when most buckets had no trades the norm is zero, which is recorded and refused", () => {
  const notes = []; const e = setup(9 * H); e.store = { saveNote: (m, n) => notes.push(n), saveEntry() {}, saveDecision() {}, saveBar() {}, feedEvent() {} };
  path(e, T0, T0 + 150 * 60, () => 100_000, 30 * 60, { sol: 0.5 });          // one small trade every 30 minutes: most 5- and 10-minute buckets are empty
  swap(e, T0 + 150 * 60 + 30, 100_000, { sol: 0.5 }); swap(e, T0 + 151 * 60 + 5, 104_000, { sol: 0.5 }); // a new high on one more small trade
  for (const f of ["HH30-5M", "HH30-10M", "HH15-5M"]) { assert.equal(hasF(e, f).length, 0, f); assert.ok(notes.some((n) => n.family === f && /zero volume baseline/.test(n.reason)), f + ": the refusal is on record"); }
});
test("older-6 the known limit of having no dollar floor (owner's decision, on record): a coin with one $50 trade every 20 minutes has a non-zero 10-minute norm, so a new high on one more $50 trade IS bought by HH30-10M — and not by the 5-minute books", () => {
  const e = setup(9 * H);
  path(e, T0, T0 + 140 * 60, () => 100_000, 20 * 60, { sol: 0.5 });          // half of the 10-minute buckets hold one $50 trade → median bucket $25
  swap(e, T0 + 140 * 60 + 30, 100_000, { sol: 0.5 }); swap(e, T0 + 141 * 60 + 5, 104_000, { sol: 0.5 });
  const h = hasF(e, "HH30-10M"); assert.equal(h.length, 1); const d = h[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "HH30-10M").detail; assert.equal(d.vol, 50); assert.equal(d.base_median, 25);
  assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH30-5M").length, 0, "only a quarter of its 5-minute buckets have a trade: zero norm"); assert.equal(hasF(e, "HH15-5M").length, 0);
});

// Not a unit test: a throughput + page smoke run on synthetic random-walk coins. Usage: node test/sim-smoke.mjs <db path> [port]
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs"; import { Engine } from "../src/engine.mjs"; import { startWeb } from "../src/web.mjs";
const db = process.argv[2], port = Number(process.argv[3] || 0);
const store = new Store(db), e = new Engine({ store, solUsd: () => 100 });
const T0 = 1_800_000_000, N = Number(process.argv[4] || 300), H = 3600, HOURS = Number(process.argv[5] || 12); let rnd = 7; const R = () => ((rnd = (rnd * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648) / 2147483648);
const coins = Array.from({ length: N }, (_, i) => ({ mint: "Sim" + i + "pump", pool: "Pool" + i, mc: 20000 + R() * 300000, create: T0 - R() * 40 * H }));
// synthetic fixtures (older-10): every coin is a classic SPL token (fee known) and the stream is measured 1 s behind throughout
e.feedUp(T0); e.noteLag(T0, 1); for (const c of coins) { e.coin(c.mint, { pool: c.pool, createTs: c.create }).info.token_program = "TokenkegQfeZyiNwAJbNbGKPFXCWuBvf9Ss623VQ5DA"; store.saveCoin(c.mint, { pool: c.pool, createTs: c.create }); }
let swaps = 0; const t0 = Date.now();
for (let t = T0; t < T0 + HOURS * H; t += 20) {
  for (let x = t; x < t + 20; x += 4) e.noteLag(x, 1); // the stream is measured current across the whole step (the synthetic swaps of one step are not in time order)
  for (const c of coins) { if (R() < 0.5) continue; c.mc *= Math.exp((R() - 0.5) * 0.06 + (R() < 0.001 ? 0.8 : 0) - (R() < 0.0006 ? 0.5 : 0)); c.mc = Math.min(Math.max(c.mc, 3000), 3e6);
    e.onSwap({ t: t + R() * 19, mint: c.mint, pool: c.pool, base: 2e8, quote: (c.mc / 1e11) * 2e8, feeBps: 30, sol: R() * 2, isBuy: R() < 0.55, user: "w" + Math.floor(R() * 500) }); swaps++; }
  store.tx(() => e.tick(t)); if (t % 60 === 0) e.prune(t);
}
const secs = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
console.log(JSON.stringify({ swaps, secs, swaps_per_s: Math.round(swaps / secs), entries: e.stats.entries, notes: e.stats.notes, rss_mb: Math.round(process.memoryUsage().rss / 2 ** 20), disk: store.disk() }));
if (port) startWeb({ port, store, engine: e, status: () => ({ feed: { connected: true, lastMsg: Date.now() / 1000, emitted: swaps, held: 0, replayed: 0, rejected: 0, nonSol: 0, nonCanonical: 0, reconnects: 0, lag_ms_avg: 800 }, engine: { ...e.stats, coins: e.coins.size, open: 0 }, decoder: { mode: "pre", fitPre: 0, fitPost: 0, fitBoth: 0, fitNeither: 0, chainOk: 0, chainBad: 0 }, tx_validation: null, mem_mb: Math.round(process.memoryUsage().rss / 2 ** 20), verify: { done: 0, within1pct: 0, suspect: 0, failed: 0 }, sol: { px: 100, src: "sim" }, gmgn: { calls: 0, limited: 0, lastErr: "" }, backfill: { done: 0, refsOk: 0, refsMissing: 0 }, backfillQ: 0, inventory: { solPools: N, canonical: N, nonCanonical: 0, priced: N, minMc: 5000, aboveMin: N, inBand: 0, pumpSuffix: N } }) });

FILE: older/test/warm.test.mjs

// WARM RESTART (owner's decision, 22 Nov 2026): the price memory survives a planned stop; the time away stays an unobserved gap,
// tolerated by hour-scale windows only when it is at most WARM_MAX_GAP_S, and recorded on every decision that spans it.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"; import { tmpdir } from "node:os"; import { join } from "node:path";
import { mkEngine, swap, path, H, T0 } from "./helpers.mjs";
import { Store } from "../src/store.mjs";
import { Series, Coverage } from "../src/series.mjs";
import { saveWarm, loadWarm } from "../src/warm.mjs";
import { Poller } from "../src/polled.mjs";
import * as S from "../src/spec.mjs";
const M = "MintAAApump", has = (e, f) => [...e.entries.values()].filter((x) => x.families.includes(f)), dir = () => mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "older-"));
// one run, stopped at `stop`; then a new process: database restored, snapshot read `readAfter` s later, feed up `upAfter` s after the stop
const first = (d, build, stop) => { const st = new Store(join(d, "w.db")), e = mkEngine({ store: st }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H, info: {} }); build(e); e.flush(); st.heartbeat(stop); const w = saveWarm(join(d, "w.warm"), e, stop); st.db.close(); return w; };
const second = (d, stop, { readAfter = 5, upAfter = 10, warm = true } = {}) => { const st = new Store(join(d, "w.db")), e = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(e); const r = warm ? loadWarm(join(d, "w.warm"), e, stop + readAfter) : { loaded: false }; e.restartGap(st.lastAlive(), stop + readAfter, { warm: r.loaded }); e.feedUp(stop + upAfter); e.warmResult = r; return e; };

test("the price memory read back is exactly what was in memory: every answer a decision reads is the same, merged seconds and buyers included", () => {
  const cov = new Coverage(); cov.up(0); const a = new Series("M", cov); a.startLive(0);
  for (let k = 0; k < S.RAW_CAP + 40; k++) a.add(1000 + k * 0.05, 1 + (k % 17) / 100, 10 + k % 7, k % 3 !== 0, "w" + (k % 50)); // more than RAW_CAP in a few seconds: merged entries exist
  for (let k = 0; k < 200; k++) a.add(2000 + k * 30, 0.8 + (k % 23) / 50, 500, k % 2 === 0, "u" + (k % 9)); a.prune(2000 + 200 * 30 + 4000); a.add(12_000, 1.5, 300, true, "z"); a.add(12_010, 1.7, 900, false, null);
  const b = Series.load("M", cov, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(a.dump()))), t = 12_011;
  assert.ok(a.anchor && a.bars.size > 10, "the case has an anchor and bars"); assert.deepEqual(b.anchor, a.anchor); assert.equal(b.bars.size, a.bars.size); assert.equal(b.raw.length, a.raw.length);
  for (const x of [t - 60, t - 300, t - 3600, t - 4 * 3600, 5000, 1000.4]) { assert.deepEqual(b.lowIn(x, t), a.lowIn(x, t)); assert.deepEqual(b.highIn(x, t), a.highIn(x, t)); assert.deepEqual(b.lowIn(x, t, true), a.lowIn(x, t, true)); assert.deepEqual(b.priceAt(x), a.priceAt(x)); assert.deepEqual(b.priceAt(x, true), a.priceAt(x, true)); assert.equal(b.volSince(x), a.volSince(x)); assert.deepEqual(b.winStats(x, t), a.winStats(x, t)); assert.equal(b.covered(x, t), a.covered(x, t)); assert.equal(b.priced(x, t), a.priced(x, t)); }
  const mA = Math.floor(7000 / 60); assert.deepEqual(b.barsIn(mA - 30, mA).map(([m, x]) => [m, x.o, x.h, x.l, x.c, x.v, x.nb, x.ns]), a.barsIn(mA - 30, mA).map(([m, x]) => [m, x.o, x.h, x.l, x.c, x.v, x.nb, x.ns])); assert.equal(b.buyersIn(Math.floor(t / 60) - 15, Math.floor(t / 60) + 1), a.buyersIn(Math.floor(t / 60) - 15, Math.floor(t / 60) + 1));
  const c2 = new Coverage(); c2.up(0); const m = new Series("M", c2); m.startLive(0); for (let k = 0; k < S.RAW_CAP; k++) m.add(100 + k * 0.1, 1, 1, false); const tt = 100 + S.RAW_CAP * 0.1 + 10; m.add(tt + 0.1, 1, 1000, true); m.add(tt + 0.5, 0.5, 299_000, true); m.add(tt + 0.9, 1, 500, true); m.add(tt + 1.5, 1, 1, true);
  const m2 = Series.load("M", c2, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m.dump()))); assert.equal(m2.volSince(tt + 0.3), null, "a merged second is still 'not known' after reading back"); assert.equal(m2.lowIn(tt, tt + 1).px, 0.5, "and still remembers the low it absorbed");
});

test("LOW4H after a warm restart: the 4-hour low is still known, so a fresh double is bought — marked with the seconds not watched; a cold start cannot buy it", () => {
  const stop = T0 + 5 * H, build = (e) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 2 * H, () => 100_000, 60); path(e, T0 + 2 * H, stop, () => 60_000, 60); }; // five hours watched; the 4-hour low is $60K
  const d = dir(), w = first(d, build, stop); assert.ok(w.coins >= 1);
  const e = second(d, stop); assert.equal(e.warmResult.loaded, true); assert.equal(e.warmResult.coins, 1);
  swap(e, stop + 12, 110_000); assert.equal(has(e, "LOW4H").length, 0, "the first trade after a restart is never a fresh crossing: the seconds before it were not watched");
  swap(e, stop + 14, 125_000); const b = has(e, "LOW4H"); assert.equal(b.length, 1, "the next one is: $110K → $125K crosses 2 × the $60K low");
  const det = b[0].cands.find((c) => c.family === "LOW4H").detail; assert.equal(det.restart_gap_s, 10, "the 10 seconds the program was away are recorded on the decision"); assert.equal(b[0].warm_gap_s, 10); assert.match(b[0].flags.join(), /restart gap of 10 s/);
  const d2 = dir(); first(d2, build, stop); const cold = second(d2, stop, { warm: false }); swap(cold, stop + 12, 110_000); swap(cold, stop + 14, 125_000);
  assert.equal(has(cold, "LOW4H").length, 0, "control: without the price memory the same trades are not a LOW4H buy (no 4-hour history)");
});

test("the limit: a snapshot older than 60 s is not read; and if the feed only comes back more than 60 s after the stop, the gap is an ordinary outage that nothing spans", () => {
  const stop = T0 + 5 * H, build = (e) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 2 * H, () => 100_000, 60); path(e, T0 + 2 * H, stop, () => 60_000, 60); };
  const d = dir(); first(d, build, stop); const late = second(d, stop, { readAfter: 61, upAfter: 65 }); assert.equal(late.warmResult.loaded, false); assert.match(late.warmResult.why, /61 s old/);
  swap(late, stop + 70, 110_000); swap(late, stop + 72, 125_000); assert.equal(has(late, "LOW4H").length, 0);
  const d2 = dir(); first(d2, build, stop); const slow = second(d2, stop, { readAfter: 5, upAfter: 75 }); assert.equal(slow.warmResult.loaded, true, "read in time…");
  assert.equal(slow.cov.gaps.at(-1)[2], undefined, "…but the feed took 75 s: the gap is not marked warm"); swap(slow, stop + 80, 110_000); swap(slow, stop + 82, 125_000); assert.equal(has(slow, "LOW4H").length, 0, "so no hour-scale window may span it");
  const d3 = dir(); first(d3, build, stop); const ok = second(d3, stop, { readAfter: 5, upAfter: 59 }); assert.equal(ok.cov.gaps.at(-1)[2], "warm"); swap(ok, stop + 62, 110_000); swap(ok, stop + 64, 125_000); assert.equal(has(ok, "LOW4H").length, 1, "59 s: tolerated, bought, and recorded"); assert.equal(has(ok, "LOW4H")[0].warm_gap_s, 59);
});

test("exact windows never span a restart: a 1-minute double across it is not a FAST buy, the 5-minute volume across it is not decided, and both work again once a full window has been watched", () => {
  const stop = T0 + 2 * H, build = (e) => path(e, T0, stop, () => 100_000, 10), d = dir(); first(d, build, stop); const e = second(d, stop);
  swap(e, stop + 12, 100_000); swap(e, stop + 14, 210_000, { sol: 2000 });   // 2.1× inside one minute, with $200K of volume
  assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-1M").length, 0, "FAST-1M: its window contains the restart"); assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-5M").length, 0); assert.equal(has(e, "VOL300K").length, 0);
  const c = e.coins.get(M).series; assert.equal(c.continuous(stop - 30, stop + 14), false); assert.equal(c.covered(stop - 30, stop + 14), true, "hour-scale: tolerated"); assert.equal(c.warmIn(stop - 30, stop + 14), 10);
  path(e, stop + 20, stop + 100, () => 100_000, 10); swap(e, stop + 100, 100_000); swap(e, stop + 102, 210_000);
  assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-1M").length, 1, "90 s after the feed came back the 1-minute window is fully watched again"); assert.equal(has(e, "FAST-1M")[0].warm_gap_s ?? 0, 0, "and an exact decision is never marked: it cannot contain a gap");
  const k = mkEngine(); k.feedUp(T0); k.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 20 * H }); path(k, T0, stop, () => 100_000, 10); swap(k, stop + 12, 100_000); swap(k, stop + 14, 210_000);
  assert.equal(has(k, "FAST-1M").length, 1, "control: without a restart the same trades ARE a FAST-1M buy");
});

test("RUG books across a warm restart: the watched peak is kept, so a collapse watched after the restart is bought (marked); a collapse that happened while the program was away is not", () => {
  const young = (e) => { e.coins.get(M).createTs = T0 - 2 * H; }, stop = T0 + 400, build = (e) => { young(e); path(e, T0, T0 + 300, (t) => 100_000 + (t - T0) * 1400, 20); swap(e, T0 + 300, 540_000); swap(e, T0 + 320, 400_000); swap(e, T0 + 340, 300_000); };
  const mk = (d) => { const st = new Store(join(d, "w.db")), e = mkEngine({ store: st }); e.feedUp(T0); e.coin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H }); st.saveCoin(M, { pool: "PoolA", createTs: T0 - 2 * H, info: {} }); build(e); e.flush(); st.heartbeat(stop); saveWarm(join(d, "w.warm"), e, stop); st.db.close(); };
  const d = dir(); mk(d); const e = second(d, stop); assert.equal(e.coins.get(M).rug.pk.mc > 539_000, true, "the exact peak ($540K) came back — not a rounded one");
  swap(e, stop + 12, 150_000); swap(e, stop + 14, 100_000); const b = has(e, "RUG450"); assert.equal(b.length, 1, "$150K → $100K freshly crosses the $108K line"); assert.ok(b[0].warm_gap_s === 10 && b[0].cands[0].detail.restart_gap_s === 10);
  const d2 = dir(); mk(d2); const g = second(d2, stop); swap(g, stop + 12, 105_000); swap(g, stop + 14, 99_000); swap(g, stop + 16, 60_000);
  assert.equal(has(g, "RUG450").length, 0, "the seventh review's case: already under the line at the first trade after the restart — the crossing was not watched, so it is not bought"); assert.equal(g.coins.get(M).rug.arm, null, "and no bounce setup is armed from it");
  const d3 = dir(); mk(d3); const x = second(d3, stop, { readAfter: 5, upAfter: 90 }); swap(x, stop + 92, 150_000); swap(x, stop + 94, 100_000); assert.equal(has(x, "RUG450").length, 0, "away for 90 s: the old peak is no longer evidence");
});

test("a snapshot is used once and never trusted when damaged: second read, cut-short file, wrong format → cold start; a planned stop with nothing observed is harmless", () => {
  const stop = T0 + 5 * H, build = (e) => path(e, T0, stop, () => 100_000, 60), d = dir(), f = join(d, "w.warm"); first(d, build, stop);
  const whole = readFileSync(f, "utf8"); const a = second(d, stop); assert.equal(a.warmResult.loaded, true); assert.equal(existsSync(f), false, "renamed after reading");
  const st = new Store(join(d, "w.db")), e2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(e2); assert.equal(loadWarm(f, e2, stop + 20).loaded, false, "a second start does not find it");
  writeFileSync(f, whole.slice(0, whole.length - 30)); const e3 = mkEngine(); const r3 = loadWarm(f, e3, stop + 5); assert.equal(r3.loaded, false); assert.match(r3.why, /incomplete|damaged/); assert.equal(e3.coins.size, 0, "nothing of a cut-short file is applied"); assert.equal(existsSync(f), false);
  writeFileSync(f, whole.replace('"warm":1', '"warm":99')); assert.match(loadWarm(f, mkEngine(), stop + 5).why, /another format/);
  writeFileSync(f, whole); assert.match(loadWarm(f, mkEngine(), stop - 5).why, /future/);
  const d4 = dir(), st4 = new Store(join(d4, "w.db")), e4 = mkEngine({ store: st4 }); e4.feedUp(T0); const w4 = saveWarm(join(d4, "w.warm"), e4, T0 + 10); assert.equal(w4.coins, 0); assert.equal(loadWarm(join(d4, "w.warm"), mkEngine(), T0 + 12).loaded, true);
});

test("one-buy flags after a warm restart: a book that bought the coin before the stop makes no second FIRST buy — only a marked re-buy on the rows it has sold; the database's observation method wins over the snapshot's", () => {
  const stop = T0 + 5 * H + 200, d = dir(); first(d, (e) => { path(e, T0, T0 + 2 * H, () => 100_000, 60); path(e, T0 + 2 * H, T0 + 5 * H, () => 60_000, 60); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H, 110_000); swap(e, T0 + 5 * H + 2, 125_000); assert.equal(has(e, "LOW4H").length, 1); path(e, T0 + 5 * H + 10, stop, () => 80_000, 10); }, stop);
  const e = second(d, stop); swap(e, stop + 12, 110_000); swap(e, stop + 14, 125_000); swap(e, stop + 16, 110_000); swap(e, stop + 18, 130_000); e.flush();
  const ls = e.store.allEntries().filter((x) => x.families.includes("LOW4H")); assert.equal(ls.filter((x) => !x.again?.LOW4H).length, 1, "still one FIRST LOW4H buy for this coin and age group");
  const re = ls.filter((x) => x.again?.LOW4H); assert.ok(re.length >= 1, "the crossing after the restart is a re-buy (older-9)"); assert.equal(re[0].versions.filter((v) => v.stall === false).length, 6, "carrying only the rows the first buy has already sold: it fell from $125K to $80K, so the 20–30% stops are out on both exits and the 40% and 60% stops still hold"); assert.ok(re[0].versions.every((v) => v.stop <= 0.3));
  const d2 = dir(); first(d2, (x) => path(x, T0, stop, () => 100_000, 60), stop); const st = new Store(join(d2, "w.db")), e2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.setCoinMode(M, null, { monitoring_mode: "polled", population: "solana-wide" }); st.restore(e2);
  const r = loadWarm(join(d2, "w.warm"), e2, stop + 5); assert.equal(e2.coins.get(M).mode, "polled"); assert.equal(r.skipped, 1, "a live snapshot is not put under a coin the database says is sampled"); assert.equal(e2.coins.get(M).series.lastT, null);
});

test("sampled coins: the history read back is judged by the ordinary hole rule, with the interval the coin was sampled at before the stop", async () => {
  const mkJup = (clock, px) => ({ prices: async (m) => ({ t: clock.t, req: clock.t, prices: new Map(m.map((x) => [x, { px, liqUsd: 5e4, decimals: 6 }])), omitted: [] }), quote: async () => ({ ok: false, why: "n/a", t: clock.t }), recent: async () => [], category: async () => [], tokens: async () => [] });
  const run = async (firstSampleAfter) => { const d = dir(), clock = { t: T0 }, P = "SampledCoinW"; let st = new Store(join(d, "w.db")), e = mkEngine({ store: st }), po = new Poller({ engine: e, jup: mkJup(clock, 1e-4), store: st, now: () => clock.t });
    po.add({ mint: P, decimals: 6, token_program: "TokenkegQfeZyiNwAJbNbGKPFXCWuBvf9Ss623VQ5DA", circ_supply: 1e9, mcap: 1e5, launchpad: "raydium-launchlab", first_pool: { id: "P", created: T0 - 20 * H }, mint_authority_disabled: true, freeze_authority_disabled: true, is_sus: false }, "t");
    for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) { clock.t += 10; await po.sweep(); } const stop = clock.t + 2, hist = e.coins.get(P).series.histFrom; saveWarm(join(d, "w.warm"), e, stop); st.heartbeat(stop); st.db.close();
    st = new Store(join(d, "w.db")); const e2 = mkEngine({ store: st }); st.restore(e2); const r = loadWarm(join(d, "w.warm"), e2, stop + 3); assert.equal(r.loaded, true); const po2 = new Poller({ engine: e2, jup: mkJup(clock, 1e-4), store: st, now: () => clock.t }); assert.equal(po2.restore(), 1);
    clock.t = stop - 2 + firstSampleAfter; await po2.sweep(); return { sr: e2.coins.get(P).series, hist, stop, t: clock.t, po2, e2 }; };
  const a = await run(25); assert.equal(a.sr.histFrom, a.hist, "first sample 25 s after the last one (10 s coin: rule 35 s): the history is kept"); assert.equal(a.e2.pollCov.gaps.at(-1)[2], "warm"); assert.equal(a.sr.covered(a.t - 300, a.t), true); assert.equal(a.sr.continuous(a.t - 300, a.t), false, "exact windows still do not span the restart");
  const b = await run(50); assert.ok(b.sr.histFrom >= b.stop, "first sample 50 s after the last one: a hole — the history starts again, as it would without a restart"); assert.equal(b.po2.stats.warmLost, 1);
});
